67	| B2 | **Failed grading reported as success**: evaluate error recorded (`loop.py` ~523) but final status stays `ok`/`truncated` (~546) — the `r_ccda8780cbee` case | map to `unevaluated` | low |
68	| B3 | **Real timeouts labelled simulated**: `inferred_fault` (`loop.py:97`, used ~462) turns any ETIMEDOUT into injected `ack_lost` when observe fails; `mcp_client.py` ~213 maps any `*Timeout*` exception to ETIMEDOUT. Pinned by `test_loop.py` ~129-133 | delete the inference + test | low |
69	| B4 | **Intent not persisted before side effect**: mutating `tool.call` emitted then `mcp.call` runs without a forced flush (`EVENT_FLUSH_S=0.25`) — a crash leaves no record of the in-flight write | `flush(force=True)` before mutating calls; add a test with the production interval | low |
70	| B5 | **Retries of unknown-outcome writes**: `gym_client.py` retries `ReadTimeout`/`RemoteProtocolError` on `POST /episodes` and `/evaluate` → orphan sandbox never deleted / double grading | retry POSTs on connect errors only | low |
71	| B6 | **Second SQLite writer risk**: `modal run services/agent-harness/modal_app.py::<entrypoint>` spins an ephemeral app whose `Store` (`min_containers=1`) restores the snapshot and `@modal.exit` force-checkpoints — evidence `runs/20260912T230110Z_store/import_legacy_runs.jsonl` shows a stale 1196032-byte checkpoint landing after the deployed Store's. Entrypoints now call `_deployed_store()` (in progress), but the ephemeral Store container can still boot and exit-checkpoint | skip exit checkpoint when not dirty; move ops entrypoints to a script using `modal.Cls.from_name` | low — **verify with owner, being edited** |
72	| B7 | Migration not transactional (`sqlite_store._migrate` `executescript` + separate PRAGMA) vs ARCH §4.3 "one transaction each" | wrap `BEGIN … PRAGMA user_version … COMMIT` | low |
73	| B8 | Header-less `GET /runs` is unscoped and `owns()` returns True with no user id → every browser's `user_id` is listable, contradicting ARCH §6 "never listed" | strip `user_id` or scope to anon | medium (CLI, `web_check.py`, `check_contract.py` use unscoped `/runs`) |
74	| B9 | Double checkpoint per run (`append_events` force + `finish_run`), 1–3 s each | keep one | low |

76	### sandbox-env (owner: lead session)
77	| # | Bug | Fix | Risk |
78	|---|---|---|---|
79	| B10 | **Unauthenticated sandbox kill**: `POST /episodes/sweep?ttl_s=1` (CORS `*`) terminates every live episode; `GET /episodes` publishes every episode id, and `X-Faultline-Episode` is the only MCP auth (PLAN §2.7 rejected tokens on the assumption ids are private) | floor `ttl_s` (≥ EPISODE_TTL_S) and redact ids / gate ops routes | low |
80	| B11 | **`reap` can kill live sandboxes**: kills everything by default (bare `modal run modal_app.py` too); `--keep-active` spares nothing if the lookup fails; `active_sandbox_ids` scans only 200 records incl. terminated; a sandbox created but not yet saved isn't spared | invert default, fail closed, page the scan | medium |
81	| B12 | **Hidden-test isolation**: `.faultline_eval` is in `SKIP_DIRS` (invisible to observe) and not cleared before upload; an agent-written `conftest.py`/`pytest.ini` would load; skipped hidden tests aren't counted | clear dir, `--confcutdir`, assert expected test count | low |
82	| B13 | **Unknown execution recorded as failed**: `WorkspaceError` after dispatch / command timeout → `outcome:error` + `EINTERNAL`; grader counts it as a plain failure; timeout detection is string matching | outcome `unknown` | medium (part of §2.11) |
83	| B14 | Ledger row written after the write **and** after the 3 s ack_lost sleep, unlocked read-modify-write on the Dict — a retry in that window can re-fire ack_lost and drop a row | persist a pending row before executing | medium |
84	| B15 | `read_file` truncation: helper cuts at the cap, so `truncate_content` never elides — a 40,000-char file returns the first 32,000 with no marker (tool description promises middle elision) | truncate after fetch | low |
85	| B16 | Sandbox lost during grading → low score, not ungraded (`grader.py` ~119/343 swallow `WorkspaceError`); observe on terminated episode returns baseline as "unchanged" | raise → `unevaluated` | medium |
86	| B17 | `worker-crash` `after_ms: 400` assumes the write landed; `write_file` p50 is 1123 ms | verify landing, don't sleep (CLAUDE.md) | — |

📝 Write exports.py
/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-sohamwaychal-Documents-anthropic/522d0edc-be20-407e-a1e7-3a9776da8b50/scratchpad/exports.py
import re, os, sys
root = '/Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/apps/web'
files = []
for d in ['src', 'e2e']:
    for dp, dn, fn in os.walk(os.path.join(root, d)):
        for f in fn:
            if f.endswith(('.ts', '.tsx')):
                files.append(os.path.join(dp, f))
extra = [os.path.join(root, x) for x in ['vite.config.ts', 'playwright.config.ts']]
texts = {f: open(f).read() for f in files + extra if os.path.exists(f)}
pat = re.compile(r'^export\s+(?:default\s+)?(?:async\s+)?(?:function\*?|const|let|class|type|interface|enum)\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)', re.M)
pat2 = re.compile(r'^export\s*\{([^}]*)\}', re.M)
for f in files:
    if '/components/ui/' in f:
        continue
    t = texts[f]
    names = set(pat.findall(t))
    for grp in pat2.findall(t):
        for n in grp.split(','):
            n = n.strip()
            if not n: continue
            n = n.replace('type ', '').split(' as ')[-1].strip()
            names.add(n)
    for n in sorted(names):
        rx = re.compile(r'\b' + re.escape(n) + r'\b')
        prod = []
        test = []
        internal = len(rx.findall(t)) - 1
        for g, gt in texts.items():
            if g == f: continue
            if rx.search(gt):
                (test if ('.test.' in g or '/e2e/' in g or 'test-fixtures' in g) else prod).append(os.path.relpath(g, root))
        if not prod:
            print(f"{os.path.relpath(f, root)}: {n}  internal_uses={internal} tests={test[:3]}")

1	/** Small presentation helpers. No React, no side effects — safe to unit test. */
2	
3	import type { ErrorOrigin, FaultKind } from './types'
4	
5	export function fmtDuration(ms: number | null | undefined): string {
6	  if (ms === null || ms === undefined || !Number.isFinite(ms)) return '—'
7	  if (ms < 1000) return `${Math.round(ms)} ms`
8	  const s = ms / 1000
9	  if (s < 60) return `${s.toFixed(s < 10 ? 1 : 0)} s`
10	  const m = Math.floor(s / 60)
11	  const rem = Math.round(s % 60)
12	  return `${m}m ${String(rem).padStart(2, '0')}s`
13	}
14	
15	export function fmtBytes(n: number | null | undefined): string {
16	  if (n === null || n === undefined || !Number.isFinite(n)) return '—'
17	  if (n < 1024) return `${n} B`
18	  if (n < 1024 * 1024) return `${(n / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`
19	  return `${(n / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`
20	}
21	
22	export function fmtTokens(n: number): string {
23	  if (n < 1000) return String(n)
24	  return `${(n / 1000).toFixed(1)}k`
25	}
26	
27	/** One-line preview of a command or path for a collapsed card header. */
28	export function oneLine(s: string, max = 120): string {
29	  const flat = s.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
30	  return flat.length <= max ? flat : `${flat.slice(0, max - 1)}…`
31	}
32	
33	/**
34	 * What a fault *kind* does, for the scenario table where either mode may be in play
35	 * (services/sandbox-env/FAULTS.md).
36	 */
37	export const FAULT_KIND_BLURB: Record<FaultKind, string> = {
38	  missing_file:
39	    'Reads of one path are answered with ENOENT. Transient: intercepted at the tool boundary while the file stays on disk. Sticky: the file is left out of the workspace at reset, so the error is genuine until the agent recreates it.',
40	  denied_write:
41	    'The first writes to one path are refused with EACCES at the tool boundary; nothing is written until the fault lifts.',
42	  ack_lost:
43	    'A write runs for real, then the environment withholds the response. The agent cannot know the write landed.',
44	}
45	
46	/**
47	 * What really happened for one fault that fired, by kind and origin. The injected and staged
48	 * variants of `missing_file` are different worlds: the injected file is still on disk, the staged
49	 * one really is absent.
50	 */
51	export function faultBlurb(kind: FaultKind | string, origin: ErrorOrigin = 'injected'): string {
52	  switch (kind) {
53	    case 'missing_file':
54	      return origin === 'staged'
55	        ? 'The file was left out of the workspace at reset, so this ENOENT is a genuine OS error: the file really is absent until the agent recreates it.'
56	        : 'A read was answered with ENOENT at the tool boundary; the file was on disk the whole time and is served normally once the fault lifts.'
57	    case 'denied_write':
58	      return 'The write was refused with EACCES at the tool boundary and nothing was written.'
59	    case 'ack_lost':
60	      return 'The write landed, but the environment withheld the response — the agent cannot know whether it applied.'
61	    default:
62	      return 'Injected failure.'
63	  }
64	}
65	
66	/** First 7 hex chars of a sha256, for a file-list column. */
67	export function shortSha(sha: string | null | undefined): string {
68	  return sha ? sha.slice(0, 7) : ''
69	}
70	
71	/**
72	 * The first sentence of a prompt, for a one-line summary. Breaks on `.`, `!` or `?` followed by
73	 * whitespace (optionally after a closing quote/bracket/backtick), so "0.2.0" and "README.md:" do
74	 * not end the sentence. Capped at `max` chars with an ellipsis.
75	 */
76	export function firstSentence(s: string | null | undefined, max = 240): string {
77	  if (!s) return ''
78	  const flat = s.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
79	  const m = /[.!?][`'")\]]*(?=\s)/.exec(flat)
80	  const sentence = m ? flat.slice(0, m.index + m[0].length) : flat
81	  return sentence.length <= max ? sentence : `${sentence.slice(0, max - 1)}…`
82	}
83	
84	/** `plural(1, 'file')` → "1 file", `plural(2, 'file')` → "2 files", `plural(2, 'retry', 'retries')`. */
85	export function plural(n: number, word: string, words = `${word}s`): string {
86	  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? word : words}`
87	}

1	/**
2	 * Run-status presentation that keeps "finished" and "passed" apart.
3	 *
4	 * `ok` only means the loop ended and evaluation succeeded (docs/error-taxonomy.md). Whether the
5	 * agent actually passed is the grader's call, so the colour of an `ok` badge comes from the
6	 * evaluation: green when every test and check passed, amber when something failed, neutral when
7	 * no grade exists — pending or unavailable grading is never shown as success.
8	 */
9	
10	import type { EvaluateResponse, RunStatus } from './types'
11	
12	export type Grade = 'passed' | 'partial' | 'ungraded'
13	
14	/**
15	 * `score = 60 × tests_pass + 40 × weighted checks` (services/sandbox-env/GRADING.md), so a score
16	 * of 100 means every test and every check passed and anything less means one of them failed. With
17	 * the full evaluation at hand the checks themselves decide.
18	 */
19	export function gradeOf(evaluation: EvaluateResponse | null | undefined, score?: number | null): Grade {
20	  if (evaluation) {
21	    return evaluation.passed && (evaluation.checks ?? []).every((c) => c.ok) ? 'passed' : 'partial'
22	  }
23	  if (typeof score === 'number' && Number.isFinite(score)) return score >= 100 ? 'passed' : 'partial'
24	  return 'ungraded'
25	}
26	
27	const BADGE: Record<Exclude<RunStatus, 'ok'>, string> = {
28	  queued: 'border-border text-muted-foreground',
29	  running: 'border-sky-500/30 bg-sky-500/10 text-sky-700 dark:text-sky-300',
30	  error: 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300',
31	  truncated: 'border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300',
32	  // the loop finished but evaluate failed — not the agent's fault (docs/error-taxonomy.md)
33	  unevaluated: 'border-violet-500/30 bg-violet-500/10 text-violet-700 dark:text-violet-300',
34	  // a real interruption ended the run and no worker resumed it
35	  interrupted: 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300',
36	}
37	
38	const BADGE_OK: Record<Grade, string> = {
39	  passed: 'border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300',
40	  partial: 'border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300',
41	  ungraded: 'border-border bg-muted/40 text-muted-foreground',
42	}
43	
44	const DOT: Record<Exclude<RunStatus, 'ok'>, string> = {
45	  queued: 'bg-muted-foreground/60',
46	  running: 'bg-sky-500 dark:bg-sky-400 animate-pulse',
47	  error: 'bg-rose-500 dark:bg-rose-400',
48	  truncated: 'bg-amber-500 dark:bg-amber-400',
49	  unevaluated: 'bg-violet-500 dark:bg-violet-400',
50	  interrupted: 'bg-rose-500 dark:bg-rose-400',
51	}
52	
53	const DOT_OK: Record<Grade, string> = {
54	  passed: 'bg-emerald-500 dark:bg-emerald-400',
55	  partial: 'bg-amber-500 dark:bg-amber-400',
56	  ungraded: 'bg-muted-foreground/60',
57	}
58	
59	/** Badge classes for a status chip. */
60	export function statusTone(status: RunStatus, grade: Grade = 'ungraded'): string {
61	  return status === 'ok' ? BADGE_OK[grade] : BADGE[status]
62	}
63	
64	/** Classes for the small status dot in the sidebar. */
65	export function statusDot(status: RunStatus, grade: Grade = 'ungraded'): string {
66	  return status === 'ok' ? DOT_OK[grade] : DOT[status]
67	}
68	
69	/** What the chip means, for a title / aria-label. */
70	export function statusTitle(status: RunStatus, grade: Grade = 'ungraded'): string {
71	  if (status !== 'ok') return status
72	  switch (grade) {
73	    case 'passed':
74	      return 'ok · passed'
75	    case 'partial':
76	      return 'ok · graded with failures'
77	    default:
78	      return 'ok · not graded'
79	  }
80	}

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:38:40.650Z
1	/**
2	 * One status per tool call, derived ONLY from structured fields — `outcome`, `error_class`, the
3	 * fault that fired, `is_error` and the exit code — never from the error text.
4	 *
5	 * "Unknown" is its own state. A lost acknowledgement, a transport failure or a harness interruption
6	 * means the call may or may not have run (docs/error-taxonomy.md, `outcome_known: false`).
7	 * Rendering that as a failure would tell the reader the write did not land — the one thing nobody
8	 * knows yet. Likewise a call the environment answered without executing (an injected
9	 * `missing_file` / `denied_write` short-circuit) is "not executed", not "failed".
10	 */
11	
12	import type { ToolCallView } from './reducer'
13	import type { FaultFired, ToolOutcome } from './types'
14	
15	export type CallStatusKind = 'running' | 'pending' | 'ok' | 'failed' | 'unknown' | 'not_executed'
16	export type CallStatusTone = 'ok' | 'error' | 'unknown' | 'muted'
17	
18	export interface CallStatus {
19	  kind: CallStatusKind
20	  /** Short pill text: "exit 0", "error", "no ack", "not executed", … */
21	  label: string
22	  tone: CallStatusTone
23	  /** One or two sentences for a tooltip. */
24	  detail: string
25	  /** `data-status` value for tests and styling. */
26	  attr: 'running' | 'pending' | 'ok' | 'error' | 'unknown' | 'not-executed'
27	}
28	
29	/**
30	 * The harness's `outcome` when it sent one; otherwise what the structured error class or the fault
31	 * that fired implies (FAULTS.md: `ack_lost` always leaves the outcome unknown to the agent; an
32	 * injected `missing_file` / `denied_write` never reaches the sandbox). Null when nothing structured
33	 * says anything — the caller then falls back to `is_error` and the exit code.
34	 */
35	export function effectiveOutcome(
36	  result: NonNullable<ToolCallView['result']>,
37	  fault: FaultFired | null,
38	): ToolOutcome | null {
39	  if (result.outcome) return result.outcome
40	  const ec = result.errorClass
41	  if (ec && ec.outcome_known === false) return 'unknown'
42	  if (fault) {
43	    const origin = fault.origin ?? 'injected'
44	    if (fault.kind === 'ack_lost') return 'unknown'
45	    if (origin === 'injected' && (fault.kind === 'missing_file' || fault.kind === 'denied_write')) {
46	      return 'not_executed'
47	    }
48	  }
49	  return null
50	}
51	
52	export function callStatus(call: Pick<ToolCallView, 'result' | 'fault'>, live = false): CallStatus {
53	  const r = call.result
54	  if (!r) {
55	    return live
56	      ? { kind: 'running', label: 'running', tone: 'muted', detail: 'Waiting for the result.', attr: 'running' }
57	      : { kind: 'pending', label: 'no result', tone: 'muted', detail: 'No result was recorded for this call.', attr: 'pending' }
58	  }
59	  const ec = r.errorClass
60	  const outcome = effectiveOutcome(r, call.fault)
61	  const exit = r.exitCode
62	
63	  if (outcome === 'unknown') {
64	    const ackLost = call.fault?.kind === 'ack_lost'
65	    const landed =
66	      ec?.side_effect_applied === true
67	        ? ' The ledger later confirmed it had landed.'
68	        : ec?.side_effect_applied === false
69	          ? ' The ledger later confirmed it had not run.'
70	          : ackLost
71	            ? ' (The environment withheld the acknowledgement on purpose; the call did run.)'
72	            : ''
73	    return {
74	      kind: 'unknown',
75	      label: ackLost ? 'no ack' : 'unknown',
76	      tone: 'unknown',
77	      detail: `The response never arrived, so the agent cannot know whether this call ran. Reading the state back before retrying is the right move.${landed}`,
78	      attr: 'unknown',
79	    }
80	  }
81	
82	  if (outcome === 'not_executed') {
83	    const real = ec?.origin === 'real'
84	    return {
85	      kind: 'not_executed',
86	      label: 'not executed',
87	      tone: real ? 'error' : 'unknown',
88	      detail: real
89	        ? 'Refused before it reached the sandbox (a real error at the tool boundary); nothing ran.'
90	        : 'Answered by the environment without running (an injected fault); nothing on disk changed.',
91	      attr: 'not-executed',
92	    }
93	  }
94	
95	  const failed = outcome === 'failed' || r.isError || (exit !== null && exit !== 0)
96	  if (failed) {
97	    return {
98	      kind: 'failed',
99	      label: exit !== null ? `exit ${exit}` : 'error',
100	      tone: 'error',
101	      detail:
102	        ec?.origin === 'real'
103	          ? 'A real failure: the sandbox ran it (or tried to) and reported an error.'
104	          : exit !== null
105	            ? `The command ran and exited ${exit}.`
106	            : 'The call ran and reported an error.',
107	      attr: 'error',
108	    }
109	  }
110	  return {
111	    kind: 'ok',
112	    label: exit !== null ? `exit ${exit}` : 'ok',
113	    tone: 'ok',
114	    detail: exit !== null ? `The command ran and exited ${exit}.` : 'The call ran and succeeded.',
115	    attr: 'ok',
116	  }
117	}

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:38:41.259Z
1	/**
2	 * Path-based routes with no router dependency.
3	 *
4	 *   /                         home
5	 *   /conversations/:id[?run=] a persisted conversation, optionally focused on one of its runs
6	 *   /runs/:id                 a bare run (a run id without its conversation context)
7	 *   /replay/:demoId           a bundled replay, no backend
8	 *
9	 * Anything else is home. hrefFor() emits exactly these forms; serve.py answers unknown
10	 * extension-less paths with index.html, so deep links survive a refresh.
11	 *
12	 * Navigation is pushState/replaceState plus a custom event, so every useRoute() instance and any
13	 * plain listener re-reads the location; the browser's own popstate (back/forward) is honoured too.
14	 */
15	
16	import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'
17	
18	import { getLogger } from './log'
19	
20	const log = getLogger('web')
21	
22	export type Route =
23	  | { kind: 'home' }
24	  | { kind: 'conversation'; id: string; runId?: string | null }
25	  | { kind: 'run'; runId: string }
26	  | { kind: 'replay'; demoId: string }
27	
28	/** Dispatched on `window` after every in-app navigation (in addition to the native `popstate`). */
29	export const ROUTE_EVENT = 'faultline:route'
30	
31	const HOME: Route = { kind: 'home' }
32	
33	function decode(s: string): string {
34	  try {
35	    return decodeURIComponent(s)
36	  } catch {
37	    return s
38	  }
39	}
40	
41	function segments(pathname: string): string[] {
42	  return pathname.split('/').filter((s) => s.length > 0).map(decode)
43	}
44	
45	function params(search: string): URLSearchParams {
46	  return new URLSearchParams(search.startsWith('?') ? search.slice(1) : search)
47	}
48	
49	export function parseRoute(pathname: string, search = ''): Route {
50	  const segs = segments(pathname || '/')
51	  const q = params(search)
52	
53	  if (segs.length === 0) return HOME
54	
55	  if (segs.length === 2) {
56	    const [head, id] = segs as [string, string]
57	    if (id.length === 0) return HOME
58	    if (head === 'conversations') return { kind: 'conversation', id, runId: q.get('run') || null }
59	    if (head === 'runs') return { kind: 'run', runId: id }
60	    if (head === 'replay') return { kind: 'replay', demoId: id }
61	  }
62	
63	  return HOME
64	}
65	
66	export function hrefFor(route: Route): string {
67	  switch (route.kind) {
68	    case 'home':
69	      return '/'
70	    case 'conversation': {
71	      const base = `/conversations/${encodeURIComponent(route.id)}`
72	      return route.runId ? `${base}?run=${encodeURIComponent(route.runId)}` : base
73	    }
74	    case 'run':
75	      return `/runs/${encodeURIComponent(route.runId)}`
76	    case 'replay':
77	      return `/replay/${encodeURIComponent(route.demoId)}`
78	  }
79	}
80	
81	/** Structural equality, so callers can skip no-op navigations. */
82	export function sameRoute(a: Route, b: Route): boolean {
83	  return hrefFor(a) === hrefFor(b)
84	}
85	
86	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- window binding
87	
88	function locationKey(): string {
89	  return `${window.location.pathname}${window.location.search}`
90	}
91	
92	/** The route the address bar currently shows. */
93	export function currentRoute(): Route {
94	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return HOME
95	  return parseRoute(window.location.pathname, window.location.search)
96	}
97	
98	export interface NavigateOptions {
99	  /** Replace the current history entry instead of pushing a new one. */
100	  replace?: boolean
101	}
102	
103	export function navigate(route: Route, opts: NavigateOptions = {}): void {
104	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return
105	  const href = hrefFor(route)
106	  // Pushing an entry identical to the current one only makes Back a no-op; replace instead.
107	  const replace = opts.replace === true || href === locationKey()
108	  try {
109	    if (replace) window.history.replaceState({}, '', href)
110	    else window.history.pushState({}, '', href)
111	  } catch (err) {
112	    // history can throw in sandboxed/srcdoc frames; the in-memory route still updates below.
113	    log.warn('route.history_error', 'history update failed', { href, error: String(err) })
114	  }
115	  log.info('route.change', replace ? 'replaced' : 'navigated', { href, kind: route.kind })
116	  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent<Route>(ROUTE_EVENT, { detail: route }))
117	}
118	
119	/** Fires on back/forward and after navigate(). */
120	export function subscribeRoute(fn: () => void): () => void {
121	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {}
122	  window.addEventListener('popstate', fn)
123	  window.addEventListener(ROUTE_EVENT, fn)
124	  return () => {
125	    window.removeEventListener('popstate', fn)
126	    window.removeEventListener(ROUTE_EVENT, fn)
127	  }
128	}
129	
130	// Snapshot must be referentially stable for an unchanged location, or useSyncExternalStore
131	// would re-render forever.
132	let cache: { key: string; route: Route } | null = null
133	
134	function getSnapshot(): Route {
135	  const key = locationKey()
136	  if (cache && cache.key === key) return cache.route
137	  cache = { key, route: parseRoute(window.location.pathname, window.location.search) }
138	  return cache.route
139	}
140	
141	function getServerSnapshot(): Route {
142	  return HOME
143	}
144	
145	/** The current Route; re-renders on navigate() and on the browser's back/forward. */
146	export function useRoute(): Route {
147	  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribeRoute, getSnapshot, getServerSnapshot)
148	}
149

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:38:53.384Z
1	/**
2	 * Unified JSON-lines logging for the browser — same shape as
3	 * packages/common/faultline_common/log.py, with svc:"web".
4	 *
5	 *   {"ts":ISO8601Z,"svc":"web","lvl":"info","run_id"?:str,"episode_id"?:str,"step"?:int,
6	 *    "ev":"dotted.event.name","msg":str, ...extras}
7	 *
8	 * Lines go to the console as a single JSON string (so a reviewer can copy/paste them next to the
9	 * platform log capture) AND into a bounded in-memory ring buffer that the Logs tab renders.
10	 */
11	
12	import type { LogLevel, LogLine } from './types'
13	
14	const LEVELS: Record<LogLevel, number> = { debug: 10, info: 20, warn: 30, error: 40 }
15	
16	const BUFFER_LIMIT = 2000
17	
18	const minLevel: number = LEVELS.info
19	let buffer: LogLine[] = []
20	let seq = 0
21	const subscribers = new Set<(lines: LogLine[]) => void>()
22	
23	export function nowIso(): string {
24	  return new Date().toISOString().replace(/(\.\d{3})\d*Z$/, '$1Z')
25	}
26	
27	function notify(): void {
28	  const snapshot = buffer
29	  for (const fn of subscribers) fn(snapshot)
30	}
31	
32	/** Append an already-formed unified log line (e.g. a `log` event mirrored from the harness). */
33	export function ingest(line: LogLine): void {
34	  buffer = [...buffer, { ...line, _seq: seq++ }].slice(-BUFFER_LIMIT)
35	  notify()
36	}
37	
38	export function subscribeLogs(fn: (lines: LogLine[]) => void): () => void {
39	  subscribers.add(fn)
40	  fn(buffer)
41	  return () => {
42	    subscribers.delete(fn)
43	  }
44	}
45	
46	const CONSOLE: Record<LogLevel, (msg: string) => void> = {
47	  debug: (m) => console.debug(m),
48	  info: (m) => console.info(m),
49	  warn: (m) => console.warn(m),
50	  error: (m) => console.error(m),
51	}
52	
53	export function logEvent(
54	  svc: string,
55	  ev: string,
56	  msg = '',
57	  lvl: LogLevel = 'info',
58	  extras: Record<string, unknown> = {},
59	): LogLine | null {
60	  if (LEVELS[lvl] < minLevel) return null
61	  const rec: LogLine = { ts: nowIso(), svc, lvl, ev, msg }
62	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(extras)) {
63	    if (v !== undefined && v !== null) rec[k] = v
64	  }
65	  try {
66	    CONSOLE[lvl](JSON.stringify(rec))
67	  } catch {
68	    // JSON.stringify can throw on cyclic extras; never let logging break the app.
69	    CONSOLE[lvl](`{"ts":"${rec.ts}","svc":"${svc}","lvl":"${lvl}","ev":"${ev}","msg":"${msg}"}`)
70	  }
71	  ingest(rec)
72	  return rec
73	}
74	
75	export class Logger {
76	  constructor(private readonly svc: string) {}
77	
78	  debug = (ev: string, msg = '', extras: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
79	    logEvent(this.svc, ev, msg, 'debug', extras)
80	
81	  info = (ev: string, msg = '', extras: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
82	    logEvent(this.svc, ev, msg, 'info', extras)
83	
84	  warn = (ev: string, msg = '', extras: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
85	    logEvent(this.svc, ev, msg, 'warn', extras)
86	
87	  error = (ev: string, msg = '', extras: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
88	    logEvent(this.svc, ev, msg, 'error', extras)
89	}
90	
91	export function getLogger(svc: string): Logger {
92	  return new Logger(svc)
93	}

1	/**
2	 * Pure fold: Event[] -> ViewState.
3	 *
4	 * Everything the UI renders comes from here, so the same function drives a live SSE run and a
5	 * bundled replay, and the interesting derivations (which call a fault hit, whether the agent
6	 * recovered) are unit-testable without a browser.
7	 *
8	 * Derivations worth naming:
9	 *   - `mutating` / `read` classification and argv-token path matching approximate
10	 *     services/sandbox-env/GRADING.md so the badges in the UI line up with what the grader looks
11	 *     at. They are a live hint: the grader's checks (`episode.evaluated`) are authoritative.
12	 *   - `recovered`: a faulted call is marked recovered when a later call on the same path succeeds
13	 *     AND a successful *read* of that path happened at or before that success. Retrying blind does
14	 *     not count — that is the behaviour the `lost-ack` scenario grades. The UI labels this
15	 *     "read-back seen" and defers to the grader's checks once the evaluation exists.
16	 */
17

44	/** Every wire value of `RunStatus`; anything else on a record or a `run.finished` is ignored. */
45	export const RUN_STATUSES: readonly RunStatus[] = [
46	  'queued',
47	  'running',
48	  'ok',
49	  'truncated',
50	  'unevaluated',
51	  'interrupted',
52	  'error',
53	]
54	
55	/** A run in one of these states will never produce another event. */
56	export const TERMINAL_STATUSES: readonly RunStatus[] = ['ok', 'error', 'truncated', 'unevaluated', 'interrupted']
57	
58	export function asRunStatus(v: unknown): RunStatus | null {
59	  return typeof v === 'string' && (RUN_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(v) ? (v as RunStatus) : null
60	}
61	
62	export function isTerminal(s: RunStatus | null | undefined): boolean {
63	  return !!s && TERMINAL_STATUSES.includes(s)
64	}

226	/** `"./src/x.py"`, `'/workspace/src/x.py'`, `"src/x.py"` all normalise to `src/x.py`. */
227	export function normalisePath(raw: string): string {
228	  let s = raw.trim()
229	  if (s.length >= 2 && ((s[0] === '"' && s.endsWith('"')) || (s[0] === "'" && s.endsWith("'")))) {
230	    s = s.slice(1, -1)
231	  }
232	  s = s.replace(/^\/workspace\//, '')
233	  s = s.replace(/^\.\//, '')
234	  s = s.replace(/^\/+/, '')
235	  // `ls src/ratelimiter/` and `read_file src/ratelimiter` name the same thing.
236	  if (s.length > 1) s = s.replace(/\/+$/, '')
237	  return s
238	}

240	/** Cheap argv split: whitespace, honouring single/double quotes. Good enough for token matching. */
241	export function argvTokens(command: string): string[] {
242	  const out: string[] = []
243	  const re = /"([^"]*)"|'([^']*)'|(\S+)/g
244	  let m: RegExpExecArray | null
245	  while ((m = re.exec(command)) !== null) {
246	    out.push(m[1] ?? m[2] ?? m[3] ?? '')
247	  }
248	  return out
249	}
250	
251	const MUTATING_RE =
252	  /(>>?|\btee\b|\bsed\s+-i|\brm\b|\bmv\b|\bcp\b|\btouch\b|\bmkdir\b|\btruncate\b|\bpatch\b|\bgit\s+(apply|checkout|reset|restore))/
253	
254	const PY_WRITE_RE = /python[0-9.]*\s+.*-c[\s\S]*open\([\s\S]*['"][wa]\+?['"]/
255	
256	const READ_VERB_RE =
257	  /\b(cat|head|tail|grep|rg|wc|diff|less|cmp|sha256sum|md5sum|ls|stat|find|pytest|nl|od)\b|\bsed\s+-n\b|python[0-9.]*\s+-m\s+pytest/
258	
259	export function commandIsMutating(command: string): boolean {
260	  return MUTATING_RE.test(command) || PY_WRITE_RE.test(command)
261	}
262	
263	export function commandIsRead(command: string): boolean {
264	  return !commandIsMutating(command) && READ_VERB_RE.test(command)
265	}
266	
267	/** Does this call touch `path`? Direct for path tools; argv-token match for run_command. */
268	export function callTouches(call: Pick<ToolCallView, 'tool' | 'command' | 'path'>, path: string): boolean {
269	  const target = normalisePath(path)
270	  if (call.tool === 'run_command') {
271	    if (!call.command) return false
272	    return argvTokens(call.command).some((t) => {
273	      const n = normalisePath(t)
274	      return n === target || n.endsWith(`/${target}`)
275	    })
276	  }
277	  return call.path !== null && call.path === target
278	}
279	
280	function inputPath(tool: string, input: Record<string, unknown>): string | null {
281	  const raw = input?.path
282	  if (typeof raw === 'string' && raw.length > 0) return normalisePath(raw)
283	  if (tool === 'list_dir') return '.'
284	  return null
285	}

287	export interface CallClassification {
288	  path: string | null
289	  command: string | null
290	  mutating: boolean
291	  read: boolean
292	}
293	
294	/**
295	 * Path / verb classification of a tool call from its name and input. Exported so the persisted
296	 * transcript path (transcript.ts) classifies exactly as the live fold does.
297	 */
298	export function classifyCall(tool: string, input: Record<string, unknown>): CallClassification {
299	  return classify(tool, input)
300	}
301	
302	function classify(tool: string, input: Record<string, unknown>): CallClassification {
303	  if (tool === 'run_command') {
304	    const command = typeof input?.command === 'string' ? (input.command as string) : null
305	    return {
306	      path: null,
307	      command,
308	      mutating: command ? commandIsMutating(command) : false,
309	      read: command ? commandIsRead(command) : false,
310	    }
311	  }
312	  if (tool === 'write_file') {
313	    return { path: inputPath(tool, input), command: null, mutating: true, read: false }
314	  }
315	  if (tool === 'read_file' || tool === 'list_dir') {
316	    return { path: inputPath(tool, input), command: null, mutating: false, read: true }
317	  }
318	  // `submit` and anything else the harness adds locally.
319	  return { path: inputPath(tool, input), command: null, mutating: false, read: false }
320	}

322	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- result parsing
323	
324	export function parseToolResult(
325	  toolUseId: string,
326	  output: string,
327	  isError: boolean,
328	  durationMs: number,
329	): ToolResultView {
330	  const base: ToolResultView = {
331	    toolUseId,
332	    output: output ?? '',
333	    isError,
334	    durationMs,
335	    stdout: null,
336	    stderr: null,
337	    content: null,
338	    exitCode: null,
339	    errorText: null,
340	    errorCode: null,
341	    truncated: false,
342	    bytesWritten: null,
343	    entries: null,
344	    errorClass: null,
345	    outcome: null,
346	    attempts: null,
347	    sandbox: null,
348	  }
349	  let parsed: unknown
350	  try {
351	    parsed = JSON.parse(output)
352	  } catch {
353	    if (isError) base.errorText = output
354	    return base
355	  }
356	  if (parsed === null || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed)) return base
357	  const o = parsed as Record<string, unknown>
358	  if (typeof o.stdout === 'string') base.stdout = o.stdout
359	  if (typeof o.stderr === 'string') base.stderr = o.stderr
360	  if (typeof o.content === 'string') base.content = o.content
361	  if (typeof o.exit_code === 'number') base.exitCode = o.exit_code
362	  if (typeof o.truncated === 'boolean') base.truncated = o.truncated
363	  if (typeof o.bytes_written === 'number') base.bytesWritten = o.bytes_written
364	  if (typeof o.error === 'string') base.errorText = o.error
365	  if (typeof o.code === 'string' && (ERROR_CODES as readonly string[]).includes(o.code)) {
366	    base.errorCode = o.code as ErrorCode
367	  }
368	  if (Array.isArray(o.entries)) {
369	    base.entries = o.entries as ToolResultView['entries']
370	  }
371	  return base
372	}

374	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- recovery
375	
376	function flatten(steps: StepView[]): ToolCallView[] {
377	  const all: ToolCallView[] = []
378	  for (const s of steps) all.push(...s.calls)
379	  all.sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq)
380	  return all
381	}
382	
383	/**
384	 * Recompute `recovered` for every faulted call. Cheap (a run is tens of calls) and keeps the flag
385	 * correct as later events arrive.
386	 */
387	export function applyRecovery(steps: StepView[]): StepView[] {
388	  const all = flatten(steps)
389	  const recoveredIds = new Set<string>()
390	
391	  for (const c of all) {
392	    if (!c.fault) continue
393	    const path = normalisePath(c.fault.path || c.path || '')
394	    if (!path) continue
395	    const later = all.filter((x) => x.seq > c.seq)
396	    const successes = later.filter((x) => x.result && !x.result.isError && callTouches(x, path))
397	    if (successes.length === 0) continue
398	    const firstSuccessSeq = successes[0]!.seq
399	    const verified = later.some(
400	      (x) => x.seq <= firstSuccessSeq && x.read && x.result && !x.result.isError && callTouches(x, path),
401	    )
402	    if (verified) recoveredIds.add(c.toolUseId)
403	  }
404	
405	  if (recoveredIds.size === 0) {
406	    return steps.map((s) =>
407	      s.calls.some((c) => c.recovered) ? { ...s, calls: s.calls.map((c) => ({ ...c, recovered: false })) } : s,
408	    )
409	  }
410	  return steps.map((s) => ({
411	    ...s,
412	    calls: s.calls.map((c) => {
413	      const next = recoveredIds.has(c.toolUseId)
414	      return c.recovered === next ? c : { ...c, recovered: next }
415	    }),
416	  }))
417	}
418	
419	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- the fold
420	
421	function upsertStep(steps: StepView[], step: number): { steps: StepView[]; idx: number } {
422	  const idx = steps.findIndex((s) => s.step === step)
423	  if (idx >= 0) return { steps, idx }
424	  const next = [...steps, { step, thinking: null, texts: [], calls: [] }].sort((a, b) => a.step - b.step)
425	  return { steps: next, idx: next.findIndex((s) => s.step === step) }
426	}
427	
428	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- usage
429	
430	function asUsage(v: unknown, fallback: Usage = { input_tokens: 0, output_tokens: 0 }): Usage {
431	  const o = asRecord(v)
432	  const out: Usage = {
433	    input_tokens: num(o.input_tokens, fallback.input_tokens),
434	    output_tokens: num(o.output_tokens, fallback.output_tokens),
435	  }
436	  const cr = typeof o.cache_read_input_tokens === 'number' ? o.cache_read_input_tokens : fallback.cache_read_input_tokens
437	  const cc =
438	    typeof o.cache_creation_input_tokens === 'number'
439	      ? o.cache_creation_input_tokens
440	      : fallback.cache_creation_input_tokens
441	  if (cr !== undefined) out.cache_read_input_tokens = cr
442	  if (cc !== undefined) out.cache_creation_input_tokens = cc
443	  return out
444	}
445	
446	/**
447	 * Sum token usage over LLM calls, counting only the LAST attempt of each step so a request that
448	 * was retried after a 429/5xx is not billed twice in the header.
449	 */
450	export function sumLlmUsage(calls: LlmCallView[]): Usage {
451	  const lastPerStep = new Map<number, LlmCallView>()
452	  for (const c of calls) {
453	    const prev = lastPerStep.get(c.step)
454	    if (!prev || c.attempt >= prev.attempt) lastPerStep.set(c.step, c)
455	  }
456	  const out: Usage = { input_tokens: 0, output_tokens: 0 }
457	  let cr = 0
458	  let cc = 0
459	  let sawCache = false
460	  for (const c of lastPerStep.values()) {
461	    out.input_tokens += c.usage.input_tokens
462	    out.output_tokens += c.usage.output_tokens
463	    if (typeof c.usage.cache_read_input_tokens === 'number') {
464	      cr += c.usage.cache_read_input_tokens
465	      sawCache = true
466	    }
467	    if (typeof c.usage.cache_creation_input_tokens === 'number') {
468	      cc += c.usage.cache_creation_input_tokens
469	      sawCache = true
470	    }
471	  }
472	  if (sawCache) {
473	    out.cache_read_input_tokens = cr
474	    out.cache_creation_input_tokens = cc
475	  }
476	  return out
477	}
478	
479	function usageIsEmpty(u: Usage | null | undefined): boolean {
480	  return !u || ((u.input_tokens ?? 0) === 0 && (u.output_tokens ?? 0) === 0)
481	}
482	
483	function asRecord(v: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
484	  return v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) ? (v as Record<string, unknown>) : {}
485	}
486	
487	function num(v: unknown, fallback: number): number {
488	  return typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? v : fallback
489	}
490	
491	function str(v: unknown): string | null {
492	  return typeof v === 'string' ? v : null
493	}
494	
495	function asOrigin(v: unknown): ErrorOrigin | null {
496	  return typeof v === 'string' && (ERROR_ORIGINS as readonly string[]).includes(v) ? (v as ErrorOrigin) : null
497	}
498	
499	function asLayer(v: unknown): ErrorLayer | null {
500	  return typeof v === 'string' && (ERROR_LAYERS as readonly string[]).includes(v) ? (v as ErrorLayer) : null
501	}
502	
503	function asCode(v: unknown): ErrorCode | null {
504	  return typeof v === 'string' && (ERROR_CODES as readonly string[]).includes(v) ? (v as ErrorCode) : null
505	}
506	
507	function asFault(v: unknown): FaultFired | null {
508	  const o = asRecord(v)
509	  if (typeof o.kind !== 'string' || typeof o.path !== 'string') return null
510	  const fault: FaultFired = {
511	    step: num(o.step, 0),
512	    kind: o.kind as FaultFired['kind'],
513	    path: o.path,
514	    mode: (o.mode as FaultFired['mode']) ?? 'transient',
515	  }
516	  const origin = asOrigin(o.origin)
517	  const layer = asLayer(o.layer)
518	  if (origin) fault.origin = origin
519	  if (layer) fault.layer = layer
520	  if (typeof o.description === 'string' && o.description.length > 0) fault.description = o.description
521	  return fault
522	}
523	
524	/** Structural check for an ErrorClass payload; anything short of (origin, layer, code, label) is ignored. */
525	export function asErrorClass(v: unknown): ErrorClass | null {
526	  const o = asRecord(v)
527	  const origin = asOrigin(o.origin)
528	  const layer = asLayer(o.layer)
529	  const code = asCode(o.code)
530	  if (!origin || !layer || !code || typeof o.label !== 'string') return null
531	  return {
532	    origin,
533	    layer,
534	    code,
535	    kind: typeof o.kind === 'string' ? (o.kind as ErrorClass['kind']) : null,
536	    label: o.label,
537	    // Unknown execution is not failed execution: only an explicit `true` counts as known.
538	    outcome_known: o.outcome_known === true,
539	    side_effect_applied: typeof o.side_effect_applied === 'boolean' ? o.side_effect_applied : null,
540	    detail: str(o.detail),
541	  }
542	}
543	
544	function asInterruption(v: unknown, fallbackStep: number | null): Interruption | null {
545	  const o = asRecord(v)
546	  const layer = asLayer(o.layer)
547	  const code = asCode(o.code)
548	  if (!layer || !code) return null
549	  return {
550	    step: typeof o.step === 'number' ? o.step : fallbackStep,
551	    layer,
552	    code,
553	    label: str(o.label) ?? '',
554	    tool_use_id: str(o.tool_use_id),
555	    tool: str(o.tool) as Interruption['tool'],
556	    path: str(o.path),
557	    outcome_known: o.outcome_known === true,
558	    planned: o.planned === true,
559	    resumed: o.resumed === true,
560	    worker_generation: Math.max(1, Math.trunc(num(o.worker_generation, 1))),
561	    at: str(o.at) ?? '',
562	    detail: str(o.detail),
563	  }
564	}
565	
566	function asToolOutcome(v: unknown): ToolOutcome | null {
567	  return v === 'executed' || v === 'failed' || v === 'not_executed' || v === 'unknown' ? v : null
568	}
569	
570	function asSandboxRef(v: unknown): { id: string; alive: boolean } | null {
571	  const o = asRecord(v)
572	  if (typeof o.id !== 'string') return null
573	  return { id: o.id, alive: o.alive !== false }
574	}
575	
576	function asScenario(v: unknown): Scenario | null {
577	  const o = asRecord(v)
578	  if (typeof o.id !== 'string' || typeof o.title !== 'string') return null
579	  return o as unknown as Scenario
580	}
581	
582	function sameInterruption(a: Interruption, b: Interruption): boolean {
583	  if (a.tool_use_id && b.tool_use_id) return a.tool_use_id === b.tool_use_id
584	  return a.at === b.at && a.code === b.code && (a.step ?? null) === (b.step ?? null)
585	}
586	
587	function nextSeq(steps: StepView[]): number {
588	  let max = -1
589	  for (const s of steps) for (const c of s.calls) if (c.seq > max) max = c.seq
590	  return max + 1
591	}
592	
593	/** Fold one event into the view state. Pure: never mutates `state`. */
594	export function reduce(state: ViewState, ev: Event): ViewState {
595	  if (!ev || typeof ev.type !== 'string') return state
596	  const data = asRecord(ev.data)
597	  const step = typeof ev.step === 'number' ? ev.step : state.step
598	  const next: ViewState = {
599	    ...state,
600	    runId: state.runId ?? str(ev.run_id),
601	    lastEventId: typeof ev.id === 'number' && ev.id > state.lastEventId ? ev.id : state.lastEventId,
602	    eventCount: state.eventCount + 1,
603	    step: Math.max(state.step, typeof ev.step === 'number' ? ev.step : 0),
604	  }
605	
606	  switch (ev.type) {
607	    case 'run.started': {
608	      next.status = 'running'
609	      next.scenarioId = str(data.scenario_id) ?? next.scenarioId
610	      next.model = str(data.model) ?? next.model
611	      next.seed = typeof data.seed === 'number' ? data.seed : next.seed
612	      next.maxSteps = num(data.max_steps, next.maxSteps)
613	      next.anthropicWorkspace = str(data.anthropic_workspace) ?? next.anthropicWorkspace
614	      next.startedAt = state.startedAt ?? str(ev.ts)
615	      return next
616	    }
617	
618	    case 'episode.reset': {
619	      next.episodeId = str(data.episode_id) ?? next.episodeId
620	      next.taskPrompt = str(data.task_prompt) ?? next.taskPrompt
621	      if (Array.isArray(data.files)) next.files = data.files as FileEntry[]
622	      if (next.status === 'queued') next.status = 'running'
623	      next.scenario = asScenario(data.scenario) ?? next.scenario
624	      const sandboxId = str(data.sandbox_id)
625	      if (sandboxId) {
626	        next.sandboxId = sandboxId
627	        next.sandboxAlive = true
628	      }
629	      if (typeof data.attempt === 'number') next.resetAttempt = data.attempt
630	      return next
631	    }
632	
633	    case 'turn.text': {
634	      const text = str(data.text)
635	      if (!text || text.trim().length === 0) return next
636	      const { steps, idx } = upsertStep(state.steps, step)
637	      const target = steps[idx]!
638	      const updated = [...steps]
639	      updated[idx] = { ...target, texts: [...target.texts, text] }
640	      next.steps = updated
641	      return next
642	    }
643	
644	    case 'turn.thinking': {
645	      const text = str(data.text)
646	      if (!text || text.trim().length === 0) return next
647	      const { steps, idx } = upsertStep(state.steps, step)
648	      const target = steps[idx]!
649	      const updated = [...steps]
650	      updated[idx] = { ...target, thinking: target.thinking ? `${target.thinking}\n\n${text}` : text }
651	      next.steps = updated
652	      return next
653	    }
654	
655	    case 'llm.call': {
656	      const view: LlmCallView = {
657	        step,
658	        attempt: Math.max(1, Math.trunc(num(data.attempt, 1))),
659	        model: str(data.model) ?? state.model ?? 'unknown',
660	        stopReason: str(data.stop_reason),
661	        usage: asUsage(data.usage),
662	        durationMs: typeof data.duration_ms === 'number' ? data.duration_ms : null,
663	        requestId: str(data.request_id),
664	        error: str(data.error),
665	      }
666	      // Same (step, attempt) twice means a replayed frame: replace rather than append.
667	      const i = state.llmCalls.findIndex((c) => c.step === view.step && c.attempt === view.attempt)
668	      const llmCalls = i >= 0 ? state.llmCalls.map((c, j) => (j === i ? view : c)) : [...state.llmCalls, view]
669	      next.llmCalls = llmCalls
670	      // `run.finished` (or a terminal record) is the authoritative total; before that, keep a
671	      // running sum so the header moves while the run is in flight.
672	      if (!isTerminal(state.status)) next.usage = sumLlmUsage(llmCalls)
673	      return next
674	    }
675	
676	    case 'tool.call': {
677	      const tool = str(data.tool) ?? 'unknown'
678	      const toolUseId = str(data.tool_use_id) ?? `${ev.id}`
679	      const input = asRecord(data.input)
680	      const { steps, idx } = upsertStep(state.steps, step)
681	      const target = steps[idx]!
682	      if (target.calls.some((c) => c.toolUseId === toolUseId)) {
683	        next.steps = steps
684	        return next
685	      }
686	      const cls = classify(tool, input)
687	      const call: ToolCallView = {
688	        seq: nextSeq(steps),
689	        step,
690	        toolUseId,
691	        tool,
692	        input,
693	        path: cls.path,
694	        command: cls.command,
695	        mutating: cls.mutating,
696	        read: cls.read,
697	        result: null,
698	        fault: null,
699	        recovered: false,
700	      }
701	      const updated = [...steps]
702	      updated[idx] = { ...target, calls: [...target.calls, call] }
703	      next.steps = applyRecovery(updated)
704	      return next
705	    }
706	
707	    case 'tool.result': {
708	      const toolUseId = str(data.tool_use_id)
709	      if (!toolUseId) return next
710	      const isError = data.is_error === true
711	      const result = parseToolResult(
712	        toolUseId,
713	        str(data.output) ?? '',
714	        isError,
715	        num(data.duration_ms, 0),
716	      )
717	      result.errorClass = isError ? asErrorClass(data.error_class) : null
718	      result.outcome = asToolOutcome(data.outcome)
719	      result.attempts = typeof data.attempts === 'number' ? Math.max(1, Math.trunc(data.attempts)) : null
720	      result.sandbox = asSandboxRef(data.sandbox)
721	      if (result.sandbox) {
722	        next.sandboxId = result.sandbox.id
723	        next.sandboxAlive = result.sandbox.alive
724	      }
725	      const fault = asFault(data.fault)
726	      let found = false
727	      const updated = state.steps.map((s) => {
728	        if (!s.calls.some((c) => c.toolUseId === toolUseId)) return s
729	        found = true
730	        return {
731	          ...s,
732	          calls: s.calls.map((c) =>
733	            c.toolUseId === toolUseId ? { ...c, result, fault: fault ?? c.fault } : c,
734	          ),
735	        }
736	      })
737	      if (!found) {
738	        // A result without its call (resumed mid-run, or events dropped): synthesise the call so
739	        // the timeline still shows the output rather than silently losing it.
740	        const tool = str(data.tool) ?? 'unknown'
741	        const { steps, idx } = upsertStep(state.steps, step)
742	        const target = steps[idx]!
743	        const cls = classify(tool, {})
744	        const synth: ToolCallView = {
745	          seq: nextSeq(steps),
746	          step,
747	          toolUseId,
748	          tool,
749	          input: {},
750	          path: cls.path,
751	          command: cls.command,
752	          mutating: cls.mutating,
753	          read: cls.read,
754	          result,
755	          fault,
756	          recovered: false,
757	        }
758	        const s2 = [...steps]
759	        s2[idx] = { ...target, calls: [...target.calls, synth] }
760	        next.steps = applyRecovery(s2)
761	        return next
762	      }
763	      if (fault && !state.faults.some((f) => f.step === fault.step && f.path === fault.path && f.kind === fault.kind)) {
764	        next.faults = [...state.faults, fault]
765	      }
766	      next.steps = applyRecovery(updated)
767	      return next
768	    }
769	
770	    case 'fault.fired': {
771	      const fault = asFault(data) ?? asFault(data.fault)
772	      if (!fault) return next
773	      const known = state.faults.some(
774	        (f) => f.step === fault.step && f.path === fault.path && f.kind === fault.kind,
775	      )
776	      next.faults = known ? state.faults : [...state.faults, fault]
777	      // Attach to the call at that step that touched the path and has no fault yet.
778	      const updated = state.steps.map((s) => {
779	        if (s.step !== fault.step) return s
780	        let attached = false
781	        const calls = s.calls.map((c) => {
782	          if (attached || c.fault) return c
783	          if (!callTouches(c, fault.path) && c.path !== normalisePath(fault.path)) return c
784	          attached = true
785	          return { ...c, fault }
786	        })
787	        return attached ? { ...s, calls } : s
788	      })
789	      next.steps = applyRecovery(updated)
790	      return next
791	    }
792	
793	    case 'workspace.diff': {
794	      if (Array.isArray(data.files)) next.files = data.files as FileEntry[]
795	      if (Array.isArray(data.diffs)) next.diffs = data.diffs as FileDiff[]
796	      return next
797	    }
798	
799	    case 'episode.evaluated': {
800	      const checks = Array.isArray(data.checks) ? (data.checks as Check[]) : []
801	      next.evaluation = {
802	        episode_id: str(data.episode_id) ?? state.episodeId ?? '',
803	        score: num(data.score, 0),
804	        passed: data.passed === true,
805	        checks,
806	        tests: asRecord(data.tests) as unknown as EvaluateResponse['tests'],
807	        ledger: Array.isArray(data.ledger) ? (data.ledger as EvaluateResponse['ledger']) : [],
808	      }
809	      return next
810	    }
811	
812	    case 'run.finished': {
813	      // A missing or unrecognised status keeps the previous one; it never defaults to success.
814	      next.status = asRunStatus(data.status) ?? state.status
815	      next.usage = asUsage(data.usage, state.usage)
816	      next.durationMs = typeof data.duration_ms === 'number' ? data.duration_ms : state.durationMs
817	      next.error = str(data.error) ?? state.error
818	      next.finishedAt = str(ev.ts) ?? state.finishedAt
819	      next.errorClass = asErrorClass(data.error_class) ?? state.errorClass
820	      const es = str(data.evaluation_status)
821	      if (es === 'ok' || es === 'failed' || es === 'skipped') next.evaluationStatus = es
822	      next.evaluationError = str(data.evaluation_error) ?? state.evaluationError
823	      return next
824	    }
825	
826	    case 'interruption': {
827	      const it = asInterruption(data, typeof ev.step === 'number' ? ev.step : null)
828	      if (!it) return next
829	      const i = state.interruptions.findIndex((x) => sameInterruption(x, it))
830	      next.interruptions = i >= 0 ? state.interruptions.map((x, j) => (j === i ? it : x)) : [...state.interruptions, it]
831	      next.workerGeneration = Math.max(state.workerGeneration, it.worker_generation)
832	      return next
833	    }
834	
835	    case 'run.resumed': {
836	      const gen = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(num(data.worker_generation, state.workerGeneration + 1)))
837	      const resumed: RunResumedData = {
838	        worker_generation: gen,
839	        resumed_from_event_id: num(data.resumed_from_event_id, state.lastEventId),
840	        dangling_tool_use_id: str(data.dangling_tool_use_id),
841	        resumed_at: str(data.resumed_at) ?? str(ev.ts) ?? '',
842	      }
843	      const known = state.resumes.some((r) => r.worker_generation === gen)
844	      next.resumes = known ? state.resumes : [...state.resumes, resumed]
845	      next.workerGeneration = Math.max(state.workerGeneration, gen)
846	      // The interruption this resume answers (matched by the dangling call) is now `resumed`.
847	      const dangling = resumed.dangling_tool_use_id
848	      if (dangling) {
849	        next.interruptions = state.interruptions.map((x) =>
850	          x.tool_use_id === dangling && !x.resumed ? { ...x, resumed: true } : x,
851	        )
852	      }
853	      if (!isTerminal(state.status)) next.status = 'running'
854	      return next
855	    }
856	
857	    case 'episode.sandbox': {
858	      const sandboxId = str(data.sandbox_id)
859	      const status = str(data.status)
860	      if (!sandboxId || (status !== 'alive' && status !== 'terminated' && status !== 'replaced')) return next
861	      const notice: EpisodeSandboxData = {
862	        sandbox_id: sandboxId,
863	        status,
864	        reason: str(data.reason),
865	        step: typeof data.step === 'number' ? data.step : typeof ev.step === 'number' ? ev.step : null,
866	      }
867	      next.sandboxEvents = [...state.sandboxEvents, notice]
868	      next.sandboxId = sandboxId
869	      next.sandboxAlive = status === 'alive'
870	      return next
871	    }
872	
873	    case 'log': {
874	      const line = data as unknown as LogLine
875	      if (typeof line?.ev !== 'string') return next
876	      next.logs = [...state.logs, line].slice(-LOG_BUFFER_LIMIT)
877	      return next
878	    }
879	
880	    default:
881	      return next
882	  }
883	}
884	
885	export function reduceAll(events: Event[], from: ViewState = initialState()): ViewState {
886	  return events.reduce(reduce, from)
887	}
888	
889	/**
890	 * Seed the view from a whole RunRecord (page refresh mid-run, or a bundled demo). Record-level
891	 * fields win over anything the events implied, because the record is the harness's own summary.
892	 */
893	export function fromRunRecord(rec: RunRecord): ViewState {
894	  const folded = reduceAll(rec.events ?? [])
895	  // The record's usage is the harness's own running total and wins whenever it says anything.
896	  // The one exception is an in-flight record whose usage is still the {0,0} default while its
897	  // events already carry `llm.call` sums — showing zeros there would be a regression.
898	  const usage =
899	    rec.usage && (isTerminal(rec.status) || !usageIsEmpty(rec.usage) || usageIsEmpty(folded.usage))
900	      ? rec.usage
901	      : folded.usage
902	  return {
903	    ...folded,
904	    runId: rec.run_id ?? folded.runId,
905	    status: asRunStatus(rec.status) ?? folded.status,
906	    scenarioId: rec.scenario_id ?? folded.scenarioId,
907	    model: rec.model ?? folded.model,
908	    seed: rec.seed ?? folded.seed,
909	    maxSteps: rec.max_steps ?? folded.maxSteps,
910	    episodeId: rec.episode_id ?? folded.episodeId,
911	    taskPrompt: rec.task_prompt ?? folded.taskPrompt,
912	    startedAt: rec.created_at ?? folded.startedAt,
913	    finishedAt: rec.finished_at ?? folded.finishedAt,
914	    evaluation: rec.evaluation ?? folded.evaluation,
915	    usage,
916	    error: rec.error ?? folded.error,
917	    errorClass: rec.error_class ?? folded.errorClass,
918	    interruptions:
919	      Array.isArray(rec.interruptions) && rec.interruptions.length > 0 ? rec.interruptions : folded.interruptions,
920	    workerGeneration: Math.max(
921	      folded.workerGeneration,
922	      typeof rec.worker_generation === 'number' ? rec.worker_generation : 1,
923	    ),
924	  }
925	}
926	
927	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- selectors
928	
929	export function allCalls(state: ViewState): ToolCallView[] {
930	  return flatten(state.steps)
931	}
932	
933	export function faultCount(state: ViewState): number {
934	  return allCalls(state).filter((c) => c.fault).length
935	}
936	
937	export function recoveredCount(state: ViewState): number {
938	  return allCalls(state).filter((c) => c.fault && c.recovered).length
939	}
940	
941	export function elapsedMs(state: ViewState, now: number = Date.now()): number | null {
942	  if (state.durationMs !== null) return state.durationMs
943	  if (!state.startedAt) return null
944	  const t0 = Date.parse(state.startedAt)
945	  if (Number.isNaN(t0)) return null
946	  const end = state.finishedAt ? Date.parse(state.finishedAt) : now
947	  return Math.max(0, (Number.isNaN(end) ? now : end) - t0)
948	}

85	
86	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- MCP tool I/O
87	
88	ToolName = Literal["run_command", "read_file", "write_file", "list_dir"]
89	ErrorCode = Literal[
90	    # OS/HTTP-style codes the AGENT sees (the same text whether injected or real):
91	    "ENOENT",      # no such file or directory
92	    "EACCES",      # permission denied
93	    "ETIMEDOUT",   # the response never arrived (ack lost); the operation MAY have completed
94	    "EINVAL",      # bad argument (path outside /workspace, bad mode, is-a-directory)
95	    # Real-failure codes (never injected; only the layer that really failed produces them):
96	    "ESANDBOX",    # the Modal Sandbox is gone/unavailable (terminated, not found, exec failed)
97	    "ETRANSPORT",  # harness <-> sandbox-env HTTP/MCP failure (connection reset, 5xx, client abort)
98	    "EHARNESS",    # the harness worker itself was interrupted while the call was in flight
99	    "EMODEL",      # Anthropic API failure that ended the turn
100	    "EGYM",        # gym control-plane (reset/observe/evaluate/delete) failure
101	    "EINTERNAL",   # unexpected exception inside sandbox-env (a bug; see detail)
102	    "ENOEPISODE",  # missing/unknown X-Faultline-Episode header
103	]
104	
105	# Provenance: WHO caused a failure. The agent never sees this; ledger, events and UI do.
106	ErrorOrigin = Literal[
107	    "injected",  # intercepted at the tool boundary by the fault plan; nothing real failed
108	    "staged",    # the scenario really changed the world at reset (e.g. deleted a file); the error is a real OS error
109	    "real",      # unplanned failure of a real component (sandbox, transport, harness, model, gym, filesystem)
110	]
111	ErrorLayer = Literal["filesystem", "sandbox", "transport", "harness", "model", "gym", "boundary"]
112	
113	
114	class ErrorClass(BaseModel):
115	    """Differentiated classification of one failure, for humans and the UI (never shown to the agent).
116	
117	    Fixed `label` vocabulary (apps/web renders these verbatim; see docs/error-taxonomy.md):
118	      injected/boundary  "simulated: missing file (file still on disk)"
119	                         "simulated: write denied (nothing written)"
120	                         "simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)"
121	      staged/filesystem  "staged: file absent since reset"
122	      real/filesystem    "real: OS error in sandbox"
123	      real/sandbox       "real: sandbox terminated or unavailable"
124	      real/transport     "real: transport failure harness<->sandbox-env (outcome unknown)"
125	      real/harness       "real: harness worker interrupted mid-call (outcome unknown)"
126	      real/model         "real: model API error"
127	      real/gym           "real: gym control-plane failure"
128	      real/boundary      "real: internal error in sandbox-env"
129	    """
130	
131	    origin: ErrorOrigin
132	    layer: ErrorLayer
133	    code: ErrorCode
134	    kind: FaultKind | None = Field(default=None, description="injected/staged only: which fault kind")
135	    label: str
136	    outcome_known: bool = Field(description="False when the side effect may or may not have happened (ack lost, transport, harness interruption)")
137	    side_effect_applied: bool | None = Field(default=None, description="ground truth from the ledger when known: did the write/command actually run?")
138	    detail: str | None = None
139	
140	
141	STDIO_CAP = 8_000  # chars per stream in run_command
142	CONTENT_CAP = 32_000  # chars in read_file
143

299	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- harness runs & events
300	
301	ToolOutcome = Literal[
302	    "executed",      # the sandbox ran it and it succeeded
303	    "failed",        # the sandbox ran it and reported failure (non-zero exit, real ENOENT/EACCES)
304	    "not_executed",  # refused/short-circuited before the sandbox saw it (injected EACCES/ENOENT, EINVAL, ESANDBOX)
305	    "unknown",       # may or may not have run (ack lost, transport failure, harness interruption)
306	]
307	
308	EventType = Literal[
309	    "run.started",
310	    "episode.reset",
311	    "turn.text",
312	    "tool.call",
313	    "tool.result",
314	    "fault.fired",
315	    "workspace.diff",

326	
327	class Event(BaseModel):
328	    """Append-only, single-writer (run_episode). `id` is the 0-based sequence within the run and is
329	    what SSE uses as the event id / Last-Event-ID.
330	
331	    data shapes by type:
332	      run.started       {scenario_id, model, seed, max_steps, anthropic_workspace?: masked}
333	      episode.reset     {episode_id, files: [FileEntry], task_prompt, sandbox_id (short), attempt (1 = first provisioning)}
334	      turn.text         {text}
335	      tool.call         {tool, input, tool_use_id}
336	      tool.result       {tool_use_id, tool, output: str, is_error: bool, duration_ms, fault?: FaultFired,
337	                         outcome: executed|failed|not_executed|unknown, error_class?: ErrorClass (iff is_error),
338	                         error_code?: str (compat), attempts: int (harness retries of this call; 1 = none),
339	                         sandbox: {id: short, alive: bool}}
340	      fault.fired       FaultFired
341	      workspace.diff    {files: [FileEntry], diffs: [FileDiff]}
342	      episode.evaluated EvaluateResponse
343	      run.finished      {status, usage: {input_tokens, output_tokens}, duration_ms, error?: str,
344	                         evaluation_status: ok|failed|skipped, evaluation_error?: str, steps, score?,
345	                         error_class?: ErrorClass (present whenever status is unevaluated|interrupted|error)}
346	      log               {svc, lvl, ev, msg, ...}   (unified log line mirrored into the stream)
347	      llm.call          {attempt, model, stop_reason?, request_id?, usage: Usage-shaped {input_tokens, output_tokens, cache_read_input_tokens?, cache_creation_input_tokens?}, duration_ms, error?}
348	      turn.thinking     {text}   (summarised thinking, models that expose it)
349	      interruption      Interruption   (a REAL interruption: origin is always "real")
350	      run.resumed       {worker_generation, resumed_from_event_id, dangling_tool_use_id?, resumed_at}
351	      episode.sandbox   {sandbox_id, status: alive|terminated|replaced, reason, step}   (the environment noticed the worker changed)
352	    Every tool.result with is_error carries data.error_class: ErrorClass; every fault.fired carries origin.
353	    """
354	
355	    id: int
356	    ts: str
357	    run_id: str
358	    type: EventType
359	    step: int | None = None
360	    data: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
361	
362	
363	RunStatus = Literal[
364	    "queued",
365	    "running",
366	    "ok",           # agent submitted / ended, evaluation succeeded
367	    "truncated",    # step budget exhausted (still evaluated)
368	    "unevaluated",  # loop finished but evaluate() failed — NOT ok, NOT an agent error
369	    "interrupted",  # a real interruption ended the run without resume (sandbox gone, worker died, ...)
370	    "error",        # the harness could not run the episode (model auth, gym reset failure, bug)
371	]
372	
373	
374	class Interruption(BaseModel):
375	    """A real interruption observed by the harness (origin is always real)."""
376	
377	    step: int | None = None
378	    layer: ErrorLayer
379	    code: ErrorCode
380	    label: str
381	    tool_use_id: str | None = Field(default=None, description="the in-flight tool call, if any")
382	    tool: ToolName | None = None
383	    path: str | None = None
384	    outcome_known: bool = False
385	    planned: bool = Field(default=False, description="True when a HarnessFault in the scenario triggered it (still a real failure)")
386	    resumed: bool = Field(default=False, description="a new worker picked the run up afterwards")
387	    worker_generation: int = Field(default=1, description="which worker observed it")
388	    at: str
389	    detail: str | None = None
390	
391	MODEL_ALLOWLIST = ["claude-haiku-4-5", "claude-sonnet-5", "claude-opus-5"]
392	DEFAULT_MODEL = "claude-haiku-4-5"
393	
394	
395	class RunRequest(BaseModel):
396	    scenario_id: str
397	    model: str | None = Field(default=None, description="must be in MODEL_ALLOWLIST; None = ANTHROPIC_MODEL env")
398	    seed: int | None = None
399	    max_steps: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1, le=40)
400

1	/**
2	 * Wire contracts mirrored from packages/common/faultline_common/schemas.py.
3	 *
4	 * Keep field names identical to the pydantic models — the harness serialises those models
5	 * straight onto the wire. If a field is missing here it is simply not rendered; if a field is
6	 * added here it MUST be optional so an older harness still type-checks at runtime.
7	 */
8	
9	export type FaultKind = 'missing_file' | 'denied_write' | 'ack_lost'
10	export type FaultMode = 'sticky' | 'transient'
11	
12	export interface FaultSpec {
13	  kind: FaultKind
14	  path: string
15	  mode: FaultMode
16	  hits: number | null
17	  delay_ms: number
18	}
19	
20	export type HarnessFaultKind = 'worker_crash' | 'transport_abort'
21	
22	/**
23	 * A REAL interruption the harness inflicts on itself at a chosen call (chaos trigger).
24	 *   worker_crash    : the run_episode process exits hard after dispatching the call; the side
25	 *                     effect completes at sandbox-env and a fresh worker resumes the run.
26	 *   transport_abort : the in-flight HTTP request is cancelled client-side; the server still
27	 *                     completes it; the agent gets ETRANSPORT with outcome unknown.
28	 */
29	export interface HarnessFault {
30	  kind: HarnessFaultKind
31	  tool: ToolName
32	  path: string
33	  nth: number
34	  after_ms: number
35	}
36	
37	export interface CheckSpec {
38	  id: string
39	  description: string
40	  weight: number
41	}
42	
43	/** A fault class in play (kind, layer, origin, description) — never paths, hits or timing. */
44	export interface FaultPublic {
45	  kind: FaultKind | HarnessFaultKind
46	  origin: ErrorOrigin
47	  layer: ErrorLayer
48	  description: string
49	}
50	
51	export interface Scenario {
52	  id: string
53	  title: string
54	  description: string
55	  task_prompt: string
56	  max_steps: number
57	  fault_kinds: FaultKind[]
58	  /** Planned REAL interruptions (the UI may say "includes a real harness interruption"). Older harness: absent. */
59	  harness_faults?: HarnessFault[]
60	  /** What the grader will look for (public: id, description, weight). Older harness: absent. */
61	  checks?: CheckSpec[]
62	  faults_public?: FaultPublic[]
63	}
64	
65	export type ToolName = 'run_command' | 'read_file' | 'write_file' | 'list_dir'
66	
67	/**
68	 * OS/HTTP-style codes the agent sees (identical whether injected or real) plus the real-failure
69	 * codes only the layer that really failed produces. Mirrors `ErrorCode` in schemas.py.
70	 */
71	export type ErrorCode =
72	  | 'ENOENT' // no such file or directory
73	  | 'EACCES' // permission denied
74	  | 'ETIMEDOUT' // the response never arrived (ack lost, or a real client timeout)
75	  | 'EINVAL' // bad argument (path outside /workspace, bad mode, is-a-directory)
76	  | 'ESANDBOX' // the sandbox is gone/unavailable
77	  | 'ETRANSPORT' // harness <-> sandbox-env HTTP/MCP failure
78	  | 'EHARNESS' // the harness worker itself was interrupted while the call was in flight
79	  | 'EMODEL' // Anthropic API failure that ended the turn
80	  | 'EGYM' // gym control-plane (reset/observe/evaluate/delete) failure
81	  | 'EINTERNAL' // unexpected exception inside sandbox-env
82	  | 'ENOEPISODE' // missing/unknown X-Faultline-Episode header
83	
84	export const ERROR_CODES: readonly ErrorCode[] = [
85	  'ENOENT',
86	  'EACCES',
87	  'ETIMEDOUT',
88	  'EINVAL',
89	  'ESANDBOX',
90	  'ETRANSPORT',
91	  'EHARNESS',
92	  'EMODEL',
93	  'EGYM',
94	  'EINTERNAL',
95	  'ENOEPISODE',
96	]
97	
98	/** WHO caused a failure. The agent never sees this; ledger, events and UI do. */
99	export type ErrorOrigin =
100	  | 'injected' // intercepted at the tool boundary by the fault plan; nothing real failed
101	  | 'staged' // the scenario really changed the world at reset; the error is a real OS error
102	  | 'real' // unplanned failure of a real component
103	
104	export const ERROR_ORIGINS: readonly ErrorOrigin[] = ['injected', 'staged', 'real']
105	
106	/** WHICH layer really failed. */
107	export type ErrorLayer = 'filesystem' | 'sandbox' | 'transport' | 'harness' | 'model' | 'gym' | 'boundary'
108	
109	export const ERROR_LAYERS: readonly ErrorLayer[] = [
110	  'filesystem',
111	  'sandbox',
112	  'transport',
113	  'harness',
114	  'model',
115	  'gym',
116	  'boundary',
117	]
118	
119	/**
120	 * Differentiated classification of one failure (docs/error-taxonomy.md). `label` is a fixed
121	 * string the UI renders verbatim.
122	 */
123	export interface ErrorClass {
124	  origin: ErrorOrigin
125	  layer: ErrorLayer
126	  code: ErrorCode
127	  /** injected/staged only: which fault kind */
128	  kind?: FaultKind | null
129	  label: string
130	  /** false when the side effect may or may not have happened (ack lost, transport, harness interruption) */
131	  outcome_known: boolean
132	  /** ground truth from the ledger when known: did the write/command actually run? */
133	  side_effect_applied?: boolean | null
134	  detail?: string | null
135	}

141	  detail?: string | null
142	}

151	  /** boundary for injected, filesystem for staged. Schema default: boundary. */
152	  layer?: ErrorLayer
153	  /** One plain-language sentence for the UI. */
154	  description?: string
155	}

251	export type RunStatus =
252	  | 'queued'
253	  | 'running'
254	  | 'ok' // agent submitted / ended, evaluation succeeded
255	  | 'truncated' // step budget exhausted (still evaluated)
256	  | 'unevaluated' // loop finished but evaluate() failed — NOT ok, NOT an agent error
257	  | 'interrupted' // a real interruption ended the run without resume
258	  | 'error' // the harness could not run the episode (model auth, gym reset failure, bug)

260	/** A real interruption observed by the harness (origin is always real). */
261	export interface Interruption {
262	  step?: number | null
263	  layer: ErrorLayer
264	  code: ErrorCode
265	  label: string
266	  /** the in-flight tool call, if any */
267	  tool_use_id?: string | null
268	  tool?: ToolName | null

270	  outcome_known: boolean
271	  /** true when a HarnessFault in the scenario triggered it (still a real failure) */
272	  planned: boolean
273	  /** a new worker picked the run up afterwards */
274	  resumed: boolean
275	  /** which worker observed it */
276	  worker_generation: number
277	  at: string
278	  detail?: string | null
279	}

281	/** How a tool call actually ended, as the harness reports it on `tool.result.data.outcome`. */
282	export type ToolOutcome =
283	  | 'executed' // the sandbox ran it and it succeeded
284	  | 'failed' // the sandbox ran it and reported failure (non-zero exit, real ENOENT/EACCES)
285	  | 'not_executed' // refused/short-circuited before the sandbox saw it
286	  | 'unknown' // may or may not have run (ack lost, transport failure, harness interruption)

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:39:44.914Z
1	/**
2	 * Typed harness client: plain cross-origin JSON fetches plus a resilient run subscription.
3	 *
4	 * Reliability model (this is the interesting part):
5	 *  - The harness closes every SSE stream at ~110 s to stay under the hosting platform's 150 s request cap, so a
6	 *    clean close mid-run is NORMAL and must not be treated as a failure. The server announces it
7	 *    with `event: done` + `{reason:"window"}`; an older harness just closes, and we distinguish
8	 *    that from a real failure by whether the stream delivered anything first.
9	 *  - EventSource cannot set headers, so the resume point is passed both natively (the browser
10	 *    sends `Last-Event-ID` on its own reconnect) and as a `last_event_id` query param on ours.
11	 *    For the same reason the SSE request carries no `X-Faultline-User`; ownership is enforced on
12	 *    the JSON routes, and a run id is unguessable.
13	 *  - After two consecutive failed opens we stop trusting SSE and fall back to polling
14	 *    GET /runs/{id} every 1.5 s, which is strictly less efficient but works through anything.
15	 *  - No credentials are ever sent: the web origin and the harness origin are different
16	 *    subdomains and these are ordinary CORS requests. Identity travels as a header instead
17	 *    (ARCHITECTURE.md §6).
18	 */
19	
20	import { getLogger } from './log'
