64	export class HarnessError extends Error {
65	  constructor(
66	    message: string,
67	    readonly status?: number,
68	    readonly url?: string,
69	  ) {
70	    super(message)
71	    this.name = 'HarnessError'
72	  }
73	}
74	
75	/** True for a HarnessError carrying HTTP 404 — the harness's "not yours / not there". */
76	export function isNotFound(err: unknown): boolean {
77	  return err instanceof HarnessError && err.status === 404
78	}
79	
80	type HeaderMap = Record<string, string>
81	
82	function toHeaderMap(h: HeadersInit | undefined): HeaderMap {
83	  if (!h) return {}
84	  if (Array.isArray(h)) return Object.fromEntries(h)
85	  if (typeof Headers !== 'undefined' && h instanceof Headers) {
86	    const out: HeaderMap = {}
87	    h.forEach((v, k) => {
88	      out[k] = v
89	    })
90	    return out
91	  }
92	  return { ...(h as HeaderMap) }
93	}
94	
95	async function jsonFetch<T>(
96	  url: string,
97	  init: RequestInit = {},
98	  fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch,
99	  headers: HeaderMap = {},
100	): Promise<T> {
101	  const t0 = Date.now()
102	  let res: Response
103	  try {
104	    res = await fetchImpl(url, {
105	      ...init,
106	      credentials: 'omit',
107	      headers: { accept: 'application/json', ...headers, ...toHeaderMap(init.headers) },
108	    })
109	  } catch (err) {
110	    log.error('http.error', 'request failed', { url, error: String(err) })
111	    throw new HarnessError(`network error contacting ${url}: ${String(err)}`, undefined, url)
112	  }
113	  const durMs = Date.now() - t0
114	  if (!res.ok) {
115	    const body = await res.text().catch(() => '')
116	    // 404 is an expected answer for routes a harness build may not have yet (/me, /conversations).
117	    log[res.status === 404 ? 'warn' : 'error']('http.response', 'non-2xx', {
118	      url,
119	      status: res.status,
120	      dur_ms: durMs,
121	    })
122	    throw new HarnessError(
123	      `${res.status} ${res.statusText} from ${url}${body ? `: ${body.slice(0, 400)}` : ''}`,
124	      res.status,
125	      url,
126	    )
127	  }
128	  log.debug('http.response', 'ok', { url, status: res.status, dur_ms: durMs })
129	  return (await res.json()) as T
130	}
131	
132	/**
133	 * PLAN.md 2.3 specifies `GET /scenarios -> [Scenario]`, but the deployed harness answers
134	 * `{"scenarios": [...]}`. Rather than break on either, accept both shapes here — this is the one
135	 * place in the app that has to know, and a client that tolerates an envelope costs nothing.
136	 * Logged as a contract deviation.
137	 */
138	export function normaliseScenarios(body: unknown): Scenario[] {
139	  if (Array.isArray(body)) return body as Scenario[]
140	  if (body && typeof body === 'object') {
141	    const inner = (body as { scenarios?: unknown }).scenarios
142	    if (Array.isArray(inner)) {
143	      log.debug('scenarios.envelope', 'harness returned {scenarios: [...]}, unwrapping', {
144	        count: inner.length,
145	      })
146	      return inner as Scenario[]
147	    }
148	  }
149	  log.warn('scenarios.shape', 'unrecognised /scenarios payload; treating as empty', {
150	    got: typeof body,
151	  })
152	  return []
153	}
154	
155	/** Same tolerance as normaliseScenarios, for any list route: bare array or `{<key>: [...]}`. */
156	function normaliseList<T>(body: unknown, key: string): T[] {
157	  if (Array.isArray(body)) return body as T[]
158	  if (body && typeof body === 'object') {
159	    const inner = (body as Record<string, unknown>)[key]
160	    if (Array.isArray(inner)) {
161	      log.debug(`${key}.envelope`, `harness returned {${key}: [...]}, unwrapping`, { count: inner.length })
162	      return inner as T[]
163	    }
164	  }
165	  log.warn(`${key}.shape`, `unrecognised /${key} payload; treating as empty`, { got: typeof body })
166	  return []
167	}
168	
169	export interface HarnessClientOptions {
170	  /** Anonymous browser id (identity.ts). Sent as `X-Faultline-User` on every request when set. */
171	  userId?: string | null
172	  fetchImpl?: typeof fetch
173	}
174	
175	export class HarnessClient {
176	  readonly base: string
177	  readonly userId: string | null
178	  private readonly fetchImpl: typeof fetch
179	
180	  /**
181	   * `new HarnessClient(base, { userId, fetchImpl })`. The older positional form
182	   * `new HarnessClient(base, fetchImpl)` is still accepted.
183	   */
184	  constructor(base: string, opts: HarnessClientOptions | typeof fetch = {}) {
185	    this.base = base
186	    if (typeof opts === 'function') {
187	      this.fetchImpl = opts
188	      this.userId = null
189	    } else {
190	      this.fetchImpl = opts.fetchImpl ?? fetch
191	      this.userId = opts.userId ?? null
192	    }
193	  }
194	
195	  /** Headers every JSON request carries (identity). Also used by the polling fallback. */
196	  headers(): HeaderMap {
197	    return this.userId ? { [USER_HEADER]: this.userId } : {}
198	  }
199	
200	  private get<T>(path: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<T> {
201	    return jsonFetch<T>(`${this.base}${path}`, { signal }, this.fetchImpl, this.headers())
202	  }
203	
204	  private send<T>(method: 'POST' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE', path: string, body?: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<T> {
205	    return jsonFetch<T>(
206	      `${this.base}${path}`,
207	      {
208	        method,
209	        ...(body === undefined
210	          ? {}
211	          : { headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
212	        signal,
213	      },
214	      this.fetchImpl,
215	      this.headers(),
216	    )
217	  }
218	
219	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- health / catalogue
220	
221	  health(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Health> {
222	    return this.get<Health>('/health', signal)
223	  }
224	
225	  async scenarios(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Scenario[]> {
226	    const body = await this.get<Scenario[] | { scenarios?: Scenario[] }>('/scenarios', signal)
227	    return normaliseScenarios(body)
228	  }
229	
230	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- runs
231	
232	  createRun(req: RunRequest, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<CreateRunResponse> {
233	    log.info('run.create', 'POST /runs', {
234	      scenario_id: req.scenario_id,
235	      model: req.model ?? null,
236	    })
237	    return this.send<CreateRunResponse>('POST', '/runs', req, signal)
238	  }
239	
240	  getRun(runId: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<RunRecord> {
241	    return this.get<RunRecord>(`/runs/${encodeURIComponent(runId)}`, signal)
242	  }
243	
244	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- identity / conversations
245	  // The harness answers 404 both for an unknown id and for one owned by another browser identity
246	  // (no existence oracle); callers use isNotFound() to say "not found", never to fall back.
247	
248	  me(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<MeResponse> {
249	    return this.get<MeResponse>('/me', signal)
250	  }
251	
252	  async listConversations(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<ConversationSummary[]> {
253	    const body = await this.get<ConversationSummary[] | { conversations?: ConversationSummary[] }>(
254	      '/conversations',
255	      signal,
256	    )
257	    return normaliseList(body, 'conversations')
258	  }
259	
260	  getConversation(id: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<ConversationDetail> {
261	    return this.get<ConversationDetail>(`/conversations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, signal)
262	  }
263	
264	  createConversation(req: CreateConversationRequest, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Conversation> {
265	    log.info('conversation.create', 'POST /conversations', { scenario_id: req.scenario_id })
266	    return this.send<Conversation>('POST', '/conversations', req, signal)
267	  }
268	
269	  createConversationRun(
270	    id: string,
271	    req: ConversationRunRequest,
272	    signal?: AbortSignal,
273	  ): Promise<ConversationRunResponse> {
274	    log.info('conversation.run', 'POST /conversations/{id}/runs', {
275	      conversation_id: id,
276	      model: req.model ?? null,
277	    })
278	    return this.send<ConversationRunResponse>(
279	      'POST',
280	      `/conversations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/runs`,
281	      req,
282	      signal,
283	    )
284	  }
285	
286	  updateConversation(
287	    id: string,
288	    patch: UpdateConversationRequest,
289	    signal?: AbortSignal,
290	  ): Promise<Conversation> {
291	    return this.send<Conversation>('PATCH', `/conversations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, patch, signal)
292	  }
293	
294	  /** Soft archive (the harness never hard-deletes). */
295	  archiveConversation(id: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<ArchiveResponse> {
296	    log.info('conversation.archive', 'DELETE /conversations/{id}', { conversation_id: id })
297	    return this.send<ArchiveResponse>('DELETE', `/conversations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, undefined, signal)
298	  }
299	}
300	
301	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ run subscription
302	
303	export type TransportMode = 'sse' | 'poll' | 'closed'
304	
305	export interface TransportState {
306	  mode: TransportMode
307	  /** Consecutive failed SSE opens. Reset whenever a stream delivers an event. */
308	  errors: number
309	  detail?: string
310	}
311	
312	export interface SubscribeOptions {
313	  base: string
314	  runId: string
315	  /** Resume point; -1 means "from the beginning". */
316	  lastEventId?: number
317	  onEvents: (events: Event[]) => void
318	  onRecord?: (record: RunRecord) => void
319	  onTransport?: (state: TransportState) => void
320	  onDone?: () => void
321	  /** Extra headers for the polling fetch (identity). EventSource cannot carry them. */
322	  headers?: Record<string, string>
323	  /** Injection points for tests. */
324	  eventSourceImpl?: typeof EventSource | null
325	  fetchImpl?: typeof fetch
326	  pollIntervalMs?: number
327	  reconnectDelayMs?: number
328	  maxSseErrors?: number
329	  setTimeoutImpl?: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => unknown
330	  clearTimeoutImpl?: (handle: unknown) => void
331	}
332	
333	export interface Subscription {
334	  close: () => void
335	  /** Test/debug introspection. */
336	  state: () => TransportState
337	}
338	
339	function parseEvent(raw: string): Event | null {
340	  try {
341	    const v = JSON.parse(raw) as Event
342	    return typeof v?.id === 'number' && typeof v?.type === 'string' ? v : null
343	  } catch {
344	    return null
345	  }
346	}
347	
348	/** Payload of the server's `event: done` frame. Older harness builds send no data at all. */
349	export interface DonePayload {
350	  reason?: 'finished' | 'window' | string
351	  status?: RunStatus | string | null
352	  last_event_id?: number
353	  run_id?: string
354	}
355	
356	export function parseDone(raw: unknown): DonePayload | null {
357	  if (typeof raw !== 'string' || raw.length === 0) return null
358	  try {
359	    const v = JSON.parse(raw) as unknown
360	    return v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) ? (v as DonePayload) : null
361	  } catch {
362	    return null
363	  }
364	}
365	
366	/**
367	 * Does this `done` frame end the run, or merely the current ~110 s window?
368	 * Only an explicit `reason: "window"` with a non-terminal status means "reconnect"; anything
369	 * ambiguous (no data, unparseable, unknown reason, terminal status) is treated as finished, which
370	 * is the safe default — a wrongly-ended subscription can be re-opened, a wrongly-kept one spins.
371	 */
372	export function doneMeansReconnect(payload: DonePayload | null): boolean {
373	  if (!payload) return false
374	  if (payload.reason !== 'window') return false
375	  return !isTerminal((payload.status ?? null) as RunStatus | null)
376	}
377	
378	/**
379	 * Tail a run. Returns immediately; every update arrives through the callbacks.
380	 * Always call `close()` — it tears down the EventSource and any pending timer.
381	 */
382	export function subscribeRun(opts: SubscribeOptions): Subscription {
383	  const {
384	    base,
385	    runId,
386	    onEvents,
387	    onRecord,
388	    onTransport,
389	    onDone,
390	    headers = {},
391	    fetchImpl = typeof fetch !== 'undefined' ? fetch : undefined,
392	    pollIntervalMs = 1500,
393	    reconnectDelayMs = 1000,
394	    maxSseErrors = 2,
395	    setTimeoutImpl = (fn, ms) => setTimeout(fn, ms),
396	    clearTimeoutImpl = (h) => clearTimeout(h as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>),
397	  } = opts
398	
399	  const ESImpl =
400	    opts.eventSourceImpl !== undefined
401	      ? opts.eventSourceImpl
402	      : typeof EventSource !== 'undefined'
403	        ? EventSource
404	        : null
405	
406	  let lastEventId = opts.lastEventId ?? -1
407	  let closed = false
408	  let es: EventSource | null = null
409	  let timer: unknown = null
410	  let transport: TransportState = { mode: 'sse', errors: 0 }
411	
412	  const setTransport = (patch: Partial<TransportState>) => {
413	    transport = { ...transport, ...patch }
414	    onTransport?.(transport)
415	  }
416	
417	  const clearTimer = () => {
418	    if (timer !== null) {
419	      clearTimeoutImpl(timer)
420	      timer = null
421	    }
422	  }
423	
424	  const emit = (events: Event[]) => {
425	    const fresh = events.filter((e) => e.id > lastEventId)
426	    if (fresh.length === 0) return
427	    lastEventId = Math.max(lastEventId, ...fresh.map((e) => e.id))
428	    onEvents(fresh)
429	  }
430	
431	  const finish = (reason: string) => {
432	    if (closed) return
433	    log.info('stream.done', reason, { run_id: runId, last_event_id: lastEventId })
434	    closed = true
435	    es?.close()
436	    es = null
437	    clearTimer()
438	    setTransport({ mode: 'closed', detail: reason })
439	    onDone?.()
440	  }
441	
442	  // ------------------------------------------------ polling fallback
443	
444	  const pollOnce = async () => {
445	    if (closed || !fetchImpl) return
446	    try {
447	      const rec = await jsonFetch<RunRecord>(
448	        `${base}/runs/${encodeURIComponent(runId)}`,
449	        {},
450	        fetchImpl,
451	        headers,
452	      )
453	      onRecord?.(rec)
454	      emit(rec.events ?? [])
455	      if (isTerminal(rec.status)) {
456	        finish('run reached a terminal status (poll)')
457	        return
458	      }
459	    } catch (err) {
460	      log.warn('poll.error', 'GET /runs failed', { run_id: runId, error: String(err) })
461	      setTransport({ detail: String(err) })
462	    }
463	    if (!closed) timer = setTimeoutImpl(() => void pollOnce(), pollIntervalMs)
464	  }
465	
466	  const startPolling = (why: string) => {
467	    if (closed) return
468	    es?.close()
469	    es = null
470	    clearTimer()
471	    log.warn('stream.fallback', `switching to polling: ${why}`, {
472	      run_id: runId,
473	      interval_ms: pollIntervalMs,
474	    })
475	    setTransport({ mode: 'poll', detail: why })
476	    void pollOnce()
477	  }
478	
479	  // ------------------------------------------------ SSE
480	
481	  const openSse = () => {
482	    if (closed || !ESImpl) return
483	    const url = new URL(`${base}/runs/${encodeURIComponent(runId)}/events`)
484	    if (lastEventId >= 0) url.searchParams.set('last_event_id', String(lastEventId))
485	    let deliveredThisStream = 0
486	
487	    log.info('stream.open', 'opening SSE', { run_id: runId, url: url.toString() })
488	    const source = new ESImpl(url.toString())
489	    es = source
490	
491	    /** Drop this stream and open a fresh one at the resume point; the error counter stays 0. */
492	    const rotate = (why: string) => {
493	      source.close()
494	      if (es === source) es = null
495	      log.info('stream.rotate', why, {
496	        run_id: runId,
497	        delivered: deliveredThisStream,
498	        last_event_id: lastEventId,
499	      })
500	      setTransport({ errors: 0 })
501	      timer = setTimeoutImpl(openSse, 0)
502	    }
503	
504	    const handle = (e: MessageEvent) => {
505	      const ev = parseEvent(e.data as string)
506	      if (!ev) {
507	        log.warn('stream.parse_error', 'unparseable SSE frame', { run_id: runId })
508	        return
509	      }
510	      deliveredThisStream += 1
511	      if (transport.errors !== 0) setTransport({ errors: 0, detail: undefined })
512	      emit([ev])
513	      if (ev.type === 'run.finished') {
514	        // Let the server send `done`; if it does not, the close handler below stops us.
515	        log.info('stream.run_finished', 'run.finished seen', { run_id: runId })
516	      }
517	    }
518	
519	    source.addEventListener('message', handle as EventListener)
520	    for (const t of EVENT_TYPES) source.addEventListener(t, handle as EventListener)
521	    source.addEventListener('done', ((e: MessageEvent) => {
522	      if (closed) return
523	      const payload = parseDone(e?.data)
524	      if (doneMeansReconnect(payload)) {
525	        rotate('server closed its ~110 s window (`done` reason=window); reconnecting')
526	        return
527	      }
528	      finish(
529	        payload?.reason
530	          ? `server sent \`event: done\` (reason=${payload.reason}, status=${String(payload.status ?? '?')})`
531	          : 'server sent `event: done`',
532	      )
533	    }) as EventListener)
534	
535	    source.addEventListener('open', () => {
536	      log.info('stream.connected', 'SSE open', { run_id: runId })
537	      setTransport({ mode: 'sse' })
538	    })
539	
540	    source.addEventListener('error', () => {
541	      if (closed) return
542	      if (es !== source) return // already rotated away from this stream
543	      if (deliveredThisStream > 0) {
544	        // Expected on an older harness: the stream closes at ~110 s with no `done` frame.
545	        rotate('stream closed after delivering events; reconnecting')
546	        return
547	      }
548	      source.close()
549	      es = null
550	      const errors = transport.errors + 1
551	      setTransport({ errors, detail: 'EventSource error' })
552	      log.warn('stream.error', 'SSE failed before delivering anything', {
553	        run_id: runId,
554	        errors,
555	      })
556	      if (errors >= maxSseErrors) {
557	        startPolling(`${errors} consecutive EventSource errors`)
558	        return
559	      }
560	      timer = setTimeoutImpl(openSse, reconnectDelayMs)
561	    })
562	  }
563	
564	  if (ESImpl) {
565	    openSse()
566	  } else {
567	    startPolling('EventSource unavailable in this environment')
568	  }
569	
570	  return {
571	    close: () => {
572	      if (closed) return
573	      closed = true
574	      es?.close()
575	      es = null
576	      clearTimer()
577	      setTransport({ mode: 'closed', detail: 'closed by caller' })
578	      log.info('stream.close', 'subscription closed by caller', { run_id: runId })
579	    },
580	    state: () => transport,
581	  }
582	}

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:39:45.557Z
1	/**
2	 * Harness URL resolution.
3	 *
4	 * Precedence (first hit wins):
5	 *   1. window.__FAULTLINE_CONFIG__.harnessUrl   — injected inline by the host page
6	 *   2. GET /config.json  -> {"harnessUrl": "..."} — written at container start from $HARNESS_URL,
7	 *      so redeploying the harness never forces a frontend rebuild
8	 *   3. import.meta.env.VITE_HARNESS_URL         — baked at build time
9	 *   4. DEFAULT_HARNESS_URL                      — empty: nothing is baked into the bundle; an empty URL means "not configured"
10	 */
11	
12	import { getLogger } from './log'
13	
14	const log = getLogger('web')
15	
16	export const DEFAULT_HARNESS_URL = ''
17	
18	export interface FaultlineConfig {
19	  harnessUrl?: string
20	}
21	
22	declare global {
23	  interface Window {
24	    __FAULTLINE_CONFIG__?: FaultlineConfig
25	  }
26	}
27	
28	export interface ResolveOptions {
29	  /** Injectable for tests. */
30	  fetchImpl?: typeof fetch
31	  win?: { __FAULTLINE_CONFIG__?: FaultlineConfig } | undefined
32	  buildEnvUrl?: string | undefined
33	  configPath?: string
34	  defaultUrl?: string
35	}
36	
37	/** Trailing slashes break `${base}/runs` concatenation; strip them once, here. */
38	export function normaliseUrl(raw: string): string {
39	  return raw.trim().replace(/\/+$/, '')
40	}
41	
42	function isUsable(v: unknown): v is string {
43	  return typeof v === 'string' && v.trim().length > 0 && /^https?:\/\//i.test(v.trim())
44	}
45	
46	export interface ResolvedConfig {
47	  harnessUrl: string
48	  source: 'window' | 'config.json' | 'build-env' | 'default'
49	}
50	
51	export async function resolveConfig(opts: ResolveOptions = {}): Promise<ResolvedConfig> {
52	  const {
53	    fetchImpl = typeof fetch !== 'undefined' ? fetch : undefined,
54	    win = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : undefined,
55	    buildEnvUrl = typeof import.meta !== 'undefined'
56	      ? (import.meta.env?.VITE_HARNESS_URL as string | undefined)
57	      : undefined,
58	    configPath = '/config.json',
59	    defaultUrl = DEFAULT_HARNESS_URL,
60	  } = opts
61	
62	  const injected = win?.__FAULTLINE_CONFIG__?.harnessUrl
63	  if (isUsable(injected)) {
64	    return { harnessUrl: normaliseUrl(injected), source: 'window' }
65	  }
66	
67	  if (fetchImpl) {
68	    try {
69	      const res = await fetchImpl(configPath, { cache: 'no-store' })
70	      if (res.ok) {
71	        const body = (await res.json()) as FaultlineConfig
72	        if (isUsable(body?.harnessUrl)) {
73	          return { harnessUrl: normaliseUrl(body.harnessUrl), source: 'config.json' }
74	        }
75	      }
76	    } catch (err) {
77	      // Expected during `pnpm dev` (no /config.json on disk) — fall through quietly.
78	      log.debug('config.fetch_failed', 'no /config.json; falling back', {
79	        error: String(err),
80	      })
81	    }
82	  }
83	
84	  if (isUsable(buildEnvUrl)) {
85	    return { harnessUrl: normaliseUrl(buildEnvUrl), source: 'build-env' }
86	  }
87	
88	  return { harnessUrl: normaliseUrl(defaultUrl), source: 'default' }
89	}

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:40:07.265Z
1	/**
2	 * Resolve the harness URL once, build the client for the current identity, then keep the two
3	 * things Home needs from it: `/health` and `/scenarios`.
4	 *
5	 * Both are allowed to fail. A dead harness must still leave a usable page — the bundled replay
6	 * is the whole reason demo mode exists — so failures land in `healthError` / `scenariosError`
7	 * rather than throwing.
8	 */
9	
10	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
11	
12	import { useIdentity } from '@/hooks/useIdentity'
13	import { HarnessClient } from '@/lib/api'
14	import { resolveConfig, type ResolvedConfig } from '@/lib/config'
15	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
16	import type { Health, Scenario } from '@/lib/types'
17	
18	const log = getLogger('web')
19	
20	const NO_URL_ERROR = 'harness URL not configured (config.json or VITE_HARNESS_URL); replays still work'
21	
22	/**
23	 * Three states, not two: a cold harness answers `/health` in ~12 s, and during that window the
24	 * UI must say "checking", not "unreachable".
25	 */
26	export type HealthPhase = 'resolving' | 'checking' | 'reachable' | 'unreachable'
27	
28	export function healthPhase(hasClient: boolean, health: Health | null, healthError: string | null): HealthPhase {
29	  if (health?.ok === true) return 'reachable'
30	  if (healthError !== null) return 'unreachable'
31	  if (health !== null) return 'unreachable' // answered, but not ok
32	  return hasClient ? 'checking' : 'resolving'
33	}
34	
35	export interface HarnessState {
36	  /** Resolved harness origin, or null while resolving. */
37	  base: string | null
38	  /** `/health` answered and reported ok — the only gate on starting a live run. */
39	  reachable: boolean
40	  /** resolving → checking → reachable | unreachable. */
41	  phase: HealthPhase
42	  /** Where the URL came from: window | config.json | build-env | default. */
43	  source: ResolvedConfig['source'] | null
44	  health: Health | null
45	  healthError: string | null
46	  scenarios: Scenario[]
47	  scenariosError: string | null
48	  loading: boolean
49	  refresh: () => void
50	  /** Bound to the current identity: every request carries `X-Faultline-User`. Null until the URL resolves. */
51	  client: HarnessClient | null
52	  /** The anonymous browser id the client sends. */
53	  userId: string
54	}
55	
56	interface Probe<T> {
57	  client: HarnessClient
58	  epoch: number
59	  value: T | null
60	  error: string | null
61	}
62	
63	export function useHarness(): HarnessState {
64	  const { userId } = useIdentity()
65	  const [cfg, setCfg] = useState<ResolvedConfig | null>(null)
66	  /** Last answers, keyed by the (client, refresh epoch) that produced them; stale keys read as "not yet". */
67	  const [healthProbe, setHealthProbe] = useState<Probe<Health> | null>(null)
68	  const [scenariosProbe, setScenariosProbe] = useState<Probe<Scenario[]> | null>(null)
69	  const [nonce, setNonce] = useState(0)
70	
71	  // 1. Resolve the base URL once.
72	  useEffect(() => {
73	    let cancelled = false
74	    void resolveConfig().then((resolved) => {
75	      if (cancelled) return
76	      setCfg(resolved)
77	      log.info('config.resolved', 'harness URL resolved', {
78	        harness_url: resolved.harnessUrl,
79	        source: resolved.source,
80	      })
81	      if (!resolved.harnessUrl) log.warn('config.missing', 'no harness URL configured; live runs disabled')
82	    })
83	    return () => {
84	      cancelled = true
85	    }
86	  }, [])
87	
88	  // 2. One client per (base, identity). A reset() of the id rebuilds it.
89	  const client = useMemo(
90	    () => (cfg && cfg.harnessUrl ? new HarnessClient(cfg.harnessUrl, { userId }) : null),
91	    [cfg, userId],
92	  )
93	
94	  // 3. Probe /health and /scenarios whenever the client changes or refresh() is called.
95	  useEffect(() => {
96	    if (!client) return
97	    let cancelled = false
98	
99	    // Deliberately NOT `await Promise.all*` before updating: a cold harness answers /health in
100	    // ~12 s while /scenarios comes back in 0.3 s (or the other way round). Coupling them would
101	    // leave the whole page on a spinner for the slower of the two, so each settles on its own.
102	    client
103	      .health()
104	      .then((value) => {
105	        if (cancelled) return
106	        setHealthProbe({ client, epoch: nonce, value, error: null })
107	        log.info('health.ok', 'harness reachable', {
108	          model_default: value.model_default ?? null,
109	          has_provider_key: value.has_provider_key,
110	        })
111	      })
112	      .catch((err: unknown) => {
113	        if (cancelled) return
114	        setHealthProbe({ client, epoch: nonce, value: null, error: String(err) })
115	        log.warn('health.error', 'harness unreachable', { error: String(err) })
116	      })
117	
118	    client
119	      .scenarios()
120	      .then((value) => {
121	        if (cancelled) return
122	        setScenariosProbe({ client, epoch: nonce, value, error: null })
123	        log.info('scenarios.loaded', 'catalogue loaded', { count: value.length })
124	      })
125	      .catch((err: unknown) => {
126	        if (cancelled) return
127	        setScenariosProbe({ client, epoch: nonce, value: null, error: String(err) })
128	        log.warn('scenarios.error', 'GET /scenarios failed', { error: String(err) })
129	      })
130	
131	    return () => {
132	      cancelled = true
133	    }
134	  }, [client, nonce])
135	
136	  const refresh = useCallback(() => setNonce((n) => n + 1), [])
137	
138	  // 2b. No URL at all (no /config.json, no VITE_HARNESS_URL): say so instead of spinning forever.
139	  const noUrl = !!cfg && !cfg.harnessUrl
140	  const healthCurrent = !!client && !!healthProbe && healthProbe.client === client && healthProbe.epoch === nonce
141	  const scenariosCurrent =
142	    !!client && !!scenariosProbe && scenariosProbe.client === client && scenariosProbe.epoch === nonce
143	
144	  const health = healthCurrent ? healthProbe.value : null
145	  const healthError = noUrl ? NO_URL_ERROR : healthCurrent ? healthProbe.error : null
146	  const scenarios = scenariosCurrent ? (scenariosProbe.value ?? []) : []
147	  const scenariosError = scenariosCurrent ? scenariosProbe.error : null
148	
149	  // Loading until the URL resolves, and again whenever the client or the refresh epoch moves on.
150	  const loading = !cfg || (client !== null && !(healthCurrent && scenariosCurrent))
151	
152	  return {
153	    base: cfg?.harnessUrl ?? null,
154	    reachable: health?.ok === true,
155	    phase: healthPhase(client !== null, health, healthError),
156	    source: cfg?.source ?? null,
157	    health,
158	    healthError,
159	    scenarios,
160	    scenariosError,
161	    loading,
162	    refresh,
163	    client,
164	    userId,
165	  }
166	}

113	            {error ? (
114	              <div className="mt-3 flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/10 px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-destructive">
115	                <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
116	                <span className="break-all">Could not start the run: {error}</span>
117	              </div>
118	            ) : null}
119	          </div>
120	
121	          <div className="mt-10 w-full">
122	            <ScenarioTable
123	              scenarios={harness.scenarios}
124	              loading={harness.loading && harness.scenarios.length === 0}
125	              error={harness.scenarios.length === 0 && !harness.loading ? harness.scenariosError : null}
126	              selectedId={scenarioId}
127	              starting={busy ? scenarioId : null}
128	              canRun={canRun}
129	              onSelect={pick}
130	              onRun={runScenario}
131	              onReplay={(demoId) => navigate({ kind: 'replay', demoId })}
132	            />
133	          </div>
134	        </div>
135	      </div>
136	    </div>
137	  )
138	}

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:40:08.633Z
1	/**
2	 * Plain-English tour of a run, one checkpoint per step, built ONLY from structured event fields
3	 * (tool names and inputs, is_error, fault / error_class / outcome, exit codes, the grader's
4	 * checks). Message text is never parsed; the agent's own prose is only quoted. Calls are
5	 * classified by the reducer's own `classifyCall` / `callTouches`, so "mutating" and "touches this
6	 * path" mean the same thing here as on the transcript.
7	 */
8	
9	import { checkText } from './checks'
10	import { codeText, taxonomyLabel } from './codes'
11	import { firstSentence } from './format'
12	import { callTouches, classifyCall, normalisePath, parseToolResult } from './reducer'
13	import type {
14	  ErrorClass,
15	  EvaluateResponse,
16	  Event,
17	  FaultFired,
18	  Scenario,
19	  ToolCallData,
20	  ToolResultData,
21	} from './types'
22	
23	export type StoryTone = 'info' | 'ok' | 'warn' | 'fail'
24	
25	export interface Checkpoint {
26	  /** How many events are folded into the view at this checkpoint. */
27	  index: number
28	  /** Harness step, or null for the prologue / epilogue. */
29	  step: number | null
30	  /** Short label for the scrubber ("Start", "Step 4", "Verdict"). */
31	  label: string
32	  title: string
33	  text: string
34	  tone: StoryTone
35	}
36	
37	export interface Story {
38	  checkpoints: Checkpoint[]
39	}
40	
41	interface CallFacts {
42	  toolUseId: string
43	  tool: string
44	  path: string | null
45	  command: string | null
46	  mutating: boolean
47	}
48	
49	function describeCall(c: CallFacts): string {
50	  switch (c.tool) {
51	    case 'write_file':
52	      return `wrote ${c.path ?? 'a file'}`
53	    case 'read_file':
54	      return `read ${c.path ?? 'a file'}`
55	    case 'list_dir':
56	      return `listed ${c.path && c.path !== '.' ? c.path : 'the workspace'}`
57	    case 'submit':
58	      return 'submitted its summary'
59	    case 'run_command':
60	      return c.command ? `ran \`${c.command.length > 72 ? `${c.command.slice(0, 69)}…` : c.command}\`` : 'ran a command'
61	    default:
62	      return `called ${c.tool}`
63	  }
64	}
65	
66	function callFacts(d: ToolCallData): CallFacts {
67	  const cls = classifyCall(String(d.tool), d.input ?? {})
68	  return { toolUseId: d.tool_use_id, tool: String(d.tool), path: cls.path, command: cls.command, mutating: cls.mutating }
69	}
70	
71	/** The exit code the tool reported, via the reducer's own result parser (structured JSON only). */
72	function exitCodeOf(r: ToolResultData): number | null {
73	  return parseToolResult(r.tool_use_id, r.output ?? '', r.is_error === true, r.duration_ms ?? 0).exitCode
74	}
75	
76	function isTestCommand(c: CallFacts): boolean {
77	  return !!c.command && /\bpytest\b/.test(c.command)
78	}
79	
80	function faultSentence(fault: FaultFired | null | undefined, ec: ErrorClass | null | undefined): { text: string; tone: StoryTone; unknown: boolean } | null {
81	  if (ec) {
82	    const label = ec.label
83	    const code = codeText(ec.code)
84	    if (ec.origin === 'real') {
85	      return {
86	        text: `This was a real failure, not a simulated one: ${label}.${ec.outcome_known === false ? ' Whether the operation ran is unknown.' : ''}`,
87	        tone: 'fail',
88	        unknown: ec.outcome_known === false,
89	      }
90	    }
91	    return {
92	      text: `The environment intercepted it: ${label}.${ec.outcome_known === false ? ' The agent cannot know whether the write landed.' : ''}${code ? ` (${code.plain})` : ''}`,
93	      tone: 'warn',
94	      unknown: ec.outcome_known === false,
95	    }
96	  }
97	  if (fault) {
98	    const label = taxonomyLabel(fault.origin ?? 'injected', fault.kind, fault.layer ?? null) ?? `simulated: ${fault.kind}`
99	    const unknown = fault.kind === 'ack_lost'
100	    return {
101	      text: `The environment intercepted it: ${label}.${unknown ? ' The agent cannot know whether the write landed.' : ''}`,
102	      tone: 'warn',
103	      unknown,
104	    }
105	  }
106	  return null
107	}
108	
109	function evaluationSentences(ev: EvaluateResponse): string[] {
110	  const out: string[] = []
111	  const t = ev.tests
112	  out.push(`The grader uploaded hidden tests into the sandbox and ran them: ${t.passed} passed, ${t.failed} failed${t.errors ? `, ${t.errors} errors` : ''}.`)
113	  const ok = ev.checks.filter((c) => c.ok)
114	  const bad = ev.checks.filter((c) => !c.ok)
115	  if (ev.checks.length) {
116	    out.push(`Recovery checks from the ledger: ${ok.length}/${ev.checks.length} passed${bad.length ? ` — failed: ${bad.map((c) => checkText(c.id).whenFailed).join('; ')}` : ''}.`)
117	  }
118	  out.push(`Score ${Math.round(ev.score)}/100.`)
119	  return out
120	}
121	
122	export function buildStory(events: Event[], scenario: Scenario | null): Story {
123	  const checkpoints: Checkpoint[] = []
124	  if (events.length === 0) return { checkpoints }
125	
126	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- prologue
127	  let taskPrompt: string | null = null
128	  let model: string | null = null
129	  let firstStepIdx = events.findIndex((e) => typeof e.step === 'number' && e.step > 0 && e.type !== 'log')
130	  if (firstStepIdx < 0) firstStepIdx = events.length
131	  for (const e of events.slice(0, firstStepIdx)) {
132	    if (e.type === 'episode.reset' && typeof e.data.task_prompt === 'string') taskPrompt = e.data.task_prompt
133	    if (e.type === 'run.started' && typeof e.data.model === 'string') model = e.data.model
134	  }
135	  const injected = (scenario?.fault_kinds ?? []).map((k) => taxonomyLabel('injected', k, 'boundary') ?? `simulated: ${k}`)
136	  const real = scenario?.harness_faults ?? []
137	  const pro: string[] = []
138	  pro.push(taskPrompt ? `The task: ${firstSentence(taskPrompt, 220)}` : 'The agent is given a task in a sandboxed copy of a small Python repo.')
139	  if (model) pro.push(`The model is ${model}.`)
140	  if (injected.length) pro.push(`Somewhere along the way the environment will inject ${injected.length === 1 ? 'one failure' : `${injected.length} failures`}: ${injected.join('; ')}.`)
141	  if (real.length) pro.push(`It also includes a real harness interruption (${real.map((f) => f.kind.replace('_', ' ')).join(', ')}) — the worker really is killed.`)
142	  pro.push('The agent is not told any of this.')
143	  checkpoints.push({
144	    index: Math.max(firstStepIdx, 0),
145	    step: null,
146	    label: 'Start',
147	    title: scenario?.title ?? 'The task',
148	    text: pro.join(' '),
149	    tone: 'info',
150	  })
151	
152	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- steps
153	  const stepOrder: number[] = []
154	  const byStep = new Map<number, Event[]>()
155	  for (const e of events) {
156	    if (typeof e.step !== 'number' || e.step <= 0 || e.type === 'log') continue
157	    if (!byStep.has(e.step)) {
158	      byStep.set(e.step, [])
159	      stepOrder.push(e.step)
160	    }
161	    byStep.get(e.step)!.push(e)
162	  }
163	  const lastIndexOfStep = new Map<number, number>()
164	  events.forEach((e, i) => {
165	    if (typeof e.step === 'number' && e.step > 0) lastIndexOfStep.set(e.step, i + 1)
166	  })
167	
168	  const calls = new Map<string, CallFacts>()
169	  /** paths whose last write ended with an unknown outcome and have not been read back since */
170	  const unverified = new Map<string, number>()
171	  const lastEvaluated = events.filter((e) => e.type === 'episode.evaluated').at(-1)
172	  const evaluation = lastEvaluated ? (lastEvaluated.data as unknown as EvaluateResponse) : null
173	
174	  for (const step of stepOrder) {
175	    const evs = byStep.get(step)!
176	    const sentences: string[] = []
177	    let tone: StoryTone = 'info'
178	    const prose = evs.find((e) => e.type === 'turn.text' && typeof e.data.text === 'string')
179	    if (prose) {
180	      const quote = firstSentence(String(prose.data.text), 160)
181	      if (quote) sentences.push(`The agent: “${quote}”`)
182	    }
183	    const stepCalls = evs.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool.call').map((e) => callFacts(e.data as unknown as ToolCallData))
184	    for (const c of stepCalls) calls.set(c.toolUseId, c)
185	    const results = evs.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool.result').map((e) => e.data as unknown as ToolResultData)
186	    const faultsFired = evs.filter((e) => e.type === 'fault.fired').map((e) => e.data as unknown as FaultFired)
187	
188	    const actions: string[] = []
189	    for (const c of stepCalls) {
190	      const r = results.find((x) => x.tool_use_id === c.toolUseId)
191	      let s = `It ${describeCall(c)}`
192	      if (r) {
193	        const ec = r.error_class ?? null
194	        const fault =
195	          r.fault ?? faultsFired.find((f) => f.step === step && (!c.path || normalisePath(f.path) === c.path)) ?? null
196	        const exit = exitCodeOf(r)
197	        if (r.is_error) {
198	          const fs = faultSentence(fault, ec)
199	          if (fs) {
200	            s += `. ${fs.text}`
201	            tone = fs.tone === 'fail' ? 'fail' : tone === 'fail' ? 'fail' : 'warn'
202	            // The path whose write is now in doubt: the call's own, else the one the fault names.
203	            const doubted = c.path ?? (fault?.path ? normalisePath(fault.path) : null)
204	            if (fs.unknown && doubted) unverified.set(doubted, step)
205	          } else {
206	            s += '. It failed — the record carries no classification, so what happened is unknown.'
207	            tone = tone === 'fail' ? 'fail' : 'warn'
208	          }
209	        } else if (exit !== null && exit !== 0) {
210	          // A test command's exit status is a fact; whether "the tests failed" is the grader's call.
211	          s += ` — ${isTestCommand(c) ? 'the test command' : 'it'} exited ${exit}.`
212	          tone = tone === 'info' ? 'warn' : tone
213	        } else if (exit === 0 && isTestCommand(c)) {
214	          s += ' — the test command exited 0.'
215	          tone = tone === 'info' ? 'ok' : tone
216	        } else {
217	          s += '.'
218	        }
219	        // recovery observations (derived from call order, not from text)
220	        if (!r.is_error) {
221	          const doubted = [...unverified.keys()].find((p) => callTouches(c, p))
222	          if (doubted) {
223	            if (!c.mutating) {
224	              s += ` That read-back is the right move after a lost acknowledgement: now it knows the file's real contents.`
225	              unverified.delete(doubted)
226	              tone = tone === 'fail' ? 'fail' : 'ok'
227	            } else {
228	              s += ` It wrote ${doubted} again without reading it back — if the first write landed, this duplicates it.`
229	              tone = 'warn'
230	            }
231	          }
232	        }
233	      } else {
234	        s += ' (no result recorded).'
235	      }
236	      actions.push(s)
237	    }
238	    sentences.push(...actions)
239	    if (stepCalls.length === 0 && !prose) sentences.push('Nothing observable happened in this step.')
240	
241	    checkpoints.push({
242	      index: lastIndexOfStep.get(step) ?? events.length,
243	      step,
244	      label: `Step ${step}`,
245	      title: `Step ${step}`,
246	      text: sentences.join(' '),
247	      tone,
248	    })
249	  }
250	
251	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- epilogue
252	  const finished = events.filter((e) => e.type === 'run.finished').at(-1)
253	  const status = finished ? String(finished.data.status ?? '') : ''
254	  const epi: string[] = []
255	  let epiTone: StoryTone = 'info'
256	  if (evaluation) {
257	    epi.push(...evaluationSentences(evaluation))
258	    epiTone = evaluation.passed && evaluation.checks.every((c) => c.ok) ? 'ok' : 'warn'
259	  }
260	  if (status && status !== 'ok') {
261	    const ec = (finished?.data.error_class as ErrorClass | undefined) ?? null
262	    epi.push(`The run ended ${status}${ec ? `: ${ec.label}` : typeof finished?.data.error === 'string' ? `: ${firstSentence(String(finished.data.error), 140)}` : ''}.`)
263	    epiTone = status === 'truncated' ? 'warn' : 'fail'
264	  }
265	  if (!evaluation && !status) epi.push('The run has not been graded.')
266	  checkpoints.push({
267	    index: events.length,
268	    step: null,
269	    label: 'Verdict',
270	    title: evaluation ? `Verdict: ${Math.round(evaluation.score)}/100` : 'Verdict',
271	    text: epi.join(' '),
272	    tone: epiTone,
273	  })
274	
275	  return { checkpoints }
276	}
277	
278	/** The checkpoint the cursor is currently inside (the last one whose index <= cursor). */
279	export function checkpointAt(story: Story, cursor: number): number {
280	  let idx = 0
281	  story.checkpoints.forEach((c, i) => {
282	    if (c.index <= cursor) idx = i
283	  })
284	  return idx
285	}

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:40:32.426Z
1	/**
2	 * Source-independent transcript view model.
3	 *
4	 * The UI renders one shape whether the run is streaming (ViewState from the reducer), being
5	 * replayed from a bundled RunRecord (also ViewState), or read back from the harness Store as
6	 * Anthropic-shaped messages (ConversationDetail.messages). The persisted path re-uses the
7	 * reducer's own classification, result parsing and recovery derivation, so a fault badge or a
8	 * "read-back seen" hint means the same thing on every path. That hint is a heuristic from call
9	 * order; the grader's checks (`evaluation`) are authoritative and the UI prefers them once they
10	 * exist. The messages projection carries no evaluation or run error of its own — the caller
11	 * overlays them from the run record (`transcriptFromMessages(messages, run, overlay)`).
12	 */
13	
14	import {
15	  applyRecovery,
16	  classifyCall,
17	  isTerminal,
18	  parseToolResult,
19	  type StepView,
20	  type ToolCallView,
21	  type ViewState,
22	} from './reducer'
23	import type { Block, EvaluateResponse, Message, RunStatus, RunSummary } from './types'
24	
25	export interface Turn {
26	  step: number
27	  thinking: string | null
28	  texts: string[]
29	  calls: ToolCallView[]
30	}
31	
32	export interface Transcript {
33	  runId: string | null
34	  status: RunStatus
35	  scenarioId: string | null
36	  model: string | null
37	  taskPrompt: string | null
38	  turns: Turn[]
39	  evaluation: EvaluateResponse | null
40	  score: number | null
41	  /** The run's error text (`run.finished.error` / `RunRecord.error`); RunSummary carries none. */
42	  error: string | null
43	  /** True while more turns may still arrive. */
44	  live: boolean
45	}
46	
47	/** Record-level fields the messages projection does not carry (from `GET /runs/{id}`). */
48	export interface TranscriptOverlay {
49	  evaluation?: EvaluateResponse | null
50	  error?: string | null
51	  status?: RunStatus | null
52	}
53	
54	export function transcriptFromViewState(state: ViewState, live: boolean): Transcript {
55	  return {
56	    runId: state.runId,
57	    status: state.status,
58	    scenarioId: state.scenarioId,
59	    model: state.model,
60	    taskPrompt: state.taskPrompt,
61	    turns: state.steps.map((s) => ({ step: s.step, thinking: s.thinking, texts: s.texts, calls: s.calls })),
62	    evaluation: state.evaluation,
63	    score: state.evaluation?.score ?? null,
64	    error: state.error,
65	    live,
66	  }
67	}
68	
69	function sortedBlocks(m: Message): Block[] {
70	  return [...(m.blocks ?? [])].sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq)
71	}
72	
73	function upsertTurn(turns: StepView[], step: number): StepView {
74	  const found = turns.find((t) => t.step === step)
75	  if (found) return found
76	  const fresh: StepView = { step, thinking: null, texts: [], calls: [] }
77	  turns.push(fresh)
78	  turns.sort((a, b) => a.step - b.step)
79	  return fresh
80	}
81	
82	function highestStep(turns: StepView[]): number {
83	  return turns.reduce((m, t) => Math.max(m, t.step), 0)
84	}
85	
86	function nextSeq(turns: StepView[]): number {
87	  let max = -1
88	  for (const t of turns) for (const c of t.calls) if (c.seq > max) max = c.seq
89	  return max + 1
90	}
91	
92	function makeCall(turns: StepView[], step: number, toolUseId: string, tool: string, input: Record<string, unknown>): ToolCallView {
93	  const cls = classifyCall(tool, input)
94	  return {
95	    seq: nextSeq(turns),
96	    step,
97	    toolUseId,
98	    tool,
99	    input,
100	    path: cls.path,
101	    command: cls.command,
102	    mutating: cls.mutating,
103	    read: cls.read,
104	    result: null,
105	    fault: null,
106	    recovered: false,
107	  }
108	}
109	
110	function findCall(turns: StepView[], toolUseId: string): { turn: StepView; idx: number } | null {
111	  for (const turn of turns) {
112	    const idx = turn.calls.findIndex((c) => c.toolUseId === toolUseId)
113	    if (idx >= 0) return { turn, idx }
114	  }
115	  return null
116	}
117	
118	function attachResult(turns: StepView[], msg: Message, b: Block): void {
119	  const toolUseId = b.tool_use_id
120	  if (!toolUseId) return
121	  const result = parseToolResult(toolUseId, b.text ?? '', b.is_error === true, b.duration_ms ?? 0)
122	  if (result.exitCode === null && typeof b.exit_code === 'number') result.exitCode = b.exit_code
123	  if (b.truncated) result.truncated = true
124	  const fault = b.fault ?? null
125	
126	  const hit = findCall(turns, toolUseId)
127	  if (hit) {
128	    const prev = hit.turn.calls[hit.idx]!
129	    hit.turn.calls[hit.idx] = { ...prev, result, fault: fault ?? prev.fault }
130	    return
131	  }
132	  const step = typeof msg.step === 'number' ? msg.step : highestStep(turns)
133	  const turn = upsertTurn(turns, step)
134	  const synth = makeCall(turns, step, toolUseId, b.tool_name ?? 'unknown', {})
135	  turn.calls.push({ ...synth, result, fault })
136	}
137	
138	/**
139	 * Build the transcript of one run from a conversation's persisted messages.
140	 *
141	 * Grouping follows ARCHITECTURE.md §4.5: the first user message's text block is the task prompt;
142	 * each assistant message is one turn (thinking / text / tool_use blocks in seq order); the user
143	 * message that follows carries the tool_result blocks, matched back by tool_use_id.
144	 *
145	 * `overlay` supplies what the projection lacks: the grader's evaluation, the run error and the
146	 * record's status. Without it the transcript has no evaluation — which the UI must render as
147	 * "not graded", never as a pass.
148	 */
149	export function transcriptFromMessages(
150	  messages: Message[],
151	  run: RunSummary,
152	  overlay: TranscriptOverlay = {},
153	): Transcript {
154	  const mine = messages.filter((m) => m.run_id === run.id).sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq)
155	
156	  let taskPrompt: string | null = null
157	  const turns: StepView[] = []
158	
159	  for (const m of mine) {
160	    const blocks = sortedBlocks(m)
161	
162	    if (m.role === 'user') {
163	      for (const b of blocks) {
164	        if (b.type === 'tool_result') {
165	          attachResult(turns, m, b)
166	        } else if (b.type === 'text' && taskPrompt === null && typeof b.text === 'string' && b.text.length > 0) {
167	          taskPrompt = b.text
168	        }
169	      }
170	      continue
171	    }
172	
173	    const step = typeof m.step === 'number' ? m.step : highestStep(turns)
174	    const turn = upsertTurn(turns, step)
175	    for (const b of blocks) {
176	      switch (b.type) {
177	        case 'thinking': {
178	          const text = b.text ?? ''
179	          if (text.trim().length === 0) break
180	          turn.thinking = turn.thinking ? `${turn.thinking}\n\n${text}` : text
181	          break
182	        }
183	        case 'text': {
184	          const text = b.text ?? ''
185	          if (text.trim().length === 0) break
186	          turn.texts.push(text)
187	          break
188	        }
189	        case 'tool_use': {
190	          const toolUseId = b.tool_use_id ?? b.id
191	          if (turn.calls.some((c) => c.toolUseId === toolUseId)) break
192	          const input = b.input && typeof b.input === 'object' ? b.input : {}
193	          turn.calls.push(makeCall(turns, step, toolUseId, b.tool_name ?? 'unknown', input))
194	          break
195	        }
196	        default:
197	          break
198	      }
199	    }
200	  }
201	
202	  const recovered = applyRecovery(turns)
203	  const evaluation = overlay.evaluation ?? null
204	  const status = overlay.status ?? run.status
205	
206	  return {
207	    runId: run.id,
208	    status,
209	    scenarioId: run.scenario_id,
210	    model: run.model,
211	    taskPrompt,
212	    turns: recovered.map((t) => ({ step: t.step, thinking: t.thinking, texts: t.texts, calls: t.calls })),
213	    evaluation,
214	    score: evaluation?.score ?? run.score ?? null,
215	    error: overlay.error ?? null,
216	    live: !isTerminal(status),
217	  }
218	}

1	/**
2	 * Plain language for the error taxonomy (docs/error-taxonomy.md, packages/common schemas.py).
3	 *
4	 * Static mappings only: code → what it means, (origin, kind | layer) → the fixed label the
5	 * taxonomy prescribes, origin → the badge word. None of them look at message text, and none of
6	 * them can tell injected from real — only `origin` (a structured field) can.
7	 */
8	
9	import type { ErrorCode, ErrorLayer, ErrorOrigin, FaultKind } from './types'
10	import { ERROR_CODES } from './types'
11	
12	export interface CodeText {
13	  /** A few words, e.g. "no such file". */
14	  title: string
15	  /** One or two sentences a reviewer can act on. */
16	  plain: string
17	  /** What to do before retrying, when the outcome may be unknown. */
18	  verifyHint?: string
19	}
20	
21	export const CODE_TEXT: Record<ErrorCode, CodeText> = {
22	  ENOENT: {
23	    title: 'no such file or directory',
24	    plain:
25	      'The path could not be found. Either the file is really absent (deleted at reset, or never created) or the environment withheld it for a few calls while it stayed on disk.',
26	  },
27	  EACCES: {
28	    title: 'permission denied',
29	    plain:
30	      'The write was refused and nothing was written. A later write to the same path can still succeed once the refusal lifts.',
31	  },
32	  ETIMEDOUT: {
33	    title: 'response never arrived',
34	    plain:
35	      'The response never arrived. The operation may or may not have completed — read the file back before retrying.',
36	    verifyHint: 'Read the file back before writing it again; a blind retry can apply the change twice.',
37	  },
38	  EINVAL: {
39	    title: 'invalid argument',
40	    plain:
41	      'The call was rejected before anything ran: a path outside /workspace, a bad mode, or a directory where a file was expected.',
42	  },
43	  ESANDBOX: {
44	    title: 'sandbox unavailable',
45	    plain:
46	      'The sandbox that holds the workspace is gone or unreachable (terminated, not found, or its exec failed). Nothing ran, and later calls will fail the same way until a new sandbox exists.',
47	  },
48	  ETRANSPORT: {
49	    title: 'transport failure',
50	    plain:
51	      'The HTTP/MCP hop between the harness and sandbox-env failed (connection reset, 5xx, or the client aborted). The server may still have completed the call.',
52	    verifyHint: 'Verify the workspace before retrying; the request may have reached the server.',
53	  },
54	  EHARNESS: {
55	    title: 'worker interrupted',
56	    plain:
57	      'The harness worker itself was interrupted while this call was in flight. The request had already been dispatched, so the side effect may have landed; the ledger has the truth after the fact.',
58	    verifyHint: 'Read the file back before writing it again; the interrupted write may have landed.',
59	  },
60	  EMODEL: {
61	    title: 'model API error',
62	    plain: 'The model API call that drives the turn failed. No tool ran; the harness retries or ends the run.',
63	  },
64	  EGYM: {
65	    title: 'gym control-plane failure',
66	    plain:
67	      'A gym control-plane operation (reset, observe, evaluate or delete) failed. This is the environment, not the agent: a run that finished its work can still be left ungraded.',
68	  },
69	  EINTERNAL: {
70	    title: 'internal error in sandbox-env',
71	    plain: 'An unexpected exception inside sandbox-env (a bug; see detail). The call did not reach the sandbox.',
72	  },
73	  ENOEPISODE: {
74	    title: 'unknown episode',
75	    plain: 'The request carried a missing or unknown episode id, so sandbox-env refused it before anything ran.',
76	  },
77	}
78	
79	/** The dictionary entry for a code, or null for an unknown/absent code. */
80	export function codeText(code: string | null | undefined): CodeText | null {
81	  if (!code) return null
82	  return (ERROR_CODES as readonly string[]).includes(code) ? CODE_TEXT[code as ErrorCode] : null
83	}
84	
85	/** Badge word for an origin: injected → "simulated", staged → "staged", real → "real". */
86	export function originText(origin: ErrorOrigin | 'unknown' | null | undefined): string {
87	  switch (origin) {
88	    case 'injected':
89	      return 'simulated'
90	    case 'staged':
91	      return 'staged'
92	    case 'real':
93	      return 'real'
94	    default:
95	      return 'unknown'
96	  }
97	}
98	
99	/** Fixed labels the taxonomy prescribes for injected/staged faults, by kind. */
100	const INJECTED_LABEL: Record<FaultKind, string> = {
101	  missing_file: 'simulated: missing file (file still on disk)',
102	  denied_write: 'simulated: write denied (nothing written)',
103	  ack_lost: 'simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)',
104	}
105	
106	const STAGED_LABEL: Partial<Record<FaultKind, string>> = {
107	  missing_file: 'staged: file absent since reset',
108	}
109	
110	/** Fixed labels the taxonomy prescribes for real failures, by layer. */
111	const REAL_LABEL: Record<ErrorLayer, string> = {
112	  filesystem: 'real: OS error in sandbox',
113	  sandbox: 'real: sandbox terminated or unavailable',
114	  transport: 'real: transport failure harness<->sandbox-env (outcome unknown)',
115	  harness: 'real: harness worker interrupted mid-call (outcome unknown)',
116	  model: 'real: model API error',
117	  gym: 'real: gym control-plane failure',
118	  boundary: 'real: internal error in sandbox-env',
119	}
120	
121	/**
122	 * The taxonomy's own (origin, kind) / (origin, layer) → label table, for records that carry the
123	 * provenance fields but no `label` (e.g. a `fault.fired` without an `error_class`). Returns null
124	 * when the table has no row — never guesses.
125	 */
126	export function taxonomyLabel(
127	  origin: ErrorOrigin | 'unknown' | null | undefined,
128	  kind: FaultKind | string | null | undefined,
129	  layer: ErrorLayer | null | undefined,
130	): string | null {
131	  if (origin === 'injected') return (kind && INJECTED_LABEL[kind as FaultKind]) ?? null
132	  if (origin === 'staged') return (kind && STAGED_LABEL[kind as FaultKind]) ?? null
133	  if (origin === 'real') return (layer && REAL_LABEL[layer]) ?? null
134	  return null
135	}

2	 * User product flows for apps/web — one test per sub-feature in
3	 * .claude/skills/verify-faultline/features/web-ui.md. Keep the ids (F1…F11) and the feature ids in
4	 * the test titles in sync with that file: the verification runner reports `flows.<id>` from them.
5	 *
6	 * Every test starts from a fresh browser context (fresh identity, fresh theme, fresh layout) and

9	import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs'
10	import { join } from 'node:path'

12	import { expect, test, type Page, type TestInfo } from '@playwright/test'

14	const SCREEN_DIR = process.env.PW_SCREEN_DIR ?? 'e2e-results/screens'
15	const OUTPUTS_DIR = process.env.PW_OUTPUTS_DIR ?? 'e2e-results/outputs'
16	
17	function writeOutput(name: string, value: unknown) {
18	  mkdirSync(OUTPUTS_DIR, { recursive: true })
19	  writeFileSync(join(OUTPUTS_DIR, name), JSON.stringify(value, null, 2))
20	}
21	const LIVE = process.env.FAULTLINE_LIVE === '1'

23	async function shot(page: Page, info: TestInfo, name: string) {
24	  const path = `${SCREEN_DIR}/${name}.png`
25	  await page.screenshot({ path, fullPage: true })
26	  await info.attach('screen', { path, contentType: 'image/png' })
27	}

29	async function gotoHome(page: Page) {
30	  await page.goto('/')
31	  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toContainText('What should the agent survive today?')
32	  // skeleton rows render first; wait for a real scenario row (it has a Run button)
33	  await expect(page.locator('table tbody tr').filter({ has: page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Run$|Starting/ }) }).first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 60_000 })
34	}

36	const promptBox = (page: Page) => page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Prompt' })
37	
38	/** Open the bottom-left user menu; the trigger also owns a tooltip, so give the popup a moment and retry once. */
39	async function openUserMenu(page: Page) {
40	  const trigger = page.locator('[data-slot=sidebar-footer] button').first()
41	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {
42	    await trigger.click()
43	    try {
44	      await expect(page.getByRole('menu')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 4_000 })
45	      return
46	    } catch {
47	      await page.keyboard.press('Escape')
48	      await page.waitForTimeout(300)
49	    }
50	  }
51	  await expect(page.getByRole('menu')).toBeVisible()
52	}
53	const rowFor = (page: Page, scenarioId: string) =>
54	  page.locator('table tbody tr').filter({ has: page.locator(`text=/^${scenarioId}$/`) })

231	    async function adoptIdentity(page: Page) {

236	          /* ignore */

238	      }, liveUserId!)

244	      await rowFor(page, 'lost-ack').getByRole('button', { name: /^Run$/ }).click()
245	      await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/conversations\/c_[a-z0-9]+\?run=r_[a-z0-9]+/, { timeout: 60_000 })
246	      conversationId = new URL(page.url()).pathname.split('/').pop()!
247	      runId = new URL(page.url()).searchParams.get('run')!
248	      liveUrl = page.url()
249	      writeOutput('live.json', { run_id: runId, conversation_id: conversationId, started_at: new Date().toISOString(), url: liveUrl })
250	      await expect(page.locator('[data-slot=sidebar]')).toContainText(/Release 0\.2\.0/)
251	      await expect(page.locator('header')).toContainText(/live · (sse|poll)/, { timeout: 60_000 })
252	      await shot(page, info, 'F3_live_streaming')
253	      // the status badge's own text is exactly the run status (header textContent concatenates nodes without spaces)
254	      const statusBadge = page.locator('header').getByText(/^(ok|unevaluated|interrupted|truncated|error)$/)
255	      await expect(statusBadge).toBeVisible({ timeout: 4 * 60_000 })
256	      const finalStatus = (await statusBadge.textContent())?.trim()
257	      await expect(page.locator('header').getByText(/sqlite/)).toBeVisible({ timeout: 60_000 })
258	      await shot(page, info, 'F3_finished_sqlite')
259	      const userId = await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem('faultline.user_id'))
260	      liveUserId = userId
261	      writeOutput('live.json', { run_id: runId, conversation_id: conversationId, user_id: userId, final_status: finalStatus, url: liveUrl, finished_at: new Date().toISOString() })
262	    })

274	      const cfg = (await (await request.get('/config.json')).json()) as { harnessUrl: string }
275	      const mine = await request.get(`${cfg.harnessUrl}/conversations/${conversationId}`, { headers: { 'X-Faultline-User': userId! } })
276	      expect(mine.ok()).toBe(true)

278	      writeOutput('conversation.json', body)
279	      writeOutput('live.json', { run_id: runId, conversation_id: conversationId, user_id: userId, harness_url: cfg.harnessUrl, url: liveUrl, verified_at: new Date().toISOString() })
280	      await info.attach('conversation.json', { body: JSON.stringify(body, null, 2), contentType: 'application/json' })
281	      expect(Array.isArray(body.messages) && body.messages.length).toBeTruthy()
282	      const other = await request.get(`${cfg.harnessUrl}/conversations/${conversationId}`, { headers: { 'X-Faultline-User': 'u_22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222' } })
283	      expect(other.status()).toBe(404)

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:41:05.890Z
1	# Web UI (apps/web)
2	
3	The browser is the product: a reviewer opens the deployed site, picks a scenario, watches a Claude
4	agent survive (or not) a failing sandbox as a chat-style transcript, reads the ten-second verdict
5	strip, and comes back later to the same conversation. Everything below is a *user* flow, driven
6	through the real page in a real Chrome (Playwright, `channel: chrome`) against the deployed Modal
7	Server — never through the API alone. This file is the maintained list of flows: when a flow
8	changes in `apps/web`, change its entry here and the matching test in `apps/web/e2e/flows.spec.ts`
9	in the same commit, and re-run `verify_web.py` before claiming the UI works.
10	
11	## Sub-features
12	
13	- `web-landing` `/` shows the five "what this is" bullets, a centered composer (`/` picks a scenario, model + seed chips), and a six-column scenario data table (Scenario · What happens · Max turns · Failure injected · Live run · Replay); no harness-health card.
14	- `web-composer` typing `/` in the prompt opens the scenario menu, filters as you type, Enter picks (task prompt fills in), Enter again submits; the hint line explains state (unreachable harness, chosen scenario).
15	- `web-live-run` *Run* creates a conversation (`POST /conversations` → `POST /conversations/{id}/runs`) and lands on `/conversations/:id?run=…`; the rail lists the conversation; the transcript streams over SSE (status chip `live · sse`, step/tokens/elapsed counters, workspace files table updating); on finish the status chip reads `ok` and the graded score card (checks + hidden tests) appears at the end of the transcript.
16	- `web-persisted` after the run ends, the page re-renders the transcript from `GET /conversations/{id}` (top-bar chip `sqlite`); a hard reload restores it; the run switcher lists runs; the composer offers "another run in this conversation".
17	- `web-replay` every injected-fault scenario has a *Replay* (real captures in `public/demo/`); `/replay/:id` plays it with no backend: a `recorded replay` chip, a transport (play/pause/restart + a slider over the story's checkpoints, "step n / N"), and the transcript filling in; at the end the story bar reads `VERDICT: 100/100 · recovered` and the score card lists the grader's checks.
18	- `web-replay-story` a plain-English "what happened" bar (`role=region`, aria-label `What happened`) narrates the current checkpoint with prev/next; checkpoints are built only from structured event fields (`src/lib/story.ts`), the fault checkpoint is toned `fault` and names the file, a real failure is toned `real failure`. *(The five-panel verdict strip that preceded this was removed by another session on 2026-09-12 ~19:26 EDT; `src/lib/reliability.ts` still exists but is not rendered — see PLAN.md §3.3.2.)*
19	- `web-workspace` the workspace column is resizable (drag handle, collapsible to zero, split remembered), with Files / Diffs / Logs tabs (the raw Timeline tab was removed in v20); Logs rows are virtualized and follow the tail while live.
20	- `web-identity` a fresh browser mints `u_<uuid4>` (localStorage + cookie mirror), every harness request carries `X-Faultline-User`, the rail shows only that identity's conversations ("No conversations yet" when fresh), another identity gets 404 for the same conversation; the user menu can copy or reset the id.
21	- `web-theme` the user menu (bottom-left) has light · dark · system icon buttons; the choice persists, "system" follows the OS, `?theme=` in the URL sets it, and there is no flash on load.
22	- `web-failure-modes` an unknown run id shows "Run not found" / "Could not load this run" (no SSE tail, no provisioning spinner); an unreachable harness keeps replays usable and says so in the composer hint ("checking the harness…" while `/health` is cold); a run that could not be graded shows "Not graded" with the taxonomy's reason; tool status pills carry `data-status` ok | failed | unknown | not-executed and an unknown outcome reads "no ack", never red.
23	- `web-sidebar` the sidebar collapses to icons (trigger button or ⌘/Ctrl+B); conversations are grouped by day with status dots.
24	
25	## How to get to it (user POV)
26	
27	- Deployed: `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.us-east.modal.direct` (Modal app `faultline-web`, Server `site`; `/config.json` names the harness it talks to).
28	- Locally: `cd apps/web && source ~/.nvm/nvm.sh && nvm use && pnpm dev` (Node 22, pnpm 9.15.4), then `http://localhost:5173`; `public/config.json` points at the deployed harness.
29	- Routes: `/`, `/conversations/:id[?run=<run_id>]`, `/runs/:id`, `/replay/:demoId`; `?theme=light|dark|system` once.
30	
31	## Driving it with verify_web.py
32	
33	Preconditions:
34	
35	- The site answers `/` with 200 and `/config.json` names a harness whose `/health` is `ok: true`.
36	- Google Chrome is installed (Playwright uses it via `channel: "chrome"`; no browser download) and `apps/web/node_modules` is installed with pnpm 9.15.4 under Node 22.
37	- For `--live`: Modal secret `anthropic-secret` present; the harness Store is up (`/health.detail.store.ok`).
38	
39	- **Doctor (HTTP, no browser).** `python3 .claude/skills/verify-faultline/helpers/verify_web.py --skip-flows` records `http.*`: root served `no-store`, `config.json` harness URL matches a healthy harness, SPA fallback answers 200 for `/conversations/x`, a missing hashed asset is 404 **and** `no-store`, every bundled replay in `DEMOS` downloads with contiguous event ids and an evaluation.
40	- **Flows.** `python3 .claude/skills/verify-faultline/helpers/verify_web.py` runs `apps/web/e2e/flows.spec.ts` (one test per sub-feature above, ids `F1…F11`; `F5`/`F6` cover `web-replay` / `web-replay-story`) against `--base-url` (default: the deployed site) with a fresh browser context per test, and records one `flows.<id>` check per test plus a full-page screenshot per flow under `runs/<ts>_verify_web/screens/`.
41	- **Live conversation.** `--live` enables `F3` (*Run* on lost-ack from the table → `/conversations/:id?run=…`, rail entry, `live · sse`, terminal status badge, `sqlite` chip once persisted; `outputs/live.json` records `run_id` / `conversation_id` / `user_id` / `final_status`), `F4` (hard reload restores the transcript from `GET /conversations/{id}`: task bubble, steps, composer hint; `outputs/conversation.json` saved; another identity gets 404) and `F4b` (the persisted transcript shows the run's grade, or a "Not graded" callout when it could not be evaluated — fixed in v20; a regression would fail here).
42	- **Evidence.** `verification.json` (pass/fail per check, overall), `playwright.json` (raw reporter output), `screens/*.png`, `artifacts/` (traces/screenshots of failures), `outputs/*.json` (HTTP probes), `manifest.json` (sha256 of everything, written last). Exit 0 only when every check passes.
43	
44	## Gotchas
45	
46	- Playwright's `Desktop Chrome` device plus `channel: "chrome"` uses the machine's Google Chrome; if Chrome is missing, run `pnpm exec playwright install chromium` in `apps/web` and pass `PW_CHANNEL=chromium`.
47	- The Modal Server rolls containers after a deploy: the served bundle can lag `apps/web/dist` for ~30 s; the doctor compares the served `index-*.js` hash with the local build and reports `http.bundle_matches_local` as a warning-only check.
48	- `F3/F4` costs a real Haiku episode (~1 min, cents) and needs four concurrent runs *not* to be in flight (the Store's first `GET /runs/{id}` was seen to take 30–40 s under that load).
49	- Pre-store run ids may 404 on the harness (Dict-era records); `F10` deliberately uses a nonsense id, not a historical one.
50	- Headless Chrome follows the OS appearance for `prefers-color-scheme`; the theme test asserts on the `dark` class and storage, not on pixel colour.
51	- Base UI's `DropdownMenuLabel` is `aria-hidden`: interactive controls must never be placed inside it (the theme controls were, until faultline-web v20 moved them to sibling rows; `F9` asserts the radiogroup has no aria-hidden ancestor so this cannot regress silently).
52	- `runs/` is gitignored evidence; never delete old `runs/<ts>_verify_web/` directories.

1	import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react'
2	
3	import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
4	import type { Checkpoint, StoryTone } from '@/lib/story'
5	
6	const TONE: Record<StoryTone, { dot: string; ring: string; word: string }> = {
7	  info: { dot: 'bg-muted-foreground', ring: 'border-border', word: '' },
8	  ok: { dot: 'bg-emerald-400', ring: 'border-emerald-500/30', word: 'recovered' },
9	  warn: { dot: 'bg-amber-400', ring: 'border-amber-500/30', word: 'fault' },
10	  fail: { dot: 'bg-rose-400', ring: 'border-rose-500/30', word: 'real failure' },
11	}
12	
13	/** The tour guide: what happened at the current checkpoint, in plain English, with prev/next. */
14	export function StoryBar({
15	  checkpoint,
16	  position,
17	  total,
18	  onPrev,
19	  onNext,
20	}: {
21	  checkpoint: Checkpoint | null
22	  position: number
23	  total: number
24	  onPrev: () => void
25	  onNext: () => void
26	}) {
27	  if (!checkpoint) return null
28	  const tone = TONE[checkpoint.tone]
29	  return (
30	    <div
31	      className={`shrink-0 border-b bg-card/40 ${tone.ring}`}
32	      role="region"
33	      aria-label="What happened"
34	      aria-live="polite"
35	    >
36	      <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-4xl items-start gap-3 px-4 py-3 md:px-6">
37	        <Button
38	          size="sm"
39	          variant="ghost"
40	          className="mt-0.5 h-7 w-7 shrink-0 p-0"
41	          onClick={onPrev}
42	          disabled={position <= 0}
43	          aria-label="Previous step"
44	        >
45	          <ChevronLeft aria-hidden />
46	        </Button>
47	        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
48	          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">
49	            <span aria-hidden className={`inline-block size-1.5 rounded-full ${tone.dot}`} />
50	            <span>{checkpoint.title}</span>
51	            {tone.word ? <span className="font-mono normal-case">· {tone.word}</span> : null}
52	          </div>
53	          <p className="mt-1 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-foreground/90">{checkpoint.text}</p>
54	        </div>
55	        <Button
56	          size="sm"
57	          variant="ghost"
58	          className="mt-0.5 h-7 w-7 shrink-0 p-0"
59	          onClick={onNext}
60	          disabled={position >= total - 1}
61	          aria-label="Next step"
62	        >
63	          <ChevronRight aria-hidden />
64	        </Button>
65	      </div>
66	    </div>
67	  )

1	import { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react'
2	
3	import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
4	import { TopBar } from '@/components/layout/TopBar'
5	import { ReplayScrubber } from '@/components/replay/ReplayScrubber'
6	import { StoryBar } from '@/components/replay/StoryBar'
7	import { TranscriptView } from '@/components/transcript/TranscriptView'
8	import { RunLayout } from '@/components/workspace/RunLayout'
9	import { useWorkspaceOpen } from '@/hooks/useWorkspaceLayout'
10	import type { HarnessState } from '@/hooks/useHarness'
11	import { useReplay } from '@/hooks/useReplay'
12	import { DEMOS } from '@/lib/replay'
13	import { buildStory, checkpointAt } from '@/lib/story'
14	import { transcriptFromViewState } from '@/lib/transcript'
15	
16	/** Bundled recorded run: a scrubbable replay with a plain-English tour of each step. */
17	export function ReplayPage({ harness, demoId }: { harness: HarnessState; demoId: string }) {
18	  const rp = useReplay(demoId)
19	  const [workspaceOpen, setWorkspaceOpen] = useWorkspaceOpen()
20	  const meta = DEMOS.find((d) => d.id === demoId)
21	
22	  useEffect(() => {
23	    document.title = `replay · ${demoId} — Faultline`
24	  }, [demoId])
25	
26	  const scenario = useMemo(
27	    () => harness.scenarios.find((s) => s.id === (rp.record?.scenario_id ?? rp.state.scenarioId)) ?? null,
28	    [harness.scenarios, rp.record?.scenario_id, rp.state.scenarioId],
29	  )
30	  const story = useMemo(() => buildStory(rp.events, scenario), [rp.events, scenario])
31	  const at = checkpointAt(story, rp.cursor)
32	  const transcript = useMemo(() => transcriptFromViewState(rp.state, rp.live), [rp.state, rp.live])
33	  const current = story.checkpoints[at] ?? null
34	  const focusStep = rp.playing
35	    ? null
36	    : current === null
37	      ? null
38	      : current.step !== null
39	        ? current.step
40	        : at === 0
41	          ? 'start'
42	          : 'end'
43	
44	  const goTo = (i: number) => {
45	    const cp = story.checkpoints[Math.max(0, Math.min(i, story.checkpoints.length - 1))]
46	    if (cp) rp.setCursor(cp.index)
47	  }
48	
49	  return (
50	    <div className="flex h-dvh min-h-0 flex-col">
51	      <TopBar
52	        crumbs={[{ label: 'Replays', route: { kind: 'home' } }, { label: meta?.label ?? demoId }]}
53	        onToggleWorkspace={() => setWorkspaceOpen((v) => !v)}
54	        workspaceOpen={workspaceOpen}
55	      >
56	        <Badge variant="outline" className="font-mono text-muted-foreground" title="A recorded run playing back in the browser; no backend involved.">
57	          recorded replay
58	        </Badge>
59	        {story.checkpoints.length > 0 ? (
60	          <ReplayScrubber
61	            story={story}
62	            cursor={rp.cursor}
63	            onCursorChange={rp.setCursor}
64	            playing={rp.playing}
65	            onPlayingChange={rp.setPlaying}
66	            onRestart={rp.restart}
67	            done={rp.done}
68	          />
69	        ) : null}
70	      </TopBar>
71	      <StoryBar
72	        checkpoint={story.checkpoints[at] ?? null}
73	        position={at}
74	        total={story.checkpoints.length}
75	        onPrev={() => goTo(at - 1)}
76	        onNext={() => goTo(at + 1)}
77	      />
78	      <RunLayout state={rp.state} live={rp.live} open={workspaceOpen} onOpenChange={setWorkspaceOpen}>
79	        <TranscriptView
80	          transcript={transcript}
81	          diffs={rp.state.diffs}
82	          maxSteps={rp.state.maxSteps}
83	          loading={rp.loading}
84	          loadingLabel="Loading bundled run…"
85	          error={rp.error}
86	          focusStep={focusStep}
87	        />
88	      </RunLayout>
89	    </div>
90	  )
91	}
