10	
11	/** Where the transcript on screen is coming from. Shown so a reviewer can see the persistence path. */
12	export type TranscriptSource = 'sse' | 'poll' | 'sqlite' | 'record'
13	
14	const SOURCE_LABEL: Record<TranscriptSource, { label: string; tip: string; icon: typeof Radio }> = {
15	  sse: { label: 'live · sse', tip: 'Tailing GET /runs/{id}/events; the harness rotates the stream every ~110 s and we resume with Last-Event-ID.', icon: Radio },
16	  poll: { label: 'live · poll', tip: 'SSE failed twice in a row — polling GET /runs/{id} every 1.5 s instead.', icon: Radio },
17	  sqlite: { label: 'sqlite', tip: 'Transcript loaded from GET /conversations/{id}: the messages/blocks projection stored in SQLite on the persistent volume.', icon: Database },
18	  record: { label: 'record', tip: 'Loaded from GET /runs/{id} (the persisted run record).', icon: Database },
19	}
20	
21	function Stat({ label, value, title }: { label: string; value: string; title?: string }) {
22	  return (
23	    <div className="hidden flex-col leading-tight lg:flex" title={title}>
24	      <span className="text-[10px] tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">{label}</span>
25	      <span className="font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums">{value}</span>
26	    </div>
27	  )
28	}
29	
30	export function RunStatusStrip({
31	  state,
32	  transport,
33	  source,
34	}: {
35	  state: ViewState | null
36	  transport: TransportState | null
37	  source: TranscriptSource | null
38	}) {
39	  const live = !!state && (state.status === 'running' || state.status === 'queued')
40	  const [, setTick] = useState(0)
41	  useEffect(() => {
42	    if (!live) return
43	    const t = setInterval(() => setTick((n) => n + 1), 1000)
44	    return () => clearInterval(t)
45	  }, [live])
46	
47	  if (!state) return null
48	  const faults = faultCount(state)
49	  const recovered = recoveredCount(state)
50	  const grade = gradeOf(state.evaluation)
51	  const checks = state.evaluation?.checks ?? []
52	  const okChecks = checks.filter((c) => c.ok).length
53	  const src = source ? SOURCE_LABEL[source] : null
54	  const SrcIcon = src?.icon ?? Radio
55	
56	  return (
57	    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
58	      <Badge
59	        variant="outline"
60	        className={`font-mono ${statusTone(state.status, grade)}`}
61	        title={statusTitle(state.status, grade)}
62	        data-grade={state.status === 'ok' ? grade : undefined}
63	      >
64	        {state.status}
65	      </Badge>
66	      {src ? (
67	        <Tooltip>
68	          <TooltipTrigger
69	            render={
70	              <Badge variant="outline" className="font-mono text-muted-foreground">
71	                <SrcIcon aria-hidden /> {src.label}
72	              </Badge>
73	            }
74	          />
75	          <TooltipContent>{src.tip}{transport?.detail ? ` (${transport.detail})` : ''}</TooltipContent>
76	        </Tooltip>
77	      ) : null}
78	      <Stat label="step" value={`${state.step}/${state.maxSteps}`} />
79	      <Stat
80	        label="tokens"
81	        value={`${fmtTokens(state.usage.input_tokens)} in · ${fmtTokens(state.usage.output_tokens)} out`}
82	        title={`${state.usage.input_tokens} input / ${state.usage.output_tokens} output`}
83	      />
84	      <Stat label="elapsed" value={fmtDuration(elapsedMs(state))} />
85	      <Stat label="faults" value={`${faults}`} title="Faults the environment fired on this run's tool calls" />
86	      {state.evaluation ? (
87	        <Stat
88	          label="checks"
89	          value={`${okChecks}/${checks.length} passed`}
90	          title="The grader's checks (authoritative)"
91	        />
92	      ) : (
93	        <Stat
94	          label="read-back"
95	          value={`${recovered}/${faults} seen`}
96	          title="Live hint from the call order (a read of the faulted path before a later success); heuristic — the grader's checks are authoritative"
97	        />
98	      )}
99	      {state.evaluation ? <Stat label="score" value={`${Math.round(state.evaluation.score)}`} /> : null}
100	    </div>
101	  )
102	}

62	/** Three states: a cold harness takes ~12 s to answer /health, and that is "checking", not down. */
63	const HEALTH_DOT: Record<HealthPhase, string> = {
64	  resolving: 'bg-muted-foreground/60',
65	  checking: 'bg-muted-foreground/60 animate-pulse',
66	  reachable: 'bg-emerald-500 dark:bg-emerald-400',
67	  unreachable: 'bg-rose-500 dark:bg-rose-400',
68	}
69	
70	const HEALTH_WORD: Record<HealthPhase, string> = {
71	  resolving: 'resolving…',
72	  checking: 'checking…',
73	  reachable: 'reachable',
74	  unreachable: 'unreachable',
75	}

77	function dayBucket(iso: string, now = new Date()): string {
78	  const d = new Date(iso)
79	  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return 'Earlier'
80	  const startOfToday = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate()).getTime()
81	  const t = d.getTime()
82	  if (t >= startOfToday) return 'Today'
83	  if (t >= startOfToday - 86_400_000) return 'Yesterday'
84	  if (t >= startOfToday - 6 * 86_400_000) return 'Previous 7 days'
85	  return 'Earlier'
86	}
87	
88	function groupByDay(items: ConversationSummary[]): { label: string; items: ConversationSummary[] }[] {
89	  const order = ['Today', 'Yesterday', 'Previous 7 days', 'Earlier']
90	  const buckets = new Map<string, ConversationSummary[]>()
91	  for (const c of items) {
92	    const key = dayBucket(c.updated_at || c.created_at)
93	    const arr = buckets.get(key) ?? []
94	    arr.push(c)
95	    buckets.set(key, arr)
96	  }
97	  return order.filter((k) => buckets.has(k)).map((k) => ({ label: k, items: buckets.get(k)! }))
98	}
99	
100	function shortId(userId: string | null): string {
101	  if (!userId) return '—'
102	  return userId.length > 12 ? `${userId.slice(0, 10)}…` : userId
103	}
104	
105	function ConversationRow({ c, active }: { c: ConversationSummary; active: boolean }) {
106	  const Icon = SCENARIO_ICON[c.scenario_id] ?? MessageSquare
107	  const status = c.last_run?.status ?? null
108	  // The summary carries only a score: 100 means every test and check passed, anything less means
109	  // something failed, null means no grade — so "ok" is green only when it really was a pass.
110	  const grade = gradeOf(null, c.last_run?.score)
111	  const route: Route = { kind: 'conversation', id: c.id }
112	  return (
113	    <SidebarMenuItem>
114	      <SidebarMenuButton
115	        isActive={active}
116	        tooltip={c.title}
117	        render={<a {...linkProps(route)} />}
118	        className="group/row"
119	      >
120	        <Icon aria-hidden className="text-muted-foreground" />
121	        <span className="truncate">{c.title}</span>
122	      </SidebarMenuButton>
123	      {status ? (
124	        <SidebarMenuBadge className="gap-1.5 font-mono text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
125	          {c.last_run?.score !== null && c.last_run?.score !== undefined
126	            ? Math.round(c.last_run.score)
127	            : ''}
128	          <span
129	            aria-label={`last run ${statusTitle(status, grade)}`}
130	            title={statusTitle(status, grade)}
131	            className={`inline-block size-1.5 rounded-full ${statusDot(status, grade)}`}
132	          />
133	        </SidebarMenuBadge>
134	      ) : null}
135	    </SidebarMenuItem>
136	  )
137	}

139	export function AppSidebar({

152	  const activeConversation = route.kind === 'conversation' ? route.id : null

154	  const userId = identity.userId ?? null
155	
156	  const copyId = async () => {

164	    }

1	import { useCallback, useState } from 'react'
2	
3	import type { ConversationsState } from '@/hooks/useConversations'
4	import type { HarnessState } from '@/hooks/useHarness'
5	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
6	import { navigate } from '@/lib/router'
7	
8	const log = getLogger('web')
9	
10	export interface StartParams {
11	  scenarioId: string
12	  model: string
13	  seed: number | null
14	  /** Task prompt override; empty/null = the scenario default (server side). */
15	  prompt: string | null
16	  /** Start the run inside an existing conversation. */
17	  conversationId?: string | null
18	  title?: string
19	}
20	
21	/**
22	 * One place that knows how to start a run:
23	 *   1. inside an existing conversation → POST /conversations/{id}/runs
24	 *   2. otherwise                        → POST /conversations, then (1)
25	 * and then navigates to the conversation. Every path refreshes the conversation rail; any
26	 * failure (a 404 included) is surfaced as the error, never worked around.
27	 */
28	export function useStartRun(harness: HarnessState, conversations: ConversationsState) {
29	  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
30	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
31	
32	  const start = useCallback(
33	    async (p: StartParams): Promise<string | null> => {
34	      const client = harness.client
35	      if (!client) {
36	        setError('harness URL not resolved yet')
37	        return null
38	      }
39	      setBusy(true)
40	      setError(null)
41	      const req = {
42	        model: p.model,
43	        seed: p.seed,
44	        task_prompt: p.prompt && p.prompt.trim() ? p.prompt : undefined,
45	      }
46	      try {
47	        const conversationId =
48	          p.conversationId ?? (await client.createConversation({ scenario_id: p.scenarioId, title: p.title })).id
49	        const r = await client.createConversationRun(conversationId, req)
50	        log.info('run.started', p.conversationId ? 'run accepted (existing conversation)' : 'run accepted (new conversation)', {
51	          run_id: r.run_id,
52	          conversation_id: conversationId,
53	          scenario_id: p.scenarioId,
54	          model: p.model,
55	        })
56	        navigate({ kind: 'conversation', id: r.conversation_id ?? conversationId, runId: r.run_id })
57	        conversations.refresh()
58	        return r.run_id
59	      } catch (err) {
60	        setError(String(err))
61	        log.error('run.start_failed', 'could not start the run', {
62	          scenario_id: p.scenarioId,
63	          error: String(err),
64	        })
65	        return null
66	      } finally {
67	        setBusy(false)
68	      }
69	    },
70	    [harness.client, conversations],
71	  )
72	
73	  return { start, busy, error }
74	}

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:42:16.660Z
1	/**
2	 * The conversation rail: GET /conversations for the current identity.
3	 *
4	 * State is the last *loaded* answer, keyed by the request that produced it (client identity +
5	 * refresh epoch); `loading` is derived from whether that key is current. No setState runs
6	 * synchronously inside the effect, so a refresh or an identity reset costs one render, not two.
7	 */
8	
9	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
10	
11	import { HarnessClient } from '@/lib/api'
12	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
13	import type { ConversationSummary } from '@/lib/types'
14	
15	const log = getLogger('web')
16	
17	export interface ConversationsState {
18	  items: ConversationSummary[]
19	  loading: boolean
20	  /** Transport / server error text (a 404 on /conversations is an error like any other). */
21	  error: string | null
22	  refresh: () => void
23	}
24	
25	interface Loaded {
26	  client: HarnessClient
27	  epoch: number
28	  items: ConversationSummary[]
29	  error: string | null
30	}
31	
32	export function useConversations(client: HarnessClient | null): ConversationsState {
33	  const [epoch, setEpoch] = useState(0)
34	  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState<Loaded | null>(null)
35	
36	  useEffect(() => {
37	    if (!client) return
38	    let cancelled = false
39	
40	    client
41	      .listConversations()
42	      .then((list) => {
43	        if (cancelled) return
44	        setLoaded({ client, epoch, items: list, error: null })
45	        log.info('conversations.loaded', 'rail loaded', { count: list.length })
46	      })
47	      .catch((err: unknown) => {
48	        if (cancelled) return
49	        // Keep whatever was on screen; just surface the error.
50	        setLoaded((prev) => ({
51	          client,
52	          epoch,
53	          items: prev?.items ?? [],
54	          error: String(err),
55	        }))
56	        log.warn('conversations.error', 'GET /conversations failed', { error: String(err) })
57	      })
58	
59	    return () => {
60	      cancelled = true
61	    }
62	  }, [client, epoch])
63	
64	  const refresh = useCallback(() => setEpoch((n) => n + 1), [])
65	
66	  return useMemo(() => {
67	    const current = !!client && !!loaded && loaded.client === client && loaded.epoch === epoch
68	    return {
69	      items: loaded?.items ?? [],
70	      loading: !!client && !current,
71	      error: current ? loaded.error : null,
72	      refresh,
73	    }
74	  }, [client, loaded, epoch, refresh])
75	}

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:42:23.905Z
1	/**
2	 * Owns one run's view state and its transport: GET /runs/{id} to seed (so a page refresh mid-run
3	 * restores everything), then subscribeRun() for the tail.
4	 *
5	 * The seed is best-effort in one direction only: a run created a moment ago can 5xx or time out
6	 * until the harness has written it, and then the tail must still start. A 404 is different — the
7	 * harness answers 404 both for an unknown run and for one that belongs to another browser identity
8	 * (no existence oracle) — so it is reported as `notFound` and no tail is opened.
9	 *
10	 * State is keyed by the source, so switching runs resets by derivation rather than by setState
11	 * inside the effect.
12	 */
13	
14	import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
15	
16	import { HarnessClient, isNotFound, subscribeRun, type Subscription, type TransportState } from '@/lib/api'
17	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
18	import { fromRunRecord, initialState, reduce, type ViewState } from '@/lib/reducer'
19	import type { Event, RunRecord } from '@/lib/types'
20	
21	const log = getLogger('web')
22	
23	export type RunSource = { kind: 'none' } | { kind: 'live'; runId: string }
24	
25	export interface RunViewState {
26	  state: ViewState
27	  transport: TransportState | null
28	  /** Transport / server error from the seed (the tail still runs). Null for a 404 — see `notFound`. */
29	  loadError: string | null
30	  loading: boolean
31	  /** GET /runs/{id} answered 404: unknown run, or one owned by another browser identity. */
32	  notFound: boolean
33	  /** The seed record, when the harness returned one (conversation_id, task_prompt, …). */
34	  record: RunRecord | null
35	}
36	
37	interface Slot {
38	  key: string
39	  state: ViewState
40	  transport: TransportState | null
41	  loadError: string | null
42	  loading: boolean
43	  notFound: boolean
44	  record: RunRecord | null
45	}
46	
47	function emptySlot(key: string): Slot {
48	  return {
49	    key,
50	    state: initialState(),
51	    transport: null,
52	    loadError: null,
53	    loading: key !== 'none',
54	    notFound: false,
55	    record: null,
56	  }
57	}
58	
59	function sourceKey(source: RunSource): string {
60	  return source.kind === 'live' ? `live:${source.runId}` : 'none'
61	}
62	
63	export function useRunView(client: HarnessClient | null, source: RunSource): RunViewState {
64	  const key = sourceKey(source)
65	  const [slot, setSlot] = useState<Slot | null>(null)
66	  const subRef = useRef<Subscription | null>(null)
67	
68	  /** Update the slot for `key` only; a stale callback for a previous run is dropped. */
69	  const patch = useCallback((forKey: string, fn: (s: Slot) => Slot) => {
70	    setSlot((prev) => {
71	      const base = prev && prev.key === forKey ? prev : emptySlot(forKey)
72	      return fn(base)
73	    })
74	  }, [])
75	
76	  const push = useCallback(
77	    (forKey: string, events: Event[]) => {
78	      patch(forKey, (s) => ({ ...s, state: events.reduce(reduce, s.state) }))
79	    },
80	    [patch],
81	  )
82	
83	  useEffect(() => {
84	    let cancelled = false
85	    const stop = () => {
86	      cancelled = true
87	      subRef.current?.close()
88	      subRef.current = null
89	    }
90	
91	    if (key === 'none') return stop
92	
93	    const runId = key.slice('live:'.length)
94	    if (!client) return stop // reported as "harness URL not resolved yet" by derivation below
95	
96	    void (async () => {
97	      let from = -1
98	      try {
99	        const rec = await client.getRun(runId)
100	        if (cancelled) return
101	        patch(key, (s) => ({ ...s, state: fromRunRecord(rec), record: rec, loadError: null, notFound: false }))
102	        from = rec.events?.length ? Math.max(...rec.events.map((e) => e.id)) : -1
103	        log.info('run.restored', 'seeded view from GET /runs/{id}', {
104	          run_id: runId,
105	          events: rec.events?.length ?? 0,
106	          status: rec.status,
107	        })
108	      } catch (err) {
109	        if (cancelled) return
110	        if (isNotFound(err)) {
111	          patch(key, (s) => ({ ...s, loading: false, notFound: true, loadError: null }))
112	          log.warn('run.not_found', 'GET /runs/{id} is 404 for this identity; not tailing', { run_id: runId })
113	          return
114	        }
115	        patch(key, (s) => ({ ...s, loadError: String(err) }))
116	        log.warn('run.load_error', 'GET /runs failed; relying on the stream', {
117	          run_id: runId,
118	          error: String(err),
119	        })
120	      }
121	      if (cancelled) return
122	      patch(key, (s) => ({ ...s, loading: false }))
123	      subRef.current = subscribeRun({
124	        base: client.base,
125	        runId,
126	        lastEventId: from,
127	        headers: client.headers(),
128	        onEvents: (events) => {
129	          if (!cancelled) push(key, events)
130	        },
131	        onRecord: (r) => {
132	          if (cancelled) return
133	          // The polling fallback returns the whole record; take the summary fields from it so the
134	          // header stays right even if a `run.finished` event was missed.
135	          patch(key, (s) => ({
136	            ...s,
137	            record: r,
138	            state: {
139	              ...s.state,
140	              status: r.status ?? s.state.status,
141	              usage: r.usage ?? s.state.usage,
142	              evaluation: r.evaluation ?? s.state.evaluation,
143	              error: r.error ?? s.state.error,
144	              finishedAt: r.finished_at ?? s.state.finishedAt,
145	            },
146	          }))
147	        },
148	        onTransport: (t) => {
149	          if (!cancelled) patch(key, (s) => ({ ...s, transport: t }))
150	        },
151	      })
152	    })()
153	
154	    return stop
155	  }, [key, client, patch, push])
156	
157	  const current = slot && slot.key === key ? slot : emptySlot(key)
158	  const noClient = key !== 'none' && client === null
159	  return {
160	    state: current.state,
161	    transport: current.transport,
162	    loadError: noClient ? 'harness URL not resolved yet' : current.loadError,
163	    loading: noClient ? false : current.loading,
164	    notFound: current.notFound,
165	    record: current.record,
166	  }
167	}

1	/**
2	 * Bundled replays: `public/demo/<scenario>.json` holds a RunRecord captured from a real run
3	 * (scripts/export_demo.py; see public/demo/README.md). `hooks/useReplay.ts` folds it through the
4	 * same reducer a live run goes through, one event every DEMO_STEP_MS, so the UI is fully
5	 * exercisable with no backend at all.
6	 */
7	
8	import type { RunRecord } from './types'
9	
10	export const DEMO_STEP_MS = 400
11	
12	export interface DemoMeta {
13	  id: string
14	  label: string
15	  file: string
16	}
17	
18	/** Demos shipped in public/demo. Adding a file here is all it takes to expose a new replay. */
19	export const DEMOS: DemoMeta[] = [
20	  // Real captures: claude-haiku-4-5 on the deployed stack (2026-09-12), exported with
21	  // scripts/export_demo.py. Ids match scenario ids so the scenario table offers them via demoFor().
22	  { id: 'lost-ack', label: 'lost-ack', file: '/demo/lost-ack.json' },
23	  { id: 'locked-file', label: 'locked-file', file: '/demo/locked-file.json' },
24	  { id: 'missing-config', label: 'missing-config', file: '/demo/missing-config.json' },
25	  { id: 'gauntlet', label: 'gauntlet', file: '/demo/gauntlet.json' },
26	]
27	
28	export function demoFor(scenarioId: string): DemoMeta | undefined {
29	  return DEMOS.find((d) => d.id === scenarioId)
30	}
31	
32	export async function loadDemo(
33	  file: string,
34	  fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch,
35	): Promise<RunRecord> {
36	  const res = await fetchImpl(file, { cache: 'no-store' })
37	  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`demo ${file} not found (${res.status})`)
38	  const rec = (await res.json()) as RunRecord
39	  if (!Array.isArray(rec?.events)) throw new Error(`demo ${file} has no events`)
40	  return rec
41	}

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:42:25.275Z
1	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
2	
3	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
4	import { fromRunRecord, reduceAll, initialState, type ViewState } from '@/lib/reducer'
5	import { DEMO_STEP_MS, DEMOS, loadDemo } from '@/lib/replay'
6	import type { Event, RunRecord } from '@/lib/types'
7	
8	const log = getLogger('web')
9	
10	export interface ReplayState {
11	  record: RunRecord | null
12	  events: Event[]
13	  /** View folded from the first `cursor` events. */
14	  state: ViewState
15	  cursor: number
16	  setCursor: (n: number) => void
17	  playing: boolean
18	  setPlaying: (p: boolean) => void
19	  restart: () => void
20	  loading: boolean
21	  error: string | null
22	  /** true while auto-advancing and not at the end */
23	  live: boolean
24	  done: boolean
25	}
26	
27	interface Loaded {
28	  demoId: string
29	  record: RunRecord | null
30	  error: string | null
31	}
32	
33	interface Playhead {
34	  demoId: string
35	  cursor: number
36	  playing: boolean
37	}
38	
39	/**
40	 * Scrubbable playback of a bundled RunRecord: the view is a pure fold of the first `cursor`
41	 * events, so the slider can move in both directions and the transcript, workspace and story stay
42	 * consistent with each other.
43	 *
44	 * State is keyed by `demoId` (the loaded record and the playhead both remember which demo they
45	 * belong to), so switching demos resets by derivation rather than by setState inside an effect.
46	 */
47	export function useReplay(demoId: string): ReplayState {
48	  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState<Loaded | null>(null)
49	  const [playhead, setPlayhead] = useState<Playhead>({ demoId, cursor: 0, playing: true })
50	
51	  useEffect(() => {
52	    let cancelled = false
53	    const meta = DEMOS.find((d) => d.id === demoId)
54	    void (async () => {
55	      try {
56	        if (!meta) throw new Error(`no bundled demo named "${demoId}"`)
57	        const rec = await loadDemo(meta.file)
58	        if (cancelled) return
59	        setLoaded({ demoId, record: rec, error: null })
60	        log.info('replay.loaded', 'bundled run loaded', { demo: demoId, events: rec.events.length })
61	      } catch (err) {
62	        if (cancelled) return
63	        setLoaded({ demoId, record: null, error: String(err) })
64	        log.error('replay.error', 'failed to load bundled run', { demo: demoId, error: String(err) })
65	      }
66	    })()
67	    return () => {
68	      cancelled = true
69	    }
70	  }, [demoId])
71	
72	  const current = loaded && loaded.demoId === demoId ? loaded : null
73	  const record = current?.record ?? null
74	  const loading = current === null
75	  const error = current?.error ?? null
76	
77	  const head = playhead.demoId === demoId ? playhead : { demoId, cursor: 0, playing: true }
78	  const events = useMemo(() => record?.events ?? [], [record])
79	  const total = events.length
80	  const cursor = Math.min(head.cursor, total)
81	  // Playing past the end is meaningless, so "playing" is derived rather than switched off.
82	  const playing = head.playing && (record === null || cursor < total)
83	
84	  useEffect(() => {
85	    if (!playing || !record || cursor >= total) return
86	    const t = setTimeout(
87	      () =>
88	        setPlayhead((h) =>
89	          h.demoId === demoId ? { ...h, cursor: Math.min(h.cursor + 1, total) } : h,
90	        ),
91	      DEMO_STEP_MS,
92	    )
93	    return () => clearTimeout(t)
94	  }, [playing, cursor, total, record, demoId])
95	
96	  const base = useMemo(() => {
97	    if (!record) return initialState()
98	    const seeded = fromRunRecord({
99	      ...record,
100	      events: [],
101	      evaluation: null,
102	      finished_at: null,
103	      error: null,
104	      error_class: null,
105	      interruptions: [],
106	      worker_generation: 1,
107	    })
108	    return { ...seeded, status: 'running' as const, evaluation: null }
109	  }, [record])
110	
111	  const state = useMemo(() => reduceAll(events.slice(0, cursor), base), [events, cursor, base])
112	
113	  const setCursor = useCallback(
114	    (n: number) => setPlayhead({ demoId, cursor: Math.max(0, Math.min(n, total)), playing: false }),
115	    [demoId, total],
116	  )
117	  const setPlaying = useCallback(
118	    (p: boolean) =>
119	      setPlayhead((h) => ({ demoId, cursor: h.demoId === demoId ? h.cursor : 0, playing: p })),
120	    [demoId],
121	  )
122	  const restart = useCallback(() => setPlayhead({ demoId, cursor: 0, playing: true }), [demoId])
123	
124	  return {
125	    record,
126	    events,
127	    state,
128	    cursor,
129	    setCursor,
130	    playing,
131	    setPlaying,
132	    restart,
133	    loading,
134	    error,
135	    live: playing && cursor < total,
136	    done: total > 0 && cursor >= total,
137	  }
138	}

60	export function ConversationPage({
61	  harness,
62	  conversations,
63	  conversationId,
64	  runId,
65	}: {
66	  harness: HarnessState
67	  conversations: ConversationsState
68	  conversationId: string
69	  runId: string | null
70	}) {
71	  const { detail, loading, error, refresh } = useConversation(harness.client, conversationId)
72	  const [workspaceOpen, setWorkspaceOpen] = useWorkspaceOpen()
73	  // The user's pick wins; before one, the harness's default (once /health answers), else ours.
74	  const [modelChoice, setModel] = useState<string | null>(null)
75	  const model = modelChoice ?? harness.health?.model_default ?? DEFAULT_MODEL
76	  const [seed, setSeed] = useState<number | null>(null)
77	  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState('')
78	  const { start, busy, error: startError } = useStartRun(harness, conversations)
79	
80	  const runs = useMemo(
81	    () => [...(detail?.runs ?? [])].sort((a, b) => a.created_at.localeCompare(b.created_at)),
82	    [detail?.runs],
83	  )
84	  const selected: RunSummary | null = useMemo(() => {
85	    if (runs.length === 0) return null
86	    return (runId ? runs.find((r) => r.id === runId) : null) ?? runs[runs.length - 1] ?? null
87	  }, [runs, runId])
88	
89	  const source = useMemo(
90	    () => (selected ? ({ kind: 'live', runId: selected.id } as const) : ({ kind: 'none' } as const)),
91	    [selected],
92	  )
93	  const rv = useRunView(harness.client, source)
94	  const live = !!selected && (rv.state.status === 'running' || rv.state.status === 'queued')
95	
96	  // When the live run reaches a terminal state, reload the conversation from the store so the
97	  // transcript switches to the persisted (SQLite) projection and the rail updates its status dot.
98	  const refreshedFor = useRef<string | null>(null)
99	  useEffect(() => {
100	    if (!selected || !isTerminal(rv.state.status) || rv.state.runId !== selected.id) return
101	    if (refreshedFor.current === selected.id) return
102	    refreshedFor.current = selected.id
103	    if (!isTerminal(selected.status)) {
104	      log.info('conversation.refresh', 'run finished; reloading persisted transcript', {
105	        conversation_id: conversationId,
106	        run_id: selected.id,
107	        status: rv.state.status,
108	      })
109	      refresh()
110	      conversations.refresh()
111	    }
112	  }, [rv.state.status, rv.state.runId, selected, conversationId, refresh, conversations])
113	
114	  const scenario = useMemo(
115	    () => harness.scenarios.find((s) => s.id === detail?.conversation.scenario_id) ?? null,
116	    [harness.scenarios, detail?.conversation.scenario_id],
117	  )
118	  useEffect(() => {
119	    if (scenario && prompt === '') setPrompt(scenario.task_prompt)
120	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
121	  }, [scenario?.id])
122	
123	  useEffect(() => {
124	    document.title = detail ? `${detail.conversation.title} — Faultline` : 'Faultline'
125	  }, [detail])
126	
127	  const messagesForRun = useMemo(
128	    () => (detail && selected ? detail.messages.filter((m) => m.run_id === selected.id) : []),
129	    [detail, selected],
130	  )
131	  const persisted = !!detail && !!selected && isTerminal(selected.status) && messagesForRun.length > 0 && !live
132	  // The messages projection has no evaluation or run error; overlay them from the run record the
133	  // view was seeded with (only when that record is this run's — the view resets on switch).
134	  const seeded = !!selected && rv.state.runId === selected.id
135	  const transcript = useMemo(
136	    () =>
137	      persisted && detail && selected
138	        ? transcriptFromMessages(
139	            messagesForRun,
140	            selected,
141	            seeded ? { evaluation: rv.state.evaluation, error: rv.state.error, status: rv.state.status } : {},
142	          )
143	        : transcriptFromViewState(rv.state, live),
144	    [persisted, detail, selected, messagesForRun, rv.state, live, seeded],
145	  )
146	  const transcriptSource: TranscriptSource | null = persisted
147	    ? 'sqlite'
148	    : live
149	      ? rv.transport?.mode === 'poll'
150	        ? 'poll'
151	        : 'sse'
152	      : selected
153	        ? 'record'
154	        : null
155	
156	  const submit = () => {
157	    if (!detail || busy) return
158	    void start({
159	      scenarioId: detail.conversation.scenario_id,
160	      model,
161	      seed,
162	      prompt: scenario && prompt.trim() === scenario.task_prompt.trim() ? null : prompt,
163	      conversationId: detail.conversation.id,
164	    })
165	  }
166	
167	  const title = detail?.conversation.title ?? (loading ? 'Loading…' : 'Conversation')
168	
169	  return (
170	    <div className="flex h-dvh min-h-0 flex-col">
171	      <TopBar
172	        crumbs={[{ label: 'Conversations', route: { kind: 'home' } }, { label: title }]}
173	        onToggleWorkspace={() => setWorkspaceOpen((v) => !v)}
174	        workspaceOpen={workspaceOpen}
175	      >
176	        <RunStatusStrip
177	          state={selected ? rv.state : null}
178	          transport={rv.transport}
179	          source={transcriptSource}
180	        />
181	      </TopBar>
182	
183	      <RunLayout state={selected ? rv.state : null} live={live} open={workspaceOpen} onOpenChange={setWorkspaceOpen}>
184	          {loading && !detail ? (
185	            <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl space-y-4 px-4 py-8">
186	              <Skeleton className="ml-auto h-16 w-2/3" />
187	              <Skeleton className="h-20 w-full" />
188	              <Skeleton className="h-32 w-full" />
189	            </div>
190	          ) : error && !detail ? (
191	            <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4 py-8">
192	              <div
193	                className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200"
194	                role="status"
195	                data-slot={error === NOT_FOUND ? 'not-found' : 'load-error'}
196	              >
197	                <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
198	                <div>
199	                  <div className="font-medium">{error === NOT_FOUND ? 'Conversation not found' : 'Conversation not available'}</div>
200	                  <div className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] break-all opacity-80">
201	                    {error === NOT_FOUND
202	                      ? 'The harness has no conversation with this id for this browser identity — it may belong to another browser, or the id is wrong.'
203	                      : error}
204	                  </div>
205	                  <a className="mt-1 inline-block underline" {...linkProps({ kind: 'home' })}>
206	                    Start a new run
207	                  </a>
208	                </div>
209	              </div>
210	            </div>
211	          ) : (
212	            <TranscriptView
213	              transcript={transcript}
214	              diffs={rv.state.diffs}
215	              maxSteps={rv.state.maxSteps || scenario?.max_steps || 20}
216	              loading={!!selected && rv.loading}
217	              loadingLabel="Loading run…"
218	              error={rv.loadError}
219	              notFound={rv.notFound}
220	              transport={rv.transport}
221	              footer={
222	                <div className="space-y-3 pt-2">
223	                  {detail ? (
224	                    <RunSwitcher conversationId={detail.conversation.id} runs={runs} selectedId={selected?.id ?? null} />
225	                  ) : null}
226	                  {detail && runs.length === 0 ? (
227	                    <p className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">
228	                      No runs in this conversation yet — send the task below to start one.
229	                    </p>
230	                  ) : null}
231	                </div>
232	              }
233	            />
234	          )}
235	
236	          {detail ? (
237	            <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-border bg-background/90 px-4 py-3 backdrop-blur md:px-6">
238	              <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl space-y-2">
239	                <Composer
240	                  scenarios={scenario ? [scenario] : harness.scenarios}
241	                  scenarioId={detail.conversation.scenario_id}
242	                  onScenarioChange={() => undefined}
243	                  models={MODEL_ALLOWLIST}
244	                  model={model}
245	                  onModelChange={setModel}
246	                  seed={seed}
247	                  onSeedChange={setSeed}
248	                  prompt={prompt}
249	                  onPromptChange={setPrompt}
250	                  onSubmit={submit}
251	                  disabled={!harness.reachable || live || busy}
252	                  busy={busy}
253	                  placeholder={live ? 'A run is in progress…' : 'Edit the task and press enter to run it again in this conversation'}
254	                  hint={
255	                    live
256	                      ? 'wait for the current run to finish'
257	                      : `${detail.conversation.scenario_id} · another run in this conversation`
258	                  }
259	                />
260	                {startError ? (
261	                  <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/10 px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-destructive">
262	                    <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
263	                    <span className="break-all">Could not start the run: {startError}</span>
264	                  </div>
265	                ) : null}
266	              </div>
267	            </div>
268	          ) : null}
269	      </RunLayout>
270	    </div>

10	function UserBubble({ text }: { text: string }) {
11	  return (
12	    <div className="flex justify-end" aria-label="Task prompt">
13	      <div className="max-w-[85%] rounded-2xl rounded-br-md border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-3">
14	        <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] tracking-wide text-ink-3 uppercase">

23	function Callout({
24	  tone,
25	  title,
26	  body,
27	  slot,
28	}: {
29	  tone: 'error' | 'warn' | 'neutral'
30	  title: string
31	  body?: string | null
32	  slot?: string
33	}) {
34	  const cls =
35	    tone === 'error'
36	      ? 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-200'
37	      : tone === 'warn'
38	        ? 'border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200'
39	        : 'border-line bg-muted/40 text-ink-2'
40	  const Icon = tone === 'error' ? OctagonAlert : tone === 'warn' ? AlertTriangle : CircleDashed
41	  return (
42	    <div role="status" data-slot={slot} className={`flex items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] ${cls}`}>
43	      <Icon className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
44	      <div className="min-w-0">
45	        <div className="font-medium">{title}</div>
46	        {body ? <div className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] break-words opacity-80">{body}</div> : null}
47	      </div>
48	    </div>
49	  )
50	}

52	function TurnBlock({
53	  turn,
54	  isLast,
55	  live,
56	  maxSteps,
57	  diffs,
58	}: {
59	  turn: Turn
60	  isLast: boolean
61	  live: boolean
62	  maxSteps: number
63	  diffs: FileDiff[]
64	}) {
65	  const pending = turn.calls.some((c) => c.result === null)
66	  const active = live && isLast && pending
67	  const done = !live || !isLast || (!pending && turn.calls.length > 0)
68	  return (
69	    <section aria-label={`Step ${turn.step}`} className="scroll-mt-4 space-y-3">
70	      {turn.texts.map((t, i) => (
71	        <AssistantText
72	          key={`${turn.step}-t-${i}`}
73	          text={t}
74	          streaming={live && isLast && i === turn.texts.length - 1 && turn.calls.length === 0}
75	        />
76	      ))}
77	      {turn.calls.length > 0 || turn.thinking ? (
78	        <ThinkingTrace
79	          step={turn.step}
80	          maxSteps={maxSteps}
81	          calls={turn.calls}
82	          thinking={turn.thinking}
83	          active={active}
84	          done={done}
85	          defaultOpen={isLast}
86	        >
87	          <ToolCallChips calls={turn.calls} diffs={diffs} live={live && isLast} />
88	        </ThinkingTrace>
89	      ) : null}
90	    </section>
91	  )
92	}

94	export function TranscriptView({
95	  transcript,
96	  diffs,
97	  maxSteps,
98	  loading,
99	  loadingLabel,
100	  error,
101	  transport,
102	  notFound = false,
103	  footer,
104	  focusStep = null,
105	}: {
106	  transcript: Transcript
107	  diffs: FileDiff[]
108	  maxSteps: number
109	  loading?: boolean
110	  loadingLabel?: string
111	  error?: string | null
112	  transport?: TransportState | null
113	  /** GET /runs/{id} answered 404: distinct from loading and from other errors. */
114	  notFound?: boolean
115	  /** Rendered below the transcript inside the same scroll container (run switcher, composer spacer). */
116	  footer?: React.ReactNode
117	  /** Replay tour: scroll this step into view ('start' / 'end' for the prologue / verdict). */
118	  focusStep?: number | 'start' | 'end' | null
119	}) {
120	  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
121	  const stick = useRef(true)
122	  const live = transcript.live
123	
124	  useEffect(() => {
125	    const el = ref.current
126	    if (!el) return
127	    const onScroll = () => {
128	      stick.current = el.scrollHeight - el.scrollTop - el.clientHeight < 96
129	    }
130	    el.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true })
131	    return () => el.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll)
132	  }, [])
133	
134	  const turnCount = transcript.turns.length
135	  const callCount = transcript.turns.reduce((n, t) => n + t.calls.length, 0)
136	  useEffect(() => {
137	    const el = ref.current
138	    if (!el || !live || !stick.current) return
139	    el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight
140	  }, [turnCount, callCount, live, transcript.evaluation])
141	
142	  useEffect(() => {
143	    const el = ref.current
144	    if (!el || focusStep === null || focusStep === undefined) return
145	    if (focusStep === 'start') {
146	      el.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' })
147	      return
148	    }
149	    if (focusStep === 'end') {
150	      el.scrollTo({ top: el.scrollHeight, behavior: 'smooth' })
151	      return
152	    }
153	    const target = el.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`section[aria-label="Step ${focusStep}"]`)
154	    if (target) {
155	      stick.current = false
156	      target.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start', behavior: 'smooth' })
157	    }
158	  }, [focusStep, turnCount])
159	
160	  const last = transcript.turns[transcript.turns.length - 1]
161	  const waitingOnModel =
162	    live && (!last || (last.calls.length > 0 && last.calls.every((c) => c.result !== null)))
163	
164	  return (
165	    <div ref={ref} className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto" tabIndex={0} aria-label="Transcript">
166	      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl space-y-6 px-4 py-6 md:px-6">
167	        {loading ? (
168	          <LoadingState label={loadingLabel ?? 'Loading…'} />
169	        ) : null}
170	
171	        {notFound ? (
172	          <Callout
173	            tone="warn"
174	            slot="not-found"
175	            title="Run not found"
176	            body="Could not load this run: the harness has no run with this id for this browser identity — it may belong to another browser, or the id is wrong."
177	          />
178	        ) : error && turnCount === 0 && !loading ? (
179	          <Callout tone="warn" title="Could not load this run" body={error} />
180	        ) : null}
181	
182	        {transcript.taskPrompt ? <UserBubble text={transcript.taskPrompt} /> : null}
183	
184	        {transcript.turns.map((t, i) => (
185	          <TurnBlock
186	            key={t.step}
187	            turn={t}
188	            isLast={i === transcript.turns.length - 1}
189	            live={live}
190	            maxSteps={maxSteps}
191	            diffs={diffs}
192	          />
193	        ))}
194	
195	        {live && turnCount === 0 && !loading && !error && !notFound ? (
196	          <LoadingState
197	            label={transcript.status === 'queued' ? 'Queued — provisioning the sandbox…' : 'Provisioning the sandbox…'}
198	            sublabel="creating an isolated sandbox, copying the fixture repo, applying the fault plan"
199	          />
200	        ) : null}
201	        {waitingOnModel && turnCount > 0 ? (
202	          <LoadingState label="Waiting on the model…" sublabel={transcript.model ?? undefined} />
203	        ) : null}
204	
205	        {transcript.evaluation ? <ScoreRows evaluation={transcript.evaluation} /> : null}
206	
207	        {/* Pending or unavailable grading is never a pass: say so when a finished run has no evaluation. */}
208	        {!transcript.evaluation && !live && isTerminal(transcript.status) && !notFound ? (
209	          <Callout
210	            tone={transcript.status === 'ok' || transcript.status === 'truncated' ? 'warn' : 'neutral'}
211	            slot="not-graded"
212	            title="Not graded"
213	            body={
214	              transcript.status === 'ok' || transcript.status === 'truncated'
215	                ? transcript.score !== null
216	                  ? `The record carries a score of ${Math.round(transcript.score)} but no evaluation detail; the grader's checks are not available for this run.`
217	                  : 'The run finished but no evaluation was recorded — grading is pending or unavailable, not a pass.'
218	                : 'No evaluation exists for this run.'
219	            }
220	          />
221	        ) : null}
222	
223	        {transcript.status === 'error' ? (
224	          <Callout tone="error" title="The run ended with an error" body={transcript.error ?? null} />
225	        ) : transcript.error && !transcript.live ? (
226	          <Callout
227	            tone="warn"
228	            title={transcript.evaluation ? 'The run reported an error' : 'The run finished but could not be graded'}
229	            body={transcript.error}
230	          />
231	        ) : null}
232	        {transcript.status === 'truncated' ? (
233	          <Callout
234	            tone="warn"
235	            title={`Step budget exhausted (${maxSteps} steps)`}
236	            body="The agent did not submit in time; whatever it left on disk was still graded."
237	          />
238	        ) : null}
239	        {transport?.mode === 'poll' ? (
240	          <p className="text-center text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
241	            live stream unavailable — polling the run record every 1.5 s
242	          </p>
243	        ) : null}
244	        {footer}
245	      </div>
246	    </div>
247	  )
248	}

1	import { AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, FileX2, Lock, OctagonAlert, Timer } from 'lucide-react'
2	
3	import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
4	import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tooltip'
5	import { originText, taxonomyLabel } from '@/lib/codes'
6	import { FAULT_KIND_BLURB, faultBlurb } from '@/lib/format'
7	import type { ErrorClass, ErrorOrigin, FaultFired, FaultKind } from '@/lib/types'
8	
9	const ICON: Record<FaultKind, typeof FileX2> = {
10	  missing_file: FileX2,
11	  denied_write: Lock,
12	  ack_lost: Timer,
13	}
14	
15	/**
16	 * Colour by origin, as docs/error-taxonomy.md prescribes: injected ("simulated") and staged are
17	 * both amber — nothing real broke, or it broke on purpose at reset — and only a real failure is
18	 * red. The word on the badge is what keeps the two amber cases apart.
19	 */
20	const ORIGIN_TONE: Record<ErrorOrigin, string> = {
21	  injected: 'bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 border-amber-500/30',
22	  staged: 'bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 border-amber-500/30',
23	  real: 'bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300 border-rose-500/30',
24	}
25	
26	/** A scenario's fault kind (scenario table): either mode of the kind may be in play. */
27	export function FaultKindBadge({ kind, className }: { kind: FaultKind; className?: string }) {
28	  const Icon = ICON[kind] ?? AlertTriangle
29	  return (
30	    <Tooltip>
31	      <TooltipTrigger
32	        render={
33	          <Badge variant="outline" className={`${ORIGIN_TONE.injected} ${className ?? ''} font-mono`}>
34	            <Icon aria-hidden />
35	            {kind}
36	          </Badge>
37	        }
38	      />
39	      <TooltipContent>{FAULT_KIND_BLURB[kind] ?? 'Injected failure.'}</TooltipContent>
40	    </Tooltip>
41	  )
42	}
43	
44	/**
45	 * The badge on a call that a fault hit. Carries the origin word ("simulated" for an injected
46	 * fault, "staged" for a condition set up at reset) so a reader never mistakes it for a real error;
47	 * the tooltip is the taxonomy's fixed label plus what actually happened on disk.
48	 */
49	export function FaultFiredBadge({ fault }: { fault: FaultFired }) {
50	  const Icon = ICON[fault.kind] ?? AlertTriangle
51	  const origin: ErrorOrigin = fault.origin ?? 'injected'
52	  const label = taxonomyLabel(origin, fault.kind, fault.layer ?? null)
53	  return (
54	    <Tooltip>
55	      <TooltipTrigger
56	        render={
57	          <Badge variant="outline" className={`${ORIGIN_TONE[origin]} font-mono`} data-origin={origin}>
58	            <Icon aria-hidden />
59	            {originText(origin)}: {fault.kind}
60	          </Badge>
61	        }
62	      />
63	      <TooltipContent className="max-w-xs">
64	        {label ? <div className="font-mono text-[11px]">{label}</div> : null}
65	        <div>{fault.description || faultBlurb(fault.kind, origin)}</div>
66	      </TooltipContent>
67	    </Tooltip>
68	  )
69	}
70	
71	/**
72	 * The badge for a classified error that no fault explains (docs/error-taxonomy.md `ErrorClass`):
73	 * red for a real failure, amber for an injected / staged one the record classified without a
74	 * `fault` payload.
75	 */
76	export function ErrorOriginBadge({ errorClass }: { errorClass: ErrorClass }) {
77	  const real = errorClass.origin === 'real'
78	  return (
79	    <Tooltip>
80	      <TooltipTrigger
81	        render={
82	          <Badge variant="outline" className={`${ORIGIN_TONE[errorClass.origin]} font-mono`} data-origin={errorClass.origin}>
83	            {real ? <OctagonAlert aria-hidden /> : <AlertTriangle aria-hidden />}
84	            {originText(errorClass.origin)}: {errorClass.code}
85	          </Badge>
86	        }
87	      />
88	      <TooltipContent className="max-w-xs">
89	        <div className="font-mono text-[11px]">{errorClass.label}</div>
90	        {errorClass.outcome_known === false ? (
91	          <div>Whether the call ran is unknown — the right move is to read the state back before retrying.</div>
92	        ) : null}
93	        {errorClass.detail ? <div className="opacity-80">{errorClass.detail}</div> : null}
94	      </TooltipContent>
95	    </Tooltip>
96	  )
97	}
98	
99	/**
100	 * A live hint, not a verdict: the reducer saw a later call on this path succeed after a
101	 * successful read of it. The grader's checks (ScoreRows) are the authoritative word on recovery.
102	 */
103	export function RecoveredBadge() {
104	  return (
105	    <Tooltip>
106	      <TooltipTrigger
107	        render={
108	          <Badge
109	            variant="outline"
110	            className="border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/15 font-mono text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300"
111	            data-hint="read-back"
112	          >
113	            <CheckCircle2 aria-hidden />
114	            read-back seen
115	          </Badge>
116	        }
117	      />
118	      <TooltipContent className="max-w-xs">
119	        Heuristic from the call order: a later call on this path succeeded, and a successful read
120	        of the same path came first — the agent verified state instead of retrying blind. The
121	        grader's checks are authoritative.
122	      </TooltipContent>
123	    </Tooltip>
124	  )
125	}

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:42:43.362Z
1	/**
2	 * ThinkingTrace — adapted from Beautiful UI `ThinkingState` ("Steps" variant; MIT, © 2026 Shane
3	 * Levine).
4	 *
5	 * A collapsible per-step trace. The header shimmers while the step is in flight and settles when
6	 * it is done; the body lists the step's tool calls down a thin timeline rail (a spinner on the
7	 * call still running, a check or cross once it has a result), an optional thinking paragraph
8	 * above, and whatever the parent passes as children (ToolCallChips) below.
9	 *
10	 * The scripted stage timers are gone: `active` / `done` come from the run reducer.
11	 */
12	
13	import { useId, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
14	import { Ban, Check, ChevronDown, CircleQuestionMark, Sparkles, Terminal, X } from 'lucide-react'
15	
16	import { ErrorOriginBadge, FaultFiredBadge, RecoveredBadge } from '@/components/FaultBadge'
17	import { callStatus } from '@/lib/callStatus'
18	import { oneLine } from '@/lib/format'
19	import type { ToolCallView } from '@/lib/reducer'
20	import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
21	
22	import { summariseCall, TOOL_ICON, toolLabel } from './toolMeta'
23	
24	export interface ThinkingTraceProps {
25	  step: number
26	  maxSteps?: number
27	  calls: ToolCallView[]
28	  /** Prose the agent produced in this step, shown above the call rows. */
29	  thinking?: string | null
30	  /** The step is in flight: shimmer header, spinner on the call without a result. */
31	  active: boolean
32	  /** The step has settled. */
33	  done: boolean
34	  /** Initial open state; defaults to open while `active`. Users can toggle regardless. */
35	  defaultOpen?: boolean
36	  /** Rendered inside the expanded body, under the rows. */
37	  children?: ReactNode
38	}
39	
40	function plural(n: number, word: string): string {
41	  return `${n} ${word}${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`
42	}
43	
44	function statusText(active: boolean, done: boolean, n: number): string {
45	  if (active) return n === 0 ? 'thinking' : `running ${plural(n, 'tool call')}`
46	  if (done) return n === 0 ? 'no tool calls' : plural(n, 'tool call')
47	  return 'pending'
48	}
49	
50	/** Rail glyph from structured status (`lib/callStatus.ts`); an unknown outcome is not a cross. */
51	function Glyph({ call, active }: { call: ToolCallView; active: boolean }) {
52	  const st = callStatus(call, active)
53	  switch (st.kind) {
54	    case 'running':
55	      return (
56	        <span
57	          className="bui-spin size-3 shrink-0 rounded-full border-[1.5px] border-line-strong border-t-ink-2"
58	          aria-label="running"
59	          role="img"
60	        />
61	      )
62	    case 'pending':
63	      return <span className="size-3 shrink-0 rounded-full border-[1.5px] border-line" aria-label="pending" role="img" />
64	    case 'failed':
65	      return (
66	        <span role="img" aria-label="error" title={st.detail} className="flex shrink-0">
67	          <X className="size-3.5 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300" strokeWidth={2.5} aria-hidden />
68	        </span>
69	      )
70	    case 'unknown':
71	      return (
72	        <span role="img" aria-label="unknown" title={st.detail} className="flex shrink-0">
73	          <CircleQuestionMark className="size-3.5 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300" strokeWidth={2.5} aria-hidden />
74	        </span>
75	      )
76	    case 'not_executed':
77	      return (
78	        <span role="img" aria-label="not executed" title={st.detail} className="flex shrink-0">
79	          <Ban className="size-3.5 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300" strokeWidth={2.5} aria-hidden />
80	        </span>
81	      )
82	    default:
83	      return (
84	        <span role="img" aria-label="ok" title={st.detail} className="flex shrink-0">
85	          <Check className="size-3.5 text-ink-3" strokeWidth={2.5} aria-hidden />
86	        </span>
87	      )
88	  }
89	}
90	
91	export function ThinkingTrace({
92	  step,
93	  maxSteps,
94	  calls,
95	  thinking,
96	  active,
97	  done,
98	  defaultOpen,
99	  children,
100	}: ThinkingTraceProps) {
101	  const [manual, setManual] = useState<boolean | null>(null)
102	  const expanded = manual ?? defaultOpen ?? active
103	  const panelId = useId()
104	
105	  const n = calls.length
106	  const faults = calls.filter((c) => c.fault).length
107	  const recovered = calls.filter((c) => c.fault && c.recovered).length
108	  const kinds = calls.map((c) => callStatus(c).kind)
109	  const errors = kinds.filter((k) => k === 'failed').length
110	  const unknown = kinds.filter((k) => k === 'unknown').length
111	  const title = maxSteps ? `Step ${step} of ${maxSteps}` : `Step ${step}`
112	
113	  return (
114	    <div className="flex w-full flex-col" data-slot="thinking-trace" data-step={step}>
115	      <button
116	        type="button"
117	        aria-expanded={expanded}
118	        aria-controls={panelId}
119	        onClick={() => setManual(!expanded)}
120	        className="-mx-1.5 flex w-fit max-w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-1.5 py-1 text-left transition-colors duration-100 hover:bg-muted/40 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-none"
121	      >
122	        <Sparkles
123	          className={cn('size-4 shrink-0 transition-colors duration-200', active ? 'text-ink-2' : 'text-ink-3')}
124	          fill="currentColor"
125	          strokeWidth={0}
126	          aria-hidden
127	        />
128	        <span className="text-[13px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-ink">{title}</span>
129	        <span role="status" className="contents">
130	          {active ? (
131	            <span className="bui-shimmer text-[13px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-ink-2">
132	              {statusText(active, done, n)}
133	            </span>
134	          ) : (
135	            <span className="bui-fade-in text-[13px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-ink-2">
136	              {statusText(active, done, n)}
137	            </span>
138	          )}
139	        </span>
140	        {faults > 0 ? (
141	          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 tabular-nums">{plural(faults, 'fault')}</span>
142	        ) : null}
143	        {recovered > 0 ? (
144	          <span
145	            className="font-mono text-[11px] text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 tabular-nums"
146	            title="Heuristic from the call order; the grader's checks are authoritative."
147	          >
148	            {recovered} read-back seen
149	          </span>
150	        ) : null}
151	        {unknown > 0 ? (
152	          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 tabular-nums">{unknown} unknown</span>
153	        ) : null}
154	        {errors > 0 ? (
155	          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300 tabular-nums">{plural(errors, 'error')}</span>
156	        ) : null}
157	        <ChevronDown
158	          className={cn('size-3.5 shrink-0 text-ink-3 transition-transform duration-300', expanded && 'rotate-180')}
159	          aria-hidden
160	        />
161	      </button>
162	
163	      <div
164	        id={panelId}
165	        aria-hidden={!expanded}
166	        inert={!expanded}
167	        className={cn(
168	          'grid motion-safe:transition-[grid-template-rows,opacity] motion-safe:duration-400 motion-safe:ease-[cubic-bezier(0.23,1,0.32,1)]',
169	          expanded ? 'grid-rows-[1fr] opacity-100' : 'grid-rows-[0fr] opacity-0',
170	        )}
171	      >
172	        <div className="min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
173	          <div className="relative mt-1 ml-[7px] pl-4">
174	            <span aria-hidden className="absolute top-0 bottom-1 left-0 w-px bg-line" />
175	            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 py-1">
176	              {thinking ? (
177	                <p className="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12.5px] leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap text-ink-2">
178	                  {thinking}
179	                </p>
180	              ) : null}
181	
182	              {calls.map((call, i) => {
183	                const Icon = TOOL_ICON[call.tool] ?? Terminal
184	                return (
185	                  <div
186	                    key={call.toolUseId}
187	                    className="bui-fade-up flex min-h-7 w-full min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1 rounded-md px-1.5 py-0.5"
188	                    style={{ animationDelay: `${active ? 0 : Math.min(i, 8) * 60}ms` }}
189	                    data-slot="trace-row"
190	                  >
191	                    <Glyph call={call} active={active} />
192	                    <Icon className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-ink-3" aria-hidden />
193	                    <span className="shrink-0 text-[12.5px] font-medium text-ink">{toolLabel(call.tool)}</span>
194	                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-3">
195	                      {oneLine(summariseCall(call), 140)}
196	                    </span>
197	                    {call.fault ? <FaultFiredBadge fault={call.fault} /> : null}
198	                    {call.result?.errorClass && (call.result.errorClass.origin === 'real' || !call.fault) ? (
199	                      <ErrorOriginBadge errorClass={call.result.errorClass} />
200	                    ) : null}
201	                    {call.recovered ? <RecoveredBadge /> : null}
202	                  </div>
203	                )
204	              })}
205	
206	              {active && n === 0 ? (
207	                <div className="flex min-h-7 items-center gap-2 px-1.5 text-[12.5px] text-ink-3">
208	                  <span className="bui-spin size-3 shrink-0 rounded-full border-[1.5px] border-line-strong border-t-ink-2" />
209	                  waiting for the model…
210	                </div>
211	              ) : null}
212	
213	              {children ? <div className="mt-1 min-w-0">{children}</div> : null}
214	            </div>
215	          </div>
216	        </div>
217	      </div>
218	    </div>
219	  )
220	}

1	/**
2	 * ToolCallChips — adapted from Beautiful UI `ToolChips` (MIT, © 2026 Shane Levine).
3	 *
4	 * One row per tool call: tool icon that swaps to a chevron on hover, a label, the command / path
5	 * in a mono chip, then status. Expanding a row reveals the call input, every result part the
6	 * harness returned (error + code, stdout, stderr, content, entries, bytes written, truncation),
7	 * and — for a mutating call whose path has a `FileDiff` — the unified diff.
8	 *
9	 * The original's hover-portal diff preview was replaced by inline expansion so it behaves inside
10	 * a scrolling column; the demo timers are gone (rows appear as the reducer adds them).
11	 */
12	
13	import { useId, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
14	import { Ban, Check, ChevronDown, CircleQuestionMark, LoaderCircle, Terminal, X } from 'lucide-react'
15	
16	import { ErrorOriginBadge, FaultFiredBadge, RecoveredBadge } from '@/components/FaultBadge'
17	import { callStatus, type CallStatusTone } from '@/lib/callStatus'
18	import { fmtDuration, oneLine } from '@/lib/format'
19	import type { ToolCallView } from '@/lib/reducer'
20	import type { FileDiff } from '@/lib/types'
21	import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
22	
23	import { CodeBlock, parseUnifiedDiff } from './CodeBlock'
24	import { diffForCall, resultParts, summariseCall, TOOL_ICON, toolLabel } from './toolMeta'
25	
26	export interface ToolCallChipsProps {
27	  calls: ToolCallView[]
28	  /** Workspace diffs; a mutating call whose path matches gets its diff inline. */
29	  diffs?: FileDiff[]
30	  /** A call without a result renders as running (instead of "no result"). */
31	  live?: boolean
32	}
33	
34	const CLIP = 4000
35	function clip(s: string): string {
36	  return s.length > CLIP ? `${s.slice(0, CLIP)}\n…[truncated in view]` : s
37	}
38	
39	function Section({ label, children }: { label: string; children: ReactNode }) {
40	  return (
41	    <div>
42	      <div className="mb-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] tracking-wide text-ink-3 uppercase">{label}</div>
43	      {children}
44	    </div>
45	  )
46	}
47	
48	function Mono({ children, tone = 'plain' }: { children: ReactNode; tone?: 'error' | 'plain' }) {
49	  return (
50	    <pre
51	      className={cn(
52	        'overflow-x-auto rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed break-words whitespace-pre-wrap',
53	        tone === 'error' ? 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300' : 'bg-muted/40 text-ink-2',
54	      )}
55	    >
56	      {children}
57	    </pre>
58	  )
59	}
60	
61	const PILL_TONE: Record<CallStatusTone, string> = {
62	  muted: 'text-ink-3 ring-line',
63	  ok: 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 ring-emerald-500/30',
64	  error: 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300 ring-rose-500/30',
65	  // outcome unknown / not executed: neither a pass nor a failure
66	  unknown: 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 ring-amber-500/30',
67	}
68	
69	/**
70	 * Status from structured fields only (`lib/callStatus.ts`): a lost acknowledgement is "no ack",
71	 * not a red cross, because the write may well have landed.
72	 */
73	function StatusPill({ call, live }: { call: ToolCallView; live: boolean }) {
74	  const st = callStatus(call, live)
75	  const base = 'inline-flex h-5 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 font-mono text-[11px] ring-1 ring-inset'
76	  let icon: ReactNode = null
77	  switch (st.kind) {
78	    case 'running':
79	      icon = <LoaderCircle className="bui-spin size-3" aria-hidden />
80	      break
81	    case 'ok':
82	      icon = <Check className="size-3" aria-hidden />
83	      break
84	    case 'failed':
85	      icon = <X className="size-3" aria-hidden />
86	      break
87	    case 'unknown':
88	      icon = <CircleQuestionMark className="size-3" aria-hidden />
89	      break
90	    case 'not_executed':
91	      icon = <Ban className="size-3" aria-hidden />
92	      break
93	    default:
94	      break
95	  }
96	  return (
97	    <span
98	      className={cn(base, st.kind === 'running' ? 'bg-muted/50 text-ink-2 ring-line' : PILL_TONE[st.tone])}
99	      data-status={st.attr}
100	      title={st.detail}
101	    >
102	      {icon}
103	      {st.label}
104	    </span>
105	  )
106	}
107	
108	function InputBlock({ call }: { call: ToolCallView }) {
109	  if (call.command) return <Mono>$ {call.command}</Mono>
110	  if (call.tool === 'write_file' && typeof call.input.content === 'string') {
111	    const { content, ...rest } = call.input
112	    return (
113	      <div className="space-y-1.5">
114	        <CodeBlock code={content} filename={call.path ?? 'content'} maxHeight={240} />
115	        {Object.keys(rest).length > 0 ? <Mono>{JSON.stringify(rest)}</Mono> : null}
116	      </div>
117	    )
118	  }
119	  return <Mono>{JSON.stringify(call.input, null, 2)}</Mono>
120	}
121	
122	function ResultBlock({ call }: { call: ToolCallView }) {
123	  const result = call.result!
124	  const parts = resultParts(result, call.path)
125	  return (
126	    <>
127	      {parts.map((p) => (
128	        <Section key={p.label} label={p.label}>
129	          {p.code ? (
130	            <CodeBlock code={clip(p.body)} filename={p.code.filename ?? undefined} maxHeight={280} />
131	          ) : (
132	            <Mono tone={p.tone}>{clip(p.body)}</Mono>
133	          )}
134	        </Section>
135	      ))}
136	      {result.truncated ? (
137	        <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-3">Output was truncated by the environment.</p>
138	      ) : null}
139	    </>
140	  )
141	}
142	
143	function Row({
144	  call,
145	  diff,
146	  live,
147	  index,
148	}: {
149	  call: ToolCallView
150	  diff: FileDiff | null
151	  live: boolean
152	  index: number
153	}) {
154	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
155	  const panelId = useId()
156	  const Icon = TOOL_ICON[call.tool] ?? Terminal
157	  const result = call.result
158	  const summary = summariseCall(call)
159	  const counts = diff ? parseUnifiedDiff(diff.unified) : null
160	  const added = counts?.filter((l) => l.kind === 'add').length ?? 0
161	  const removed = counts?.filter((l) => l.kind === 'del').length ?? 0
162	
163	  return (
164	    <div
165	      className="bui-fade-up"
166	      style={{ animationDelay: `${live ? 0 : Math.min(index, 8) * 50}ms` }}
167	      data-slot="tool-call"
168	      data-tool={call.tool}
169	    >
170	      <div className="flex min-h-7 w-full min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1">
171	        <button
172	          type="button"
173	          aria-expanded={open}
174	          aria-controls={panelId}
175	          onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
176	          className="group/row flex h-7 min-w-0 flex-1 basis-48 items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-1 text-left transition-colors duration-100 hover:bg-muted/40 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-none"
177	        >
178	          <span className="relative flex size-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center text-ink-3">
179	            <Icon
180	              className={cn(
181	                'size-[13px] transition-opacity duration-100 group-hover/row:opacity-0',
182	                open && 'opacity-0',
183	              )}
184	              aria-hidden
185	            />
186	            <ChevronDown
187	              className={cn(
188	                'absolute size-3 transition-[opacity,transform] duration-150 group-hover/row:opacity-100',
189	                open ? 'rotate-0 opacity-100' : '-rotate-90 opacity-0',
190	              )}
191	              aria-hidden
192	            />
193	          </span>
194	          <span className="shrink-0 text-[12.5px] font-medium text-ink">{toolLabel(call.tool)}</span>
195	          <span className="inline-flex h-5.5 min-w-0 flex-1 items-center rounded-md bg-muted/50 px-1.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-2 ring-1 ring-line ring-inset">
196	            <span className="truncate">{oneLine(summary, 160)}</span>
197	          </span>
198	        </button>
199	
200	        {diff ? (
201	          <span
202	            className="shrink-0 font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums"
203	            aria-label={`${added} lines added, ${removed} lines removed`}
204	          >
205	            <span className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300">+{added}</span>{' '}
206	            <span className="text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300">−{removed}</span>
207	          </span>
208	        ) : null}
209	        {call.fault ? <FaultFiredBadge fault={call.fault} /> : null}
210	        {result?.errorClass && (result.errorClass.origin === 'real' || !call.fault) ? (
211	          <ErrorOriginBadge errorClass={result.errorClass} />
212	        ) : null}
213	        {call.recovered ? <RecoveredBadge /> : null}
214	        <StatusPill call={call} live={live} />
215	        {result ? (
216	          <span className="shrink-0 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-3 tabular-nums">
217	            {fmtDuration(result.durationMs)}
218	          </span>
219	        ) : null}
220	      </div>
221	
222	      <div
223	        id={panelId}
224	        aria-hidden={!open}
225	        inert={!open}
226	        className={cn(
227	          'grid motion-safe:transition-[grid-template-rows,opacity] motion-safe:duration-300 motion-safe:ease-[cubic-bezier(0.23,1,0.32,1)]',
228	          open ? 'grid-rows-[1fr] opacity-100' : 'grid-rows-[0fr] opacity-0',
229	        )}
230	      >
231	        <div className="min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
232	          <div className="mt-0.5 mb-1 ml-3 flex flex-col gap-2 border-l border-line py-1 pl-3.5">
233	            <Section label="input">
234	              <InputBlock call={call} />
235	            </Section>
236	            {result ? (
237	              <ResultBlock call={call} />
238	            ) : (
239	              <p className="text-[11.5px] text-ink-3">{live ? 'Waiting for the result…' : 'No result recorded.'}</p>
240	            )}
241	            {diff ? (
242	              <Section label="diff">
243	                <CodeBlock diff={diff.unified} filename={diff.path} maxHeight={320} />
244	              </Section>
245	            ) : null}
246	          </div>
247	        </div>
248	      </div>
249	    </div>
250	  )
251	}

253	export function ToolCallChips({ calls, diffs = [], live = false }: ToolCallChipsProps) {
254	  if (calls.length === 0) {
255	    return <p className="px-1 text-[12px] text-ink-3">No tool calls.</p>
256	  }
257	  return (
258	    <div className="flex w-full min-w-0 flex-col gap-1" data-slot="tool-call-chips">
259	      {calls.map((c, i) => (
260	        <Row key={c.toolUseId} call={c} diff={diffForCall(c, diffs)} live={live} index={i} />
261	      ))}
262	    </div>
263	  )
264	}

1	import { useEffect, useMemo, type ReactNode } from 'react'
2	import { useDefaultLayout, usePanelRef, type LayoutStorage } from 'react-resizable-panels'

4	import { ResizableHandle, ResizablePanel, ResizablePanelGroup } from '@/components/ui/resizable'

10	const LAYOUT_ID = 'faultline.run-layout'
11	const PANEL_IDS = ['transcript', 'workspace']
12	
13	function storageOrUndefined(): LayoutStorage | undefined {
14	  try {
15	    const s = window.localStorage
16	    // A blocked storage throws on access; probe once.
17	    s.getItem(LAYOUT_ID)
18	    return s
19	  } catch {
20	    return undefined
21	  }
22	}

24	/**
25	 * Transcript column + workspace panel.
26	 *
27	 * ≥1280 px: shadcn Resizable panels — drag the handle, double-click it to reset, collapse the
28	 * workspace to zero; the split is remembered per browser (localStorage). Below that: the
29	 * transcript alone, with the workspace as an overlay sheet (the Sheet is portaled, so it is not
30	 * rendered at all on wide screens).
31	 */
32	export function RunLayout({
33	  children,
34	  state,
35	  live,
36	  open,
37	  onOpenChange,
38	}: {
39	  children: ReactNode
40	  state: ViewState | null
41	  live: boolean
42	  open: boolean
43	  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
44	}) {
45	  const wide = useIsWide()
46	  const panelRef = usePanelRef()
47	  const storage = useMemo(() => storageOrUndefined(), [])
48	  const { defaultLayout, onLayoutChanged } = useDefaultLayout({
49	    id: LAYOUT_ID,
50	    panelIds: PANEL_IDS,
51	    storage,
52	    onlySaveAfterUserInteractions: true,
53	  })
54	
55	  // Keep the imperative panel in step with the `open` state (top-bar toggle, breakpoint changes).
56	  useEffect(() => {
57	    if (!wide) return
58	    const p = panelRef.current
59	    if (!p) return
60	    if (open && p.isCollapsed()) p.expand()
61	    else if (!open && !p.isCollapsed()) p.collapse()
62	  }, [open, wide, panelRef])
63	
64	  if (!wide) {
65	    return (
66	      <>
67	        <div className="flex min-h-0 min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">{children}</div>
68	        <Sheet open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
69	          <SheetContent side="right" className="flex w-[92vw] flex-col gap-0 p-0 sm:max-w-[520px]">
70	            <SheetHeader className="border-b border-border px-4 py-3">
71	              <SheetTitle>Workspace</SheetTitle>
72	              <SheetDescription>Files, diffs and logs for this run.</SheetDescription>
73	            </SheetHeader>
74	            <WorkspaceTabs state={state} live={live} />
75	          </SheetContent>
76	        </Sheet>
77	      </>
78	    )
79	  }
80	
81	  return (
82	    <ResizablePanelGroup
83	      orientation="horizontal"
84	      defaultLayout={defaultLayout}
85	      onLayoutChanged={onLayoutChanged}
86	      className="h-auto min-h-0 flex-1"
87	    >
88	      <ResizablePanel id="transcript" defaultSize="64" minSize={420}>
89	        <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 min-w-0 flex-col">{children}</div>
90	      </ResizablePanel>
91	      <ResizableHandle
92	        withHandle
93	        aria-label="Resize the workspace panel"
94	        className="w-1.5 bg-transparent hover:bg-border/60 data-[resize-handle-active]:bg-brand/40"
95	      />
96	      <ResizablePanel
97	        id="workspace"
98	        defaultSize="36"
99	        minSize={300}
100	        maxSize="65"
101	        collapsible
102	        collapsedSize={0}
103	        panelRef={panelRef}
104	        onResize={() => {
105	          // Dragging the handle to zero collapses the panel; reflect that in the toggle state.
106	          const collapsed = panelRef.current?.isCollapsed() ?? false
107	          if (collapsed === open) onOpenChange(!collapsed)
108	        }}
109	      >
110	        <aside className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col border-l border-border bg-card/30" aria-label="Workspace">
111	          <WorkspaceTabs state={state} live={live} />
112	        </aside>
113	      </ResizablePanel>
114	    </ResizablePanelGroup>
115	  )
116	}

1	import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
2	
3	import { CodeBlock, FileStatusTable } from '@/components/bui'
4	import { LogsPanel } from '@/components/LogsPanel'
5	import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
6	import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tabs'
7	import type { ViewState } from '@/lib/reducer'
8	import type { FileDiff, FileEntry } from '@/lib/types'
9	
10	const NO_FILES: FileEntry[] = []
11	const NO_DIFFS: FileDiff[] = []
12	
13	export function WorkspaceTabs({ state, live }: { state: ViewState | null; live: boolean }) {
14	  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null)
15	  const [tab, setTab] = useState('files')
16	  const files = state?.files ?? NO_FILES
17	  const diffs = state?.diffs ?? NO_DIFFS
18	  const changed = useMemo(() => files.filter((f) => f.status !== 'unchanged').length, [files])
19	  const ordered = useMemo(
20	    () => (selected ? [...diffs].sort((a, b) => (a.path === selected ? -1 : b.path === selected ? 1 : 0)) : diffs),
21	    [diffs, selected],
22	  )
23	
24	  return (
25	    <Tabs value={tab} onValueChange={(v) => setTab(String(v))} className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0">
26	      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-border px-3 py-2">
27	        <TabsList variant="line">
28	          <TabsTrigger value="files">Files</TabsTrigger>
29	          <TabsTrigger value="diffs">Diffs</TabsTrigger>
30	          <TabsTrigger value="logs">Logs</TabsTrigger>
31	        </TabsList>
32	        <span className="flex-1" />
33	        {files.length > 0 ? (
34	          <Badge variant="outline" className="font-mono text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
35	            {changed} changed
36	          </Badge>
37	        ) : null}
38	      </div>
39	      <TabsContent value="files" className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-3">
40	        {files.length === 0 ? (
41	          <p className="p-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
42	            No workspace snapshot yet — it arrives with the first mutating step.
43	          </p>
44	        ) : (
45	          <FileStatusTable
46	            files={files}
47	            selected={selected}
48	            onSelect={(p) => {
49	              setSelected(p)
50	              if (diffs.some((d) => d.path === p)) setTab('diffs')
51	            }}
52	          />
53	        )}
54	      </TabsContent>
55	      <TabsContent value="diffs" className="min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-3 overflow-y-auto p-3">
56	        {ordered.length === 0 ? (
57	          <p className="p-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nothing has changed on disk yet.</p>
58	        ) : (
59	          ordered.map((d) => <CodeBlock key={d.path} diff={d.unified} filename={d.path} />)
60	        )}
61	      </TabsContent>
62	      <TabsContent value="logs" className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-hidden">
63	        {state ? <LogsPanel state={state} live={live} /> : null}
64	      </TabsContent>
65	    </Tabs>
66	  )
67	}
