1	import { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react'
2	
3	import { RunStatusStrip, type TranscriptSource } from '@/components/layout/RunStatusStrip'
4	import { TopBar } from '@/components/layout/TopBar'
5	import { TranscriptView } from '@/components/transcript/TranscriptView'
6	import { RunLayout } from '@/components/workspace/RunLayout'
7	import { useWorkspaceOpen } from '@/hooks/useWorkspaceLayout'
8	import type { HarnessState } from '@/hooks/useHarness'
9	import { useRunView } from '@/hooks/useRunView'
10	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
11	import { navigate } from '@/lib/router'
12	import { transcriptFromViewState } from '@/lib/transcript'
13	
14	const log = getLogger('web')
15	
16	/**
17	 * A run addressed by id alone. Upgrades itself to /conversations/{id} as soon as the seed record
18	 * (the one useRunView fetched — no second GET) says which conversation it belongs to. A 404 is
19	 * its own state: the run does not exist, or belongs to another browser identity.
20	 */
21	export function RunPage({ harness, runId }: { harness: HarnessState; runId: string }) {
22	  const source = useMemo(() => ({ kind: 'live', runId }) as const, [runId])
23	  const rv = useRunView(harness.client, source)
24	  const [workspaceOpen, setWorkspaceOpen] = useWorkspaceOpen()
25	  const live = !rv.notFound && (rv.state.status === 'running' || rv.state.status === 'queued')
26	
27	  const conversationId = rv.record?.conversation_id ?? null
28	  useEffect(() => {
29	    if (!conversationId) return
30	    log.info('run.upgrade', 'run belongs to a conversation; redirecting', {
31	      run_id: runId,
32	      conversation_id: conversationId,
33	    })
34	    navigate({ kind: 'conversation', id: conversationId, runId }, { replace: true })
35	  }, [conversationId, runId])
36	
37	  useEffect(() => {
38	    document.title = `${rv.state.scenarioId ?? 'run'} · ${runId} — Faultline`
39	  }, [rv.state.scenarioId, runId])
40	
41	  const transcript = useMemo(() => transcriptFromViewState(rv.state, live), [rv.state, live])
42	  const src: TranscriptSource = live ? (rv.transport?.mode === 'poll' ? 'poll' : 'sse') : 'record'
43	
44	  return (
45	    <div className="flex h-dvh min-h-0 flex-col">
46	      <TopBar
47	        crumbs={[{ label: 'Runs', route: { kind: 'home' } }, { label: rv.state.scenarioId ? `${rv.state.scenarioId} · ${runId}` : runId }]}
48	        onToggleWorkspace={() => setWorkspaceOpen((v) => !v)}
49	        workspaceOpen={workspaceOpen}
50	      >
51	        {rv.notFound ? null : <RunStatusStrip state={rv.state} transport={rv.transport} source={src} />}
52	      </TopBar>
53	      <RunLayout state={rv.notFound ? null : rv.state} live={live} open={workspaceOpen} onOpenChange={setWorkspaceOpen}>
54	          <TranscriptView
55	            transcript={transcript}
56	            diffs={rv.state.diffs}
57	            maxSteps={rv.state.maxSteps}
58	            loading={rv.loading}
59	            loadingLabel="Loading run…"
60	            error={rv.loadError}
61	            notFound={rv.notFound}
62	            transport={rv.transport}
63	          />
64	      </RunLayout>
65	    </div>
66	  )
67	}

1	/**
2	 * ScoreRows — adapted from Beautiful UI `TaskRows` ("Capsules" variant; MIT, © 2026 Shane
3	 * Levine).
4	 *
5	 * A score headline, then one capsule per grading check (round pass/fail badge, id, weight,
6	 * status pill) that expands to the grader's detail. A final capsule holds the pytest summary
7	 * and expands to the raw output in a CodeBlock. The demo's staged running/failed sequence was
8	 * removed — an evaluation is always settled.
9	 */
10	
11	import { useId, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
12	import { Check, ChevronDown, X } from 'lucide-react'
13	
14	import type { Check as CheckResult, EvaluateResponse } from '@/lib/types'
15	import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
16	
17	import { CodeBlock } from './CodeBlock'
18	
19	export interface ScoreRowsProps {
20	  evaluation: EvaluateResponse
21	}
22	
23	export function scoreTone(score: number): string {
24	  if (score >= 85) return 'text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300'
25	  if (score >= 50) return 'text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300'
26	  return 'text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300'
27	}
28	
29	function RoundBadge({ ok }: { ok: boolean }) {
30	  return (
31	    <span
32	      className={cn(
33	        'bui-pop-in flex size-5.5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-white',
34	        ok ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-rose-500',
35	      )}
36	      role="img"
37	      aria-label={ok ? 'pass' : 'fail'}
38	    >
39	      {ok ? <Check className="size-3" strokeWidth={3.5} aria-hidden /> : <X className="size-3" strokeWidth={3.5} aria-hidden />}
40	    </span>
41	  )
42	}
43	
44	function Pill({ ok, children }: { ok: boolean; children: ReactNode }) {
45	  return (
46	    <span
47	      className={cn(
48	        'inline-flex h-5.5 shrink-0 items-center rounded-full px-2 text-[11.5px] font-medium',
49	        ok ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300' : 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300',
50	      )}
51	    >
52	      {children}
53	    </span>
54	  )
55	}
56	
57	function Capsule({
58	  ok,
59	  label,
60	  mono = false,
61	  amount,
62	  pill,
63	  index,
64	  open,
65	  onToggle,
66	  children,
67	}: {
68	  ok: boolean
69	  label: string
70	  mono?: boolean
71	  amount: string
72	  pill: string
73	  index: number
74	  open: boolean
75	  onToggle: () => void
76	  children: ReactNode
77	}) {
78	  const panelId = useId()
79	  return (
80	    <div
81	      className="bui-fade-up self-stretch overflow-hidden border border-line bg-surface transition-[border-radius,background-color] duration-300 hover:bg-muted/30"
82	      style={{ borderRadius: open ? 14 : 22, animationDelay: `${index * 80}ms` }}
83	      data-slot="score-row"
84	      data-ok={ok}
85	    >
86	      <button
87	        type="button"
88	        aria-expanded={open}
89	        aria-controls={panelId}
90	        onClick={onToggle}
91	        className="flex h-11 w-full items-center gap-2.5 px-2.5 text-left focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-inset"
92	      >
93	        <span className="flex size-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center">
94	          <RoundBadge ok={ok} />
95	        </span>
96	        <span className={cn('min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-[13px] font-medium text-ink', mono && 'font-mono text-[12.5px]')}>
97	          {label}
98	        </span>
99	        <span className="shrink-0 text-[12px] text-ink-2 tabular-nums">{amount}</span>
100	        <Pill ok={ok}>{pill}</Pill>
101	        <span aria-hidden className="-ml-1 flex size-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-ink-3">
102	          <ChevronDown className={cn('size-[15px] transition-transform duration-300', open && 'rotate-180')} />
103	        </span>
104	      </button>
105	
106	      <div
107	        id={panelId}
108	        aria-hidden={!open}
109	        inert={!open}
110	        className={cn(
111	          'grid motion-safe:transition-[grid-template-rows,opacity] motion-safe:duration-300 motion-safe:ease-[cubic-bezier(0.23,1,0.32,1)]',
112	          open ? 'grid-rows-[1fr] opacity-100' : 'grid-rows-[0fr] opacity-0',
113	        )}
114	      >
115	        <div className="min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
116	          <div className="mb-2.5 grid grid-cols-[24px_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-2.5 px-2.5">
117	            <span aria-hidden className="mx-auto h-full w-px bg-line" />
118	            <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-1.5">{children}</div>
119	          </div>
120	        </div>
121	      </div>
122	    </div>
123	  )
124	}
125	
126	function CheckBody({ check }: { check: CheckResult }) {
127	  return (
128	    <div className="bui-fade-up flex items-start justify-between gap-3" style={{ animationDelay: '120ms' }}>
129	      <span className="min-w-0 text-[12px] leading-relaxed break-words text-ink-2">{check.detail || '—'}</span>
130	      <span className="shrink-0 font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-3 tabular-nums">{check.ok ? 'ok' : 'fail'}</span>
131	    </div>
132	  )
133	}
134	
135	export function ScoreRows({ evaluation }: ScoreRowsProps) {
136	  const tests = evaluation.tests ?? { passed: 0, failed: 0, errors: 0, output: '' }
137	  const checks = evaluation.checks ?? []
138	  const okChecks = checks.filter((c) => c.ok).length
139	  const testsOk = tests.failed === 0 && tests.errors === 0
140	  const [open, setOpen] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({})
141	  const toggle = (key: string) => setOpen((cur) => ({ ...cur, [key]: !cur[key] }))
142	
143	  return (
144	    <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-2" data-slot="score-rows">
145	      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 gap-y-1 px-1">
146	        <span className={cn('font-mono text-3xl tabular-nums', scoreTone(evaluation.score))} aria-label="score">
147	          {Math.round(evaluation.score)}
148	        </span>
149	        <span className="text-[12.5px] text-ink-3">/ 100</span>
150	        <Pill ok={evaluation.passed}>{evaluation.passed ? 'Passed' : 'Failed'}</Pill>
151	        <span className="font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-3 tabular-nums">
152	          {tests.passed} passed · {tests.failed} failed · {tests.errors} errors
153	        </span>
154	        <span className="font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-3 tabular-nums">
155	          checks {okChecks}/{checks.length}
156	        </span>
157	      </div>
158	
159	      {checks.map((c, i) => (
160	        <Capsule
161	          key={c.id}
162	          ok={c.ok}
163	          label={c.id}
164	          mono
165	          amount={`weight ${c.weight}`}
166	          pill={c.ok ? 'Passed' : 'Failed'}
167	          index={i}
168	          open={!!open[`check:${c.id}`]}
169	          onToggle={() => toggle(`check:${c.id}`)}
170	        >
171	          <CheckBody check={c} />
172	        </Capsule>
173	      ))}
174	
175	      <Capsule
176	        ok={testsOk}
177	        label="pytest"
178	        amount={`${tests.passed} passed · ${tests.failed} failed · ${tests.errors} errors`}
179	        pill={testsOk ? 'Passed' : 'Failed'}
180	        index={checks.length}
181	        open={!!open.tests}
182	        onToggle={() => toggle('tests')}
183	      >
184	        <CodeBlock
185	          code={tests.output || '(no output)'}
186	          filename="python -m pytest -q"
187	          language="log"
188	          maxHeight={240}
189	        />
190	      </Capsule>
191	
192	      <p className="px-1 text-[11px] text-ink-3">
193	        score = 60 × tests_pass + 40 × weighted recovery checks (services/sandbox-env/GRADING.md)
194	      </p>
195	    </div>
196	  )
197	}

128	                    {f.nth > 1 ? ` (attempt ${f.nth})` : ''}.
129	                  </TooltipContent>
130	                </Tooltip>
131	              ))}
132	              {kinds.length === 0 && real.length === 0 ? (
133	                <span className="text-[12px] text-muted-foreground">none listed</span>
134	              ) : null}
135	            </div>
136	          )
137	        },
138	      }),
139	      columnHelper.display({
140	        id: 'run',
141	        header: () => <span className="whitespace-nowrap">Live run</span>,
142	        cell: ({ row }) => {
143	          const s = row.original
144	          const busy = starting === s.id
145	          return (
146	            <Button
147	              size="sm"
148	              className="w-full justify-center"
149	              onClick={(e) => {
150	                e.stopPropagation()
151	                onRun(s)
152	              }}
153	              disabled={!canRun || busy}
154	              title={canRun ? `Start a live run of ${s.id}` : 'Harness unreachable'}
155	            >
156	              {busy ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" aria-hidden /> : <Play aria-hidden />}
157	              {busy ? 'Starting…' : 'Run'}
158	            </Button>
159	          )
160	        },
161	      }),
162	      columnHelper.display({
163	        id: 'replay',
164	        header: 'Replay',
165	        cell: ({ row }) => {
166	          const s = row.original
167	          const demo = demoFor(s.id)
168	          return demo ? (
169	            <Button
170	              size="sm"
171	              variant="outline"
172	              className="w-full justify-center"
173	              onClick={(e) => {
174	                e.stopPropagation()
175	                onReplay(demo.id)
176	              }}
177	              title={`Replay a recorded ${s.id} run (no backend needed)`}
178	            >
179	              <Rewind aria-hidden /> Replay
180	            </Button>
181	          ) : (
182	            <Tooltip>
183	              <TooltipTrigger
184	                render={
185	                  <span className="inline-flex w-full">
186	                    <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="w-full justify-center" disabled>
187	                      <Rewind aria-hidden /> Replay
188	                    </Button>
189	                  </span>
190	                }
191	              />
192	              <TooltipContent>No recording bundled for this scenario yet.</TooltipContent>
193	            </Tooltip>
194	          )
195	        },
196	      }),
197	    ],
198	    [columnHelper, starting, canRun, onRun, onReplay],
199	  )
200	
201	  const table = useTable<typeof features, Scenario>({
202	    features,
203	    columns,
204	    data: scenarios,
205	    getRowId: (row) => row.id,
206	  })
207	
208	  return (
209	    <section aria-label="Scenarios" className="w-full">
210	      <div className="mb-2 flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
211	        <h2 className="text-sm font-medium">Scenarios</h2>
212	        <span className="text-[12px] text-muted-foreground">
213	          {canRun
214	            ? 'Run starts a live episode on the harness · Replay plays a recorded run in the browser'
215	            : 'live runs need the harness; replays still work'}
216	        </span>
217	      </div>
218	      <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-border bg-card/30">
219	        <Table className="w-full min-w-[900px] table-fixed">
220	          <TableHeader>
221	            {table.getHeaderGroups().map((hg) => (
222	              <TableRow key={hg.id} className="hover:bg-transparent">
223	                {hg.headers.map((h) => (
224	                  <TableHead
225	                    key={h.id}
226	                    style={{ width: COLUMN_WIDTH[h.column.id] }}
227	                    className={h.column.id === 'run' || h.column.id === 'replay' ? 'text-center' : undefined}
228	                  >
229	                    {h.isPlaceholder ? null : flexRender(h.column.columnDef.header, h.getContext())}
230	                  </TableHead>
231	                ))}
232	              </TableRow>
233	            ))}
234	          </TableHeader>
235	          <TableBody>
236	            {loading ? (
237	              [0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
238	                <TableRow key={`sk-${i}`}>
239	                  <TableCell><Skeleton className="h-5 w-40" /><Skeleton className="mt-2 h-3 w-20" /></TableCell>
240	                  <TableCell><Skeleton className="h-4 w-full" /><Skeleton className="mt-2 h-4 w-3/4" /></TableCell>
241	                  <TableCell><Skeleton className="h-4 w-8" /></TableCell>
242	                  <TableCell><Skeleton className="h-5 w-24" /></TableCell>
243	                  <TableCell><Skeleton className="h-8 w-full" /></TableCell>
244	                  <TableCell><Skeleton className="h-8 w-full" /></TableCell>
245	                </TableRow>
246	              ))
247	            ) : table.getRowModel().rows.length === 0 ? (
248	              <TableRow>
249	                <TableCell colSpan={columns.length} className="py-8 text-center text-sm whitespace-normal text-muted-foreground">
250	                  {error ? `No scenarios from the harness (${error}).` : 'No scenarios.'} Replays in the sidebar still work.
251	                </TableCell>
252	              </TableRow>
253	            ) : (
254	              table.getRowModel().rows.map((row) => {
255	                const active = row.original.id === selectedId
256	                return (
257	                  <TableRow
258	                    key={row.id}
259	                    data-state={active ? 'selected' : undefined}
260	                    aria-selected={active}
261	                    tabIndex={0}
262	                    onClick={() => onSelect(row.original.id)}
263	                    onKeyDown={(e) => {
264	                      if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
265	                        e.preventDefault()
266	                        onSelect(row.original.id)
267	                      }
268	                    }}
269	                    className={`cursor-pointer align-top ${active ? 'bg-muted/40' : ''}`}
270	                  >
271	                    {row.getAllCells().map((cell) => (
272	                      <TableCell
273	                        key={cell.id}
274	                        className={
275	                          cell.column.id === 'run' || cell.column.id === 'replay'
276	                            ? 'py-3 align-middle whitespace-normal'
277	                            : 'py-3 whitespace-normal'
278	                        }
279	                      >

281	                      </TableCell>
282	                    ))}
283	                  </TableRow>
284	                )
285	              })
286	            )}
287	          </TableBody>
288	        </Table>
289	      </div>
290	    </section>

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:43:01.383Z
1	/**
2	 * Pure helpers shared by the tool-call renderers (ToolCallChips, ThinkingTrace): icon and label
3	 * per tool, the one-line summary of a call, which workspace diff a call produced, and the result
4	 * parts to show. No React components here (keeps fast refresh happy).
5	 */
6	
7	import { FileText, FolderTree, PenLine, Send, Terminal, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react'
8	
9	import { callTouches, normalisePath, type ToolCallView, type ToolResultView } from '@/lib/reducer'
10	import type { FileDiff } from '@/lib/types'
11	
12	export const TOOL_ICON: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
13	  run_command: Terminal,
14	  read_file: FileText,
15	  write_file: PenLine,
16	  list_dir: FolderTree,
17	  submit: Send,
18	}
19	
20	export const TOOL_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
21	  run_command: 'Run',
22	  read_file: 'Read',
23	  write_file: 'Write',
24	  list_dir: 'List',
25	  submit: 'Submit',
26	}
27	
28	export function toolLabel(tool: string): string {
29	  return TOOL_LABEL[tool] ?? tool
30	}
31	
32	/** One-line description of the call: the command, the path, or the raw input. */
33	export function summariseCall(call: ToolCallView): string {
34	  if (call.command) return call.command
35	  if (call.path) return call.path
36	  if (call.tool === 'submit' && typeof call.input.summary === 'string') return call.input.summary
37	  if (Object.keys(call.input).length === 0) return '(no input)'
38	  return JSON.stringify(call.input)
39	}
40	
41	/** The workspace diff this call produced, if it is mutating and a diff exists for its path. */
42	export function diffForCall(call: ToolCallView, diffs: readonly FileDiff[]): FileDiff | null {
43	  if (!call.mutating) return null
44	  return (
45	    diffs.find((d) => (call.path ? normalisePath(d.path) === call.path : callTouches(call, d.path))) ??
46	    null
47	  )
48	}
49	
50	export interface ResultPart {
51	  label: string
52	  body: string
53	  tone: 'error' | 'plain'
54	  /** Render as a numbered listing (file content) rather than a log block. */
55	  code?: { filename: string | null }
56	}
57	
58	/** Same parts the timeline card shows, in the same order. */
59	export function resultParts(result: ToolResultView, path: string | null): ResultPart[] {
60	  const parts: ResultPart[] = []
61	  if (result.errorText) parts.push({ label: result.errorCode ?? 'error', body: result.errorText, tone: 'error' })
62	  if (result.stdout) parts.push({ label: 'stdout', body: result.stdout, tone: 'plain' })
63	  if (result.stderr) parts.push({ label: 'stderr', body: result.stderr, tone: 'plain' })
64	  if (result.content !== null) {
65	    parts.push({ label: 'content', body: result.content, tone: 'plain', code: { filename: path } })
66	  }
67	  if (result.entries) {
68	    parts.push({
69	      label: 'entries',
70	      body: result.entries.map((e) => `${e.type === 'dir' ? 'd' : '-'} ${e.name}`).join('\n'),
71	      tone: 'plain',
72	    })
73	  }
74	  if (result.bytesWritten !== null) {
75	    parts.push({ label: 'write', body: `${result.bytesWritten} bytes written`, tone: 'plain' })
76	  }
77	  if (parts.length === 0 && result.output) parts.push({ label: 'output', body: result.output, tone: 'plain' })
78	  return parts
79	}

1	import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
2	import { useVirtualizer } from '@tanstack/react-virtual'
3	
4	import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
5	import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
6	import { subscribeLogs } from '@/lib/log'
7	import type { ViewState } from '@/lib/reducer'
8	import type { LogLevel, LogLine } from '@/lib/types'
9	
10	const LEVELS: (LogLevel | 'all')[] = ['all', 'debug', 'info', 'warn', 'error']
11	const RANK: Record<LogLevel, number> = { debug: 10, info: 20, warn: 30, error: 40 }
12	
13	const LVL_TONE: Record<LogLevel, string> = {
14	  debug: 'text-muted-foreground',
15	  info: 'text-sky-700 dark:text-sky-300',
16	  warn: 'text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300',
17	  error: 'text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300',
18	}
19	
20	const ROW_ESTIMATE_PX = 22
21	
22	function renderExtras(line: LogLine): string {
23	  const skip = new Set(['ts', 'svc', 'lvl', 'ev', 'msg', '_seq'])
24	  const parts: string[] = []
25	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(line)) {
26	    if (skip.has(k) || v === null || v === undefined) continue
27	    parts.push(`${k}=${typeof v === 'string' ? v : JSON.stringify(v)}`)
28	  }
29	  return parts.join(' ')
30	}
31	
32	/**
33	 * Unified log lines from the run's event stream (harness + sandbox-env) merged with the browser's
34	 * own log ring buffer. Rows are virtualized (@tanstack/react-virtual) so a long run's thousands of
35	 * lines stay cheap; each row is measured after render because lines wrap.
36	 */
37	export function LogsPanel({ state, live = false }: { state: ViewState; live?: boolean }) {
38	  const [level, setLevel] = useState<LogLevel | 'all'>('all')
39	  const [raw, setRaw] = useState(false)
40	  const [follow, setFollow] = useState(true)
41	  const [webLines, setWebLines] = useState<LogLine[]>([])
42	  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
43	
44	  useEffect(() => subscribeLogs(setWebLines), [])
45	
46	  const lines = useMemo(() => {
47	    const merged = [...state.logs, ...webLines].sort((a, b) =>
48	      String(a.ts ?? '').localeCompare(String(b.ts ?? '')),
49	    )
50	    if (level === 'all') return merged
51	    const min = RANK[level]
52	    return merged.filter((l) => (RANK[(l.lvl as LogLevel) ?? 'info'] ?? 20) >= min)
53	  }, [state.logs, webLines, level])
54	
55	  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({
56	    count: lines.length,
57	    getScrollElement: () => scrollRef.current,
58	    estimateSize: () => ROW_ESTIMATE_PX,
59	    overscan: 12,
60	    getItemKey: (i) => `${lines[i]?.ts ?? ''}-${i}`,
61	  })
62	
63	  // Follow the tail while live unless the reader scrolled up.
64	  useEffect(() => {
65	    const el = scrollRef.current
66	    if (!el) return
67	    const onScroll = () => {
68	      setFollow(el.scrollHeight - el.scrollTop - el.clientHeight < 48)
69	    }
70	    el.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true })
71	    return () => el.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll)
72	  }, [])
73	
74	  useEffect(() => {
75	    if (!follow || lines.length === 0) return
76	    virtualizer.scrollToIndex(lines.length - 1, { align: 'end' })
77	  }, [lines.length, follow, virtualizer, raw])
78	
79	  const items = virtualizer.getVirtualItems()
80	
81	  return (
82	    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col" aria-label="Logs">
83	      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-b border-border px-3 py-2">
84	        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">level</span>
85	        {LEVELS.map((l) => (
86	          <Button
87	            key={l}
88	            size="sm"
89	            variant={level === l ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
90	            className="h-6 px-2 font-mono text-[11px]"
91	            aria-pressed={level === l}
92	            onClick={() => setLevel(l)}
93	          >
94	            {l}
95	          </Button>
96	        ))}
97	        <span className="flex-1" />
98	        {live ? (
99	          <Button
100	            size="sm"
101	            variant={follow ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
102	            className="h-6 px-2 font-mono text-[11px]"
103	            aria-pressed={follow}
104	            onClick={() => {
105	              setFollow(true)
106	              if (lines.length) virtualizer.scrollToIndex(lines.length - 1, { align: 'end' })
107	            }}
108	            title="Keep the newest line in view"
109	          >
110	            follow
111	          </Button>
112	        ) : null}
113	        <Button
114	          size="sm"
115	          variant={raw ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
116	          className="h-6 px-2 font-mono text-[11px]"
117	          aria-pressed={raw}
118	          onClick={() => setRaw((v) => !v)}
119	        >
120	          raw json
121	        </Button>
122	        <Badge variant="outline" className="font-mono text-[11px]" title="lines after the level filter">
123	          {lines.length}
124	        </Badge>
125	      </div>
126	
127	      <div
128	        ref={scrollRef}
129	        className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto font-mono text-[11.5px] leading-[1.6]"
130	        role="log"
131	        aria-live={live ? 'polite' : 'off'}
132	      >
133	        {lines.length === 0 ? (
134	          <p className="p-4 text-muted-foreground">
135	            No log lines yet. The harness mirrors its unified JSON log into the event stream.
136	          </p>
137	        ) : (
138	          <div style={{ height: virtualizer.getTotalSize(), position: 'relative' }} className="w-full">
139	            {items.map((item) => {
140	              const l = lines[item.index]!
141	              return (
142	                <div
143	                  key={item.key}
144	                  ref={virtualizer.measureElement}
145	                  data-index={item.index}
146	                  className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full px-3 py-px"
147	                  style={{ transform: `translateY(${item.start}px)` }}
148	                >
149	                  {raw ? (
150	                    <div className="break-all whitespace-pre-wrap text-foreground/80">{JSON.stringify(l)}</div>
151	                  ) : (
152	                    <div className="grid grid-cols-[84px_40px_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-x-2">
153	                      <span className="truncate text-muted-foreground/70" title={typeof l.ts === 'string' ? l.ts : ''}>
154	                        {typeof l.ts === 'string' ? l.ts.slice(11, 23) : ''}
155	                      </span>
156	                      <span className={LVL_TONE[(l.lvl as LogLevel) ?? 'info']}>{l.lvl}</span>
157	                      <span className="min-w-0 [overflow-wrap:anywhere] whitespace-pre-wrap">
158	                        <span className="text-muted-foreground">{l.svc}</span>{' '}
159	                        <span className="text-foreground">{l.ev}</span>
160	                        {l.msg ? <span className="text-foreground/70"> {l.msg}</span> : null}
161	                        {(() => {
162	                          const extras = renderExtras(l)
163	                          return extras ? <span className="text-muted-foreground/70"> {extras}</span> : null
164	                        })()}
165	                      </span>
166	                    </div>
167	                  )}
168	                </div>
169	              )
170	            })}
171	          </div>
172	        )}
173	      </div>
174	    </div>
175	  )
176	}

1	import { TooltipProvider } from '@/components/ui/tooltip'
2	import { AppShell } from '@/components/layout/AppShell'
3	import { useConversations } from '@/hooks/useConversations'
4	import { useHarness } from '@/hooks/useHarness'
5	import { useIdentity } from '@/hooks/useIdentity'

7	import { ConversationPage } from '@/pages/ConversationPage'
8	import { HomePage } from '@/pages/HomePage'
9	import { ReplayPage } from '@/pages/ReplayPage'
10	import { RunPage } from '@/pages/RunPage'
11	
12	/**
13	 * Routes (path based, no router dependency — see src/lib/router.ts):
14	 *   /                        new run (composer + scenarios)
15	 *   /conversations/:id       a conversation loaded from the SQLite store via REST; ?run=<id> picks a run
16	 *   /runs/:id                a run outside a conversation (harness without the store)
17	 *   /replay/:demoId          bundled recorded run
18	 */
19	export default function App() {
20	  const route = useRoute()
21	  const identity = useIdentity()
22	  const harness = useHarness()
23	  const conversations = useConversations(harness.client)
24	
25	  let page: React.ReactNode
26	  switch (route.kind) {
27	    case 'conversation':
28	      page = (
29	        <ConversationPage
30	          key={route.id}
31	          harness={harness}
32	          conversations={conversations}
33	          conversationId={route.id}
34	          runId={route.runId ?? null}
35	        />
36	      )
37	      break
38	    case 'run':
39	      page = <RunPage key={route.runId} harness={harness} runId={route.runId} />
40	      break
41	    case 'replay':
42	      page = <ReplayPage key={route.demoId} harness={harness} demoId={route.demoId} />
43	      break
44	    default:
45	      page = <HomePage harness={harness} conversations={conversations} />
46	  }
47	
48	  return (
49	    <TooltipProvider>
50	      <AppShell harness={harness} conversations={conversations} route={route} identity={identity}>
51	        {page}
52	      </AppShell>
53	    </TooltipProvider>
54	  )
55	}

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:44:01.633Z
1	/** Breakpoint and open state for the workspace panel (RunLayout). */
2	
3	import { useCallback, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'
4	
5	const WIDE_QUERY = '(min-width: 1280px)'
6	
7	function subscribeWide(cb: () => void): () => void {
8	  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof window.matchMedia !== 'function') return () => undefined
9	  const mql = window.matchMedia(WIDE_QUERY)
10	  mql.addEventListener('change', cb)
11	  return () => mql.removeEventListener('change', cb)
12	}
13	
14	function isWide(): boolean {
15	  return typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.matchMedia === 'function'
16	    ? window.matchMedia(WIDE_QUERY).matches
17	    : true
18	}
19	
20	/** True at ≥1280 px, where the workspace is a side column rather than an overlay sheet. */
21	export function useIsWide(): boolean {
22	  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribeWide, isWide, () => true)
23	}
24	
25	/**
26	 * Workspace panel open state: open by default on wide screens (a side column), closed by default
27	 * on narrow ones (where it is an overlay sheet the reader must ask for). Follows the breakpoint on
28	 * resize so rotating a tablet does not leave a sheet covering the transcript: the user's choice is
29	 * kept per breakpoint, and a breakpoint change falls back to that breakpoint's default.
30	 */
31	export function useWorkspaceOpen(): [boolean, (open: boolean | ((v: boolean) => boolean)) => void] {
32	  const wide = useIsWide()
33	  const [choice, setChoice] = useState<{ wide: boolean; open: boolean } | null>(null)
34	  const open = choice && choice.wide === wide ? choice.open : wide
35	  const setOpen = useCallback(
36	    (next: boolean | ((v: boolean) => boolean)) =>
37	      setChoice((prev) => {
38	        const cur = prev && prev.wide === wide ? prev.open : wide
39	        return { wide, open: typeof next === 'function' ? next(cur) : next }
40	      }),
41	    [wide],
42	  )
43	  return [open, setOpen]
44	}

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:44:03.002Z
1	/**
2	 * One persisted conversation: GET /conversations/{id} → runs + messages.
3	 *
4	 * The harness answers 404 both for an unknown id and for an id that belongs to another browser
5	 * (no existence oracle), so the two are reported identically as "not found".
6	 *
7	 * State is the last *loaded* answer, keyed by (client, id, refresh epoch). `detail` / `error`
8	 * are only exposed while that key is current, so switching conversations never shows the
9	 * previous one's transcript while the next loads — and no setState runs synchronously in the
10	 * effect.
11	 */
12	
13	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
14	
15	import { HarnessClient, isNotFound } from '@/lib/api'
16	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
17	import type { ConversationDetail } from '@/lib/types'
18	
19	const log = getLogger('web')
20	
21	export const NOT_FOUND = 'not found'
22	
23	export interface ConversationState {
24	  detail: ConversationDetail | null
25	  loading: boolean
26	  /** `NOT_FOUND` for a 404, otherwise the transport / server error text. */
27	  error: string | null
28	  refresh: () => void
29	}
30	
31	interface Loaded {
32	  client: HarnessClient
33	  id: string
34	  epoch: number
35	  detail: ConversationDetail | null
36	  error: string | null
37	}
38	
39	export function useConversation(client: HarnessClient | null, id: string | null): ConversationState {
40	  const [epoch, setEpoch] = useState(0)
41	  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState<Loaded | null>(null)
42	
43	  useEffect(() => {
44	    if (!client || !id) return
45	    let cancelled = false
46	
47	    client
48	      .getConversation(id)
49	      .then((value) => {
50	        if (cancelled) return
51	        setLoaded({ client, id, epoch, detail: value, error: null })
52	        log.info('conversation.loaded', 'GET /conversations/{id}', {
53	          conversation_id: id,
54	          runs: value.runs?.length ?? 0,
55	          messages: value.messages?.length ?? 0,
56	        })
57	      })
58	      .catch((err: unknown) => {
59	        if (cancelled) return
60	        if (isNotFound(err)) {
61	          setLoaded({ client, id, epoch, detail: null, error: NOT_FOUND })
62	          log.warn('conversation.not_found', 'conversation is 404 for this identity', { conversation_id: id })
63	          return
64	        }
65	        setLoaded({ client, id, epoch, detail: null, error: String(err) })
66	        log.warn('conversation.error', 'GET /conversations/{id} failed', { conversation_id: id, error: String(err) })
67	      })
68	
69	    return () => {
70	      cancelled = true
71	    }
72	  }, [client, id, epoch])
73	
74	  const refresh = useCallback(() => setEpoch((n) => n + 1), [])
75	
76	  return useMemo(() => {
77	    const wanted = !!client && !!id
78	    const current =
79	      wanted && !!loaded && loaded.client === client && loaded.id === id && loaded.epoch === epoch
80	    return {
81	      detail: current ? loaded.detail : null,
82	      loading: wanted && !current,
83	      error: current ? loaded.error : null,
84	      refresh,
85	    }
86	  }, [client, id, loaded, epoch, refresh])
87	}

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:44:07.625Z
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Static file server for the built Faultline SPA.
3	
4	`python -m http.server` is not enough here: it has no SPA fallback (a refresh on `/?run=…` is
5	fine, but any future path route would 404), no cache-control, and its log format is not the
6	unified JSON we use everywhere else. This is ~150 lines and does exactly what the deployment
7	needs and nothing more.
8	
9	Behaviour:
10	  * serves `--root` (default /site)
11	  * SPA fallback: an *extension-less* path that does not exist is answered with index.html (200),
12	    so the app boots and reads the route itself; a missing `/assets/x.js` still 404s honestly
13	  * `index.html` and `config.json` are `no-store` — the point of config.json is that redeploying
14	    the harness changes it without rebuilding the frontend, so it must never be cached
15	  * hashed build output under `/assets/` is immutable for a year
16	  * one unified JSON log line per request on stdout, `svc:"web-server"`, flushed (stdout is block
17	    buffered when it is not a TTY, which it never is inside a container)
18	
19	Run standalone:  python serve.py --root dist --port 8000 --harness-url https://…
20	"""
21	
22	from __future__ import annotations
23	
24	import argparse
25	import json
26	import mimetypes
27	import os
28	import posixpath
29	import sys
30	import time
31	from datetime import datetime, timezone
32	from functools import partial
33	from http.server import SimpleHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
34	from pathlib import Path
35	from urllib.parse import unquote, urlsplit
36	
37	SVC = "web-server"
38	
39	# Content types the stdlib can get wrong (or miss) depending on the base image.
40	_EXTRA_TYPES = {
41	    ".js": "text/javascript; charset=utf-8",
42	    ".mjs": "text/javascript; charset=utf-8",
43	    ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
44	    ".json": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
45	    ".map": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
46	    ".svg": "image/svg+xml",
47	    ".woff": "font/woff",
48	    ".woff2": "font/woff2",
49	    ".ttf": "font/ttf",
50	    ".wasm": "application/wasm",
51	    ".webmanifest": "application/manifest+json",
52	    ".md": "text/markdown; charset=utf-8",
53	}
54	for _ext, _ct in _EXTRA_TYPES.items():
55	    mimetypes.add_type(_ct.split(";")[0], _ext)
56	
57	IMMUTABLE_PREFIXES = ("/assets/",)
58	NEVER_CACHE_NAMES = {"index.html", "config.json"}
59	
60	
61	def _now() -> str:
62	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
63	
64	
65	def log(ev: str, msg: str = "", lvl: str = "info", **extras: object) -> None:
66	    """One unified JSON line, same field names as faultline_common.log."""
67	    rec: dict[str, object] = {"ts": _now(), "svc": SVC, "lvl": lvl, "ev": ev, "msg": msg}
68	    for key, value in extras.items():
69	        if value is not None:
70	            rec[key] = value
71	    sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(rec, default=str, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
72	    sys.stdout.flush()
73	
74	
75	class SpaHandler(SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):
76	    """SimpleHTTPRequestHandler + SPA fallback + cache headers + unified logging."""
77	
78	    server_version = "faultline-web"
79	    sys_version = ""
80	    protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
81	
82	    def __init__(self, *args: object, root: str = "/site", **kwargs: object) -> None:
83	        self._root = Path(root).resolve()
84	        self._fellback = False
85	        self._t0 = time.perf_counter()
86	        super().__init__(*args, directory=str(self._root), **kwargs)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
87	
88	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ routing
89	
90	    def translate_path(self, path: str) -> str:
91	        full = Path(super().translate_path(path))
92	        if full.is_dir():
93	            index = full / "index.html"
94	            if index.is_file():
95	                return str(index)
96	        if full.exists():
97	            return str(full)
98	
99	        # SPA fallback, but only for something that looks like a route rather than an asset:
100	        # a missing hashed bundle must 404 so a broken deploy is visible instead of silently
101	        # serving HTML with a `text/javascript` expectation.
102	        name = posixpath.basename(unquote(urlsplit(self.path).path))
103	        if "." not in name:
104	            index = self._root / "index.html"
105	            if index.is_file():
106	                self._fellback = True
107	                return str(index)
108	        return str(full)
109	
110	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ headers
111	
112	    def send_response_only(self, code: object, message: str | None = None) -> None:
113	        # Remember the status so end_headers() can decide whether this response is cacheable.
114	        try:
115	            self._status = int(code)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
116	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
117	            self._status = 0
118	        super().send_response_only(code, message)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
119	
120	    def end_headers(self) -> None:
121	        req_path = unquote(urlsplit(self.path).path)
122	        name = posixpath.basename(req_path)
123	        status = getattr(self, "_status", 200)
124	        if status >= 400:
125	            # NEVER cache an error. A 404 on a hashed `/assets/…` path is transient (it happens for
126	            # a few seconds during a redeploy, while a container still serves the previous image)
127	            # but the immutable one-year policy below would pin it in the browser cache forever and
128	            # leave that visitor staring at a blank page. Observed in the wild; do not "simplify".
129	            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
130	        elif self._fellback or name in NEVER_CACHE_NAMES or req_path in ("", "/"):
131	            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate")
132	        elif any(req_path.startswith(p) for p in IMMUTABLE_PREFIXES):
133	            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=31536000, immutable")
134	        else:
135	            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=300")
136	        self.send_header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
137	        self.send_header("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer")
138	        super().end_headers()
139	
140	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ logging
141	
142	    def handle_one_request(self) -> None:
143	        self._t0 = time.perf_counter()
144	        self._fellback = False
145	        super().handle_one_request()
146	
147	    def log_request(self, code: object = "-", size: object = "-") -> None:
148	        try:
149	            status = int(code)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
150	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
151	            status = 0
152	        log(
153	            "http.request",
154	            f"{self.command} {self.path}",
155	            lvl="info" if status < 400 else "warn",
156	            method=self.command,
157	            path=self.path,
158	            status=status,
159	            bytes=size if size != "-" else None,
160	            dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - self._t0) * 1000),
161	            spa_fallback=True if self._fellback else None,
162	        )
163	
164	    def log_error(self, fmt: str, *args: object) -> None:
165	        log("http.error", fmt % args if args else fmt, lvl="warn", path=self.path)
166	
167	    def log_message(self, fmt: str, *args: object) -> None:  # pragma: no cover - silenced
168	        return
169	
170	
171	def write_config(root: Path, harness_url: str) -> None:
172	    """Runtime config the SPA fetches before its first request to the harness."""
173	    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
174	    target = root / "config.json"
175	    payload = {"harnessUrl": harness_url, "generatedAt": _now()}
176	    target.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
177	    log("config.written", "wrote runtime config", path=str(target), harness_url=harness_url)
178	
179	
180	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
181	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Serve the built Faultline SPA.")
182	    parser.add_argument("--root", default=os.environ.get("SITE_ROOT", "/site"))
183	    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("PORT", "8000")))
184	    parser.add_argument("--host", default="0.0.0.0")
185	    parser.add_argument(
186	        "--harness-url",
187	        default=os.environ.get("HARNESS_URL"),
188	        help="written to <root>/config.json before serving; omit to leave any existing file alone",
189	    )
190	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
191	
192	    root = Path(args.root).resolve()
193	    if not (root / "index.html").is_file():
194	        log("startup.error", f"no index.html under {root}", lvl="error", root=str(root))
195	        return 2
196	
197	    if args.harness_url:
198	        write_config(root, args.harness_url)
199	
200	    handler = partial(SpaHandler, root=str(root))
201	    httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer((args.host, args.port), handler)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
202	    httpd.daemon_threads = True
203	    log(
204	        "startup.ok",
205	        "serving",
206	        root=str(root),
207	        port=args.port,
208	        files=sum(1 for _ in root.rglob("*") if _.is_file()),
209	        harness_url=args.harness_url,
210	    )
211	    try:
212	        httpd.serve_forever()
213	    except KeyboardInterrupt:  # pragma: no cover
214	        log("shutdown", "interrupted")
215	    finally:
216	        httpd.server_close()
217	    return 0
218	
219	
220	if __name__ == "__main__":
221	    raise SystemExit(main())
222

1	"""Modal app `faultline-web` — hosts the built SPA as a static site.
2	
3	    modal deploy -e local apps/web/modal_app.py
4	    HARNESS_URL=https://…harness-api.modal.run modal deploy -e local apps/web/modal_app.py
5	
6	URL shape (workspace `appliedlabsai`, environment `local`, Server named `site`):
7	
8	    https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.modal.run
9	
10	Two build paths, because the fast one is fast and the slow one is the one that works on a clean
11	checkout:
12	
13	  * **prebuilt** (default when `apps/web/dist/index.html` exists, or `FAULTLINE_WEB_PREBUILT=1`):
14	    `pnpm build` already ran locally, so the image just copies `dist/` to `/site`. No node in the
15	    image, build takes seconds.
16	  * **in-image**: no local `dist/`, so the image installs node 22 + pnpm and runs
17	    `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile && pnpm build` itself. Slower and it re-builds the image on
18	    every source change, but it needs nothing on the developer's machine.
19	    Force it with `FAULTLINE_WEB_PREBUILT=0`.
20	
21	  `add_local_dir(copy=False)` — the default — mounts at *container start*, i.e. after the image is
22	  built, so a `run_commands("pnpm build")` would see an empty /src. Both paths therefore use
23	  `copy=True`.
24	
25	The harness URL is **not** baked into the bundle. `/site/config.json` is written at container
26	start from `$HARNESS_URL` (see `serve.py`), and the SPA prefers it over the build-time
27	`VITE_HARNESS_URL`. Redeploying the harness is then `modal deploy` with a different env var, not a
28	frontend rebuild.

59	SERVER_NAME = "site"

61	SITE_ROOT = "/site"

167	            [
168	                sys.executable,

178	            stderr=None,
179	        )
180	
181	    @modal.exit()
182	    def stop(self) -> None:
183	        proc = getattr(self, "proc", None)
184	        if proc is None:
185	            return
186	        log.info("server.stop", "terminating static server", pid=proc.pid)
187	        proc.terminate()
188	        try:
189	            proc.wait(timeout=5)
190	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:  # pragma: no cover
191	            proc.kill()

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:44:28.575Z
1	# apps/web — Faultline browser UI
2	
3	A chat-style viewer for Faultline runs: a Claude agent executes real shell commands in an isolated
4	Sandbox while the environment injects faults at the tool boundary. The UI shows every model turn,
5	every tool call with its output, the injected fault and whether the agent recovered, the workspace
6	diff, and the graded score — grouped into **conversations that belong to this browser**.
7	
8	Static SPA served by a small static server (`serve.py`); talks to the harness API over
9	CORS. See `../../ARCHITECTURE.md` for the system design and `../../PLAN.md` §2.9–§2.10, §3.3 for
10	the plan.
11	
12	## Stack
13	
14	- Vite 8 · React 19 · TypeScript 6 · Tailwind v4 (`@tailwindcss/vite`)
15	- shadcn (v4 CLI, style `base-nova` on Base UI) — primitives + the **sidebar block** (collapsible to icons)
16	- [Beautiful UI](https://www.beautifului.dev) (MIT, © 2026 Shane Levine) — AI-native composites,
17	  copied and adapted into `src/components/bui/` (see its README/LICENSE there)
18	- vitest + Testing Library (jsdom)
19	
20	**Node ≥ 22.12 is required** (Vite 8 / vitest 5). `.nvmrc` pins 22; the lockfile is pnpm v9
21	(`packageManager: pnpm@9.15.4`).
22	
23	```bash
24	source ~/.nvm/nvm.sh && nvm use        # Node 22 from .nvmrc
25	corepack pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
26	pnpm dev                               # http://localhost:5173, harness URL from public/config.json
27	pnpm test                              # vitest run
28	pnpm build                             # tsc -b && vite build → dist/
29	```
30	
31	## Routes (path based, `src/lib/router.ts`, no router dependency)
32	
33	| Path | What |
34	|---|---|
35	| `/` | new run: composer (`/` picks a scenario, model + seed chips) and the scenario table (Run / Replay per row) |
36	| `/conversations/:id` | a conversation loaded from the SQLite store via `GET /conversations/{id}`; `?run=<id>` selects a run |
37	| `/runs/:id` | a run addressed by id alone; upgrades itself to its conversation when the record carries `conversation_id`; a 404 (unknown id, or another browser's run) is shown as "Run not found" |
38	| `/replay/:demoId` | bundled recorded run from `public/demo/*.json` with a scrubber (`hooks/useReplay.ts`, `components/replay/`), no backend |
39	
40	`serve.py` answers extension-less paths with `index.html`, so deep links work when hosted.
41	
42	## Data flow
43	
44	- **Identity** — `src/lib/identity.ts` mints `u_<uuid4>` once per browser (localStorage, cookie
45	  mirror) and every request carries `X-Faultline-User`. Conversations are scoped to it. Scoping,
46	  not auth.
47	- **Conversations** — `GET /conversations` fills the sidebar; `GET /conversations/{id}` returns
48	  `{conversation, runs, messages}`; finished runs render from the persisted `messages/blocks`
49	  projection (the top-bar chip says `sqlite`) with the grader's evaluation and the run error
50	  overlaid from `GET /runs/{id}`; a finished run with no evaluation reads "Not graded", never as a
51	  pass. A 404 on any of these routes is an error to display, not a fallback path.
52	- **Live runs** — seed from `GET /runs/{id}`, then tail `GET /runs/{id}/events` (SSE, resumes with
53	  `Last-Event-ID`, ~110 s rotations, polling fallback). `src/lib/reducer.ts` folds events into the
54	  view state; `src/lib/transcript.ts` maps either source onto one transcript model.
55	- **Config** — harness URL precedence: `window.__FAULTLINE_CONFIG__` → `/config.json` (written by
56	  `serve.py` from `$HARNESS_URL` at container start) → `VITE_HARNESS_URL` → built-in default.
57	
58	## Layout
59	
60	```
61	src/
62	  App.tsx                       route switch inside the sidebar shell
63	  components/app-sidebar.tsx    conversations (grouped by day) · replays · identity menu
64	  components/layout/            AppShell (SidebarProvider), TopBar (breadcrumb + status chips), Link
65	  components/transcript/        TranscriptView: task bubble → turns (AssistantText, ThinkingTrace, ToolCallChips) → ScoreRows
66	  components/workspace/         Files / Diffs / Logs column (resizable; sheet on narrow screens)
67	  components/replay/            StoryBar, ReplayScrubber (replay page)
68	  components/bui/               Beautiful UI ports (AssistantText, ThinkingTrace, ToolCallChips, CodeBlock, ScoreRows, LoadingState, FileStatusTable, Composer)
69	  components/ui/                shadcn primitives + sidebar block
70	  pages/                        HomePage, ConversationPage, RunPage, ReplayPage
71	  hooks/                        useHarness, useIdentity, useConversations, useConversation, useRunView, useStartRun, useReplay, useWorkspaceLayout
72	  lib/                          api, config, identity, router, reducer, transcript, replay, story, callStatus, runStatus, codes, checks, theme, format, log, types
73	public/demo/                    bundled replays (export with ../../scripts/export_demo.py)
74	e2e/flows.spec.ts               Playwright product flows (run by .claude/skills/verify-faultline/helpers/verify_web.py)
75	modal_app.py · serve.py         hosting (prebuilt dist/ or in-image Node 22 build)
76	```
77	
78	## Deploy
79	
80	Deployment is driven from the repo root (`scripts/deploy.sh`; PLAN.md §5). The harness API URL is
81	injected at container start as `$HARNESS_URL` and written to `/config.json` by `serve.py`; nothing is
82	baked into the bundle. For a local dev server, set `VITE_HARNESS_URL` in `.env.development.local`.
83

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:44:29.266Z
1	# Bundled replays
2	
3	Each `*.json` in this directory is a **`RunRecord`** — exactly the shape
4	`GET /runs/{id}` returns (`faultline_common.schemas.RunRecord`, mirrored in
5	`apps/web/src/lib/types.ts`). The UI folds it through the same reducer a live
6	SSE run goes through, so a replay renders identically to the real thing.
7	
8	Why they exist: the demo must survive a cold harness, a missing provider key, or
9	a rate limit at review time. *Replay* on a scenario row (or in the sidebar) needs no
10	backend and no network beyond fetching this file.
11	
12	## Playback
13	
14	`src/hooks/useReplay.ts` folds the first *n* events through the reducer and advances *n* every
15	`DEMO_STEP_MS` (400 ms, `src/lib/replay.ts`); the scrubber moves *n* in both directions. Register a new
16	file by adding it to `DEMOS` in `src/lib/replay.ts` (ids match scenario ids, so the scenario table
17	offers the replay automatically):
18	
19	```ts
20	export const DEMOS: DemoMeta[] = [
21	  { id: 'lost-ack', label: 'lost-ack', file: '/demo/lost-ack.json' },
22	  { id: 'locked-file', label: 'locked-file', file: '/demo/locked-file.json' },
23	  { id: 'missing-config', label: 'missing-config', file: '/demo/missing-config.json' },
24	  { id: 'gauntlet', label: 'gauntlet', file: '/demo/gauntlet.json' },
25	]
26	```
27	
28	The `id` is also the `/replay/<id>` route, so `/replay/lost-ack` deep-links a replay.
29	
30	## Producing one from a real run
31	
32	`scripts/export_demo.py` (repo root) reads a run captured under
33	`runs/<ts>_<run_id>/run.json` and writes a demo file here:
34	
35	```bash
36	# after scripts/run_episode_cli.py has written runs/20260912T173000Z_r_abc/run.json
37	python3 scripts/export_demo.py runs/20260912T173000Z_r_abc
38	# -> apps/web/public/demo/lost-ack.json   (named after the run's scenario_id)
39	
40	python3 scripts/export_demo.py runs/20260912T173000Z_r_abc --out-name gauntlet --pretty
41	python3 scripts/export_demo.py runs/20260912T173000Z_r_abc --check   # validate only
42	```
43	
44	It validates the record (ids contiguous from 0, `run_id` consistent, required
45	fields present), strips nothing but obvious secrets (`anthropic_workspace` is
46	masked), and refuses to write a record with no `episode.evaluated` event unless
47	`--allow-unevaluated` is passed — an unfinished run makes a poor demo.
48	
49	## What is shipped today
50	
51	- **`lost-ack.json`** — a real capture: `claude-haiku-4-5` on the deployed stack
52	  (run `r_e9bc5c8c6739`, 2026-09-12, 48 events, score 100/100), exported with
53	  `scripts/export_demo.py` from `runs/20260912T215923Z_lost-ack_live/run.json`.
54	  The story: read `CHANGELOG.md` → write it → the ack is lost (`504`, `ack_lost`
55	  fault at step 4) → the agent **reads back instead of retrying** → the section is
56	  already there, so no duplicate write → bump `version.py` → `pytest` green →
57	  `submit` → graded 100/100.
58	
59	- **`locked-file.json`** (run `r_0c2184dd9727`), **`missing-config.json`**
60	  (`r_62416121aefd`) and **`gauntlet.json`** (`r_39c78b2b3a0b`) — real captures on
61	  the same stack, each graded 100 with its faults fired.
62	
63	`src/lib/demo.test.ts` checks every bundled replay on each `pnpm test` run
64	(contiguous ids, graded, at least one fault, no secrets), so a replay cannot rot
65	silently. All four predate the reliability fields (`outcome`, `error_class`,
66	`evaluation_status`); re-export once the backend emits them. There is no
67	`worker-crash` replay yet.

1	# `components/bui` — Beautiful UI ports
2	
3	Components adapted from [Beautiful UI](https://www.beautifului.dev) (MIT, © 2026 Shane Levine —
4	see [LICENSE](./LICENSE)) and rewired to this app's run-view types (`src/lib/reducer.ts`,
5	`src/lib/types.ts`). Look, motion and structure follow the originals; demo data, scripted timers
6	and the `glimm` / `@central-icons-react` / `atoms` dependencies were replaced.
7	
8	| File | Origin | Adaptations |
9	| --- | --- | --- |
10	| `AssistantText.tsx` | `StreamingText` | Text comes from `turn.text` events instead of a word-by-word demo. Keeps the prose style and the streaming cursor; the last line shimmers while `streaming`. Sources, action buttons and follow-ups removed. Newlines preserved; `` `code` `` and `**bold**` spans styled inline, no markdown library. |
11	| `ThinkingTrace.tsx` | `ThinkingState` ("Steps") | Header shimmers while `active`, settles when `done`. Rows are `ToolCallView`s (tool icon, label, command/path, spinner / check / cross, `FaultFiredBadge`, `RecoveredBadge` — the latter is the "read-back seen" hint). Optional `thinking` paragraph; `children` render inside the expanded body. Stage timers removed; `defaultOpen` (else open while active). |
12	| `ToolCallChips.tsx` | `ToolChips` | One row per call with the icon→chevron hover swap and the mono chip. Expansion is inline (grid-rows reveal) instead of a hover portal so it works in a scrolling column. Body shows input, every result part the timeline shows (error + code, stdout, stderr, content, entries, bytes written, truncation), `exit n` / ok / error / no ack / not executed pill (`lib/callStatus.ts`, structured fields only), duration, fault-origin + read-back badges, and the unified diff (`CodeBlock`) for a mutating call with a matching `FileDiff`. `+n −m` count on the row. |
13	| `CodeBlock.tsx` | `CodeBlock` | Takes raw `code` or unified `diff` text (parsed here: `---`/`+++` meta, `@@` hunks drive line numbers, `+`/`-` tinted with the original's left bar / hatch). Copy button guarded on `navigator.clipboard`. Keyword tint extended with Python. No `fetch`. |
14	| `ScoreRows.tsx` | `TaskRows` ("Capsules") | Score headline (score/100, tests passed/failed/errors, checks ok/total) then one capsule per `Check` (round pass/fail badge, id, weight, pill) expanding to the detail, plus a pytest capsule expanding to a `CodeBlock`. Running / retry sequence removed. |
15	| `LoadingState.tsx` | `LoadingState` ("Drive") | Pixel-grid loader, shimmer label, elapsed clock. Video "Surfer" variant dropped; `sublabel` added. |
16	| `FileStatusTable.tsx` | `DiffTable` | Card + table + dotted status pill kept. Rows are `FileEntry`s (status, path, size via `fmtBytes`, short sha), changed files first, added/deleted rows tinted. Toggle/apply flow removed; optional `onSelect` / `selected` make rows focusable. |
17	| `Composer.tsx` | `PromptBar` ("Rounded", `demo=false`) | `/` at the start opens the scenario list above the bar (arrow keys, Enter/Tab pick, Esc dismiss, sliding hover highlight); picking sets `scenarioId` and fills the prompt with `task_prompt`. Model chip is a shadcn `DropdownMenu` radio group; seed chip is a small numeric input; Enter sends, Shift+Enter newlines; `hint` under the bar. `@` sources, attachments, dictation and the shader/sound celebration removed. |
18	
19	## Tokens
20	
21	The originals use `ink`, `ink-2`, `ink-3`, `canvas`, `surface`, `line`, `line-strong` and
22	`accent*`. shadcn already owns `accent`, so the port uses `brand*`; both sets are bridged to the
23	shadcn palette in the `@theme inline` block appended to `src/index.css`. Semantic colours
24	(`green`/`red`/`orange`) map to the app's existing `emerald` / `rose` / `amber` usage.
25	
26	## Motion
27	
28	Keyframes and the `.bui-*` animation classes live at the end of `src/index.css` under
29	`@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)`; transitions use Tailwind's `motion-safe:`
30	variant. With reduced motion every animation is a no-op and the shimmer renders as static text.

1	---
2	name: verify-faultline
3	description: Drive the deployed Faultline backend (sandbox-env gym + MCP shell tools on Modal, agent-harness live episodes) the way the agent does, exercise the injected faults, read the actual workspace files back, cross-check the grader, AND drive the deployed web UI (apps/web) through a real Chrome with Playwright across the user product flows (landing, composer, live run → conversation, persisted transcript, replays, verdict strip, workspace, identity, theme, failure modes); keep evidence under runs/<ts>_verify/ and runs/<ts>_verify_web/. Use it after any change to services/*, packages/common, scenarios, graders or apps/web, before claiming a run or a flow "works", and before submission.
4	---
5	
6	# Verify Faultline (backend flow + browser flow)
7	
8	Faultline is three Modal apps in environment `local`. This skill drives all three:
9	`faultline-sandbox-env` (gym REST + MCP tools, one Modal Sandbox per episode) and
10	`faultline-harness` (model loop + run API) through `helpers/verify_backend.py`, and the browser UI
11	`faultline-web` through `helpers/verify_web.py` (Playwright in the installed Google Chrome, against
12	the deployed site). The backend sections come first; the browser flow is the section "Browser flow
13	(apps/web)" below and its recipe is `features/web-ui.md`.
14	
15	Proof standard: a score from `evaluate` is a *claim*. Every run here also reads the workspace
16	files back through MCP, saves them, and re-checks the grader's assertions from the file contents
17	and the ledger independently. A green score with contradicting file evidence is a FAIL.
18	
19	## Launch
20	
21	The services are deployed; nothing runs locally. To (re)deploy from a clean checkout:
22	
23	```bash
24	scripts/deploy.sh local        # sandbox-env → harness → web, prints URLs; needs Modal secret anthropic-secret
25	```
26	
27	Ready when both health checks answer (`ok: true`, `has_provider_key: false`):
28	
29	```bash
30	curl -s https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-sandbox-env-api.modal.run/health
31	curl -s https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run/health
32	```
33	
34	Cold start after a deploy is ~10 s for sandbox-env; the harness `api` keeps one warm container.
35	Wait for `/health` from the *new* version before trusting a run (a draining container can still
36	serve the previous code for a few seconds after `modal deploy` returns). Teardown of the deployment
37	is not part of verification; each run tears down only the episodes it created.
38	
39	Override targets with `SANDBOX_ENV_URL` / `HARNESS_URL` (env) or `--sandbox-url` / `--harness-url`.
40	
41	## Doctor
42	
43	Read-only, run first whenever anything looks off:
44	
45	```bash
46	services/sandbox-env/.venv/bin/python .claude/skills/verify-faultline/helpers/verify_backend.py --skip careful,careless,faults
47	```
48	
49	It records `doctor.*` checks: sandbox-env `ok` + no provider key + catalogue has
50	`lost-ack`/`locked-file`/`missing-config` with no `fault_plan`/`hidden_tests` leak; harness `ok`
51	+ no provider key + `detail.sandbox_env_reachable: true`. Any FAIL here means the instance is not
52	worth driving. Also useful: `modal app list -e local | grep faultline` (three apps `deployed`) and
53	`modal app logs faultline-sandbox-env -e local` for the unified JSON log lines.
54	
55	## Drive
56	
57	Single entry point (needs the sandbox-env venv, which has `httpx` + `fastmcp`; create it with
58	`cd services/sandbox-env && uv venv && uv pip install -e ../../packages/common -e .` if missing):
59	
60	```bash
61	# backend flow without a model call (≈45 s, three sandboxes, ~$0)
62	services/sandbox-env/.venv/bin/python .claude/skills/verify-faultline/helpers/verify_backend.py
63	
64	# plus one real Haiku episode through the harness (≈+60 s, one more sandbox, a few cents)
65	services/sandbox-env/.venv/bin/python .claude/skills/verify-faultline/helpers/verify_backend.py --live
66	```
67	
68	What it does, in order (each step is an MCP tool call with header `X-Faultline-Episode`, exactly
69	as the harness makes them; see `features/` for the per-feature recipes):
70	
71	1. `doctor` as above.
72	2. `careful` — `POST /episodes {scenario_id: lost-ack}` → `read_file CHANGELOG.md`, `read_file
73	   src/ratelimiter/version.py` → `write_file version.py` (ok) → `write_file CHANGELOG.md` with the
74	   0.2.0 section on top → expect `is_error` with `code: ETIMEDOUT` after ≥2.5 s → `read_file
75	   CHANGELOG.md` again → the content must equal what was sent (the write landed) with exactly one
76	   `## [0.2.0]` heading → `run_command "python -m pytest -q -o addopts="` exit 0 → `GET
77	   /episodes/{id}` shows both files `modified`, `ack_lost` in `faults_fired`, and a diff adding
78	   `+## [0.2.0]` → `POST /episodes/{id}/evaluate` must be 100 with all three checks ok, and the
79	   ledger must show the ack_lost on the `write_file` and a `read_file` as the next touch.
80	3. `careless` — same scenario, but `write_file … mode: append` → ETIMEDOUT → blind second append
81	   → read back shows **two** headings → `evaluate` must report `no_duplicate_entry: false`,
82	   `verified_before_rewrite: false`, hidden tests failing, score < 50, and ≥50 points below `careful`.
83	4. `faults` — `missing-config`: `list_dir .` hides `README.md` while the transient fault is live,
84	   first `read_file README.md` is `ENOENT`, second succeeds, listing shows it again, `ls -la
85	   README.md` exits 0 (the file never left disk), and the sticky `config/settings.json` is really
86	   gone for both `read_file` and `cat`. `locked-file`: two `write_file` attempts on
87	   `src/ratelimiter/limits.py` return `EACCES` with the sha unchanged, the third lands, pytest passes,
88	   `evaluate` has `write_eventually_succeeded` and `bounded_retries` ok.
89	5. `live` (`--live`) — `scripts/run_episode_cli.py --scenario lost-ack --out runs/<ts>_verify/live
90	   --quiet` (POST /runs → SSE tail with Last-Event-ID reconnect). From `run.json` it re-derives,
91	   without the grader: `fault.fired ack_lost`, the model's next call on `CHANGELOG.md` after the
92	   ETIMEDOUT result is a read, the content the model read back has exactly one 0.2.0 heading, no
93	   mutating call on the changelog after the lost ack, contiguous event ids, `run.finished` last.
94	6. `cleanup` and `survival` (below).
95	
96	Exit code 0 = every assertion passed; 1 otherwise. Never run the same episode's tools in
97	parallel (the ledger is read-modify-write per episode); separate episodes are fine, so concurrent
98	verification runs do not interfere.
99	
100	## Browser flow (apps/web)
101	
102	The user product flows, driven through the real page. The maintained list of flows is
103	`features/web-ui.md` (ids `web-landing` … `web-sidebar`); the executable form is
104	`apps/web/e2e/flows.spec.ts` (one Playwright test per flow, `F1`…`F11`). **Maintenance rule:** a change
105	to a flow in `apps/web` updates its entry in `features/web-ui.md` and its test in the same change;
106	this section does not list flows so it cannot go stale.
107	
108	```bash
109	# doctor only (HTTP probes of the deployed site, no browser, ≈5 s)
110	python3 .claude/skills/verify-faultline/helpers/verify_web.py --skip-flows
111	
112	# all browser flows except the live episode (≈2 min, no model call)
113	python3 .claude/skills/verify-faultline/helpers/verify_web.py
114	
115	# plus a real run started from the table and followed to the persisted transcript (≈+2–4 min, one Haiku episode)
116	python3 .claude/skills/verify-faultline/helpers/verify_web.py --live
117	
118	# against a local dev server instead of the deployment
119	python3 .claude/skills/verify-faultline/helpers/verify_web.py --base-url http://localhost:5173
120	```
121	
122	Preconditions: Google Chrome installed (Playwright `channel: "chrome"`; otherwise
123	`cd apps/web && pnpm exec playwright install chromium` and `PW_CHANNEL=chromium`), `apps/web/node_modules`
124	installed with pnpm 9.15.4 under Node 22 (the runner finds Node 22 via nvm if the shell default is
125	older), and for `--live` a healthy harness with its Store up. Ready when the doctor's `http.*` checks
126	pass; a lagging Server container after a deploy shows up as `http.bundle_matches_local` failing for
127	~30 s — wait, do not redeploy.
128	
129	Evidence goes to `runs/<UTC ts>_verify_web/`: `verification.json` (pass/fail per `http.*` and
130	`flows.<id>` check, overall), `playwright.json` (raw reporter), `screens/<flow>.png` (full-page
131	screenshot per flow), `artifacts/` (traces + screenshots of failures), `outputs/*.json` (HTTP probe
132	bodies, and `conversation.json` for `--live`), `manifest.json` (written last). Exit 0 only when every
133	check passes; a skipped live test is reported as `flows.F3_F4_live_conversation = skipped`, never as
134	a pass. Cleanup: the runner creates nothing on the backend except (with `--live`) one conversation
135	and one run under a throw-away browser identity, which it leaves as evidence.
136	
137	## Evidence
138	
139	Everything lands in `runs/<UTC ts>_verify/` (the `runs/` folder is gitignored and is the
140	project's evidence store — keep it out of git, never delete old runs):
141	
142	| Path | Contents |
143	|---|---|
144	| `commands.log` | one JSON line per HTTP call, MCP tool call, and subprocess command, in order, with status/`is_error`/duration |
145	| `outputs/*.json` | every raw response: reset, tools, each tool result, observe, evaluate, delete, health |
146	| `files/careful/`, `files/careless/`, `files/faults/`, `files/live/` | the workspace files as read back through MCP (before/after each fault), unified diffs from `observe`, pytest stdout |
147	| `live/` | `events.jsonl`, `run.json`, `evaluate.json`, `summary.txt` from the CLI (only with `--live`) |
148	| `scores.json` | episode scores, checks and test counts per stage |
149	| `verification.json` | pass/fail per assertion and per stage, the episode ids used, overall PASS/FAIL (written last) |
150	| `manifest.json` | sha256 of every evidence file, computed **after** cleanup |
151	
152	Proof standards: exercise the real path (MCP tools with the episode header, the public REST
153	routes, the CLI the harness ships) — never the Modal Dict, the sandbox filesystem API, or grader
154	internals; capture the action *and* the resulting state (the read-back file, the observe diff, the
155	ledger); verify side effects independently of the score; no mocks — the only stubs are the
156	scripted "agent" in `careful`/`careless`, which exist precisely to show the grader discriminating.
157	
158	## Cleanup
159	
160	The helper `DELETE`s every episode it created (each stage also deletes in a `finally`) and then
161	proves each is gone (`GET /episodes/{id}` → 404 or `done: true`), recording the result under
162	`cleanup.*`. It never kills sandboxes it did not create. If a run crashed hard and left a sandbox:
163	
164	```bash
165	modal run -e local services/sandbox-env/modal_app.py::reap   # terminates EVERY faultline sandbox — only when no other run is active
166	```
167	
168	Cleanup never touches `runs/`. The `survival` stage runs after cleanup and re-lists and hashes
169	the evidence (`manifest.json`); `survival.*` checks fail if `commands.log`, the careful evaluate
170	output, the read-back changelog, or `scores.json` are missing.
171	
172	## Helpers
173	
174	- `helpers/verify_web.py` (executable, stdlib only) — the browser flow: HTTP doctor of the deployed
175	  site, then `apps/web/e2e/flows.spec.ts` via Playwright. Flags: `--base-url`, `--live`, `--skip-flows`,
176	  `--grep F5`, `--out DIR`, `--channel chrome|chromium`.
177	- `helpers/verify_backend.py` (executable) — the whole backend drive above. Flags: `--live`,
178	  `--live-scenario lost-ack|locked-file|missing-config`, `--skip careful,careless,faults`, `--out DIR`,
179	  `--sandbox-url`, `--harness-url`.
180	- Reused repo scripts: `scripts/smoke_roundtrip.py --scenario <id> [--fault-proof]` (broader
181	  reset→tools→observe→evaluate→delete smoke with its own `summary.json`), `scripts/run_episode_cli.py`
182	  (live episode + SSE tail; evidence per run), `scripts/deploy.sh`.
183	
184	## Known gaps (tracked in PLAN.md, not claimed here)
185	
186	- `apps/web`: `F3/F4` (live run → persisted conversation) is only exercised with `--live`; the
187	  worker-crash scenario has no bundled replay yet; page-refresh mid-run (V8) is asserted only in the
188	  live test.
189	- `gauntlet` scenario has unit coverage and reset/observe on Modal but no scripted careful/careless pass.
190	- Harness persistence (`/conversations`, `X-Faultline-User`) is in flight; `verify_backend.py`
191	  only relies on `/health`, `/runs`, `/runs/{id}`, `/runs/{id}/events`.
192	- **Failure provenance (PLAN.md §2.11) is in flight and NOT yet verified**: `tool.result.data.outcome`
193	  / `error_class`, `fault.fired.origin`, run statuses `unevaluated` / `interrupted`, the
194	  `interruption` / `run.resumed` / `episode.sandbox` events, code `ESANDBOX`, and the `worker-crash`
195	  scenario (real harness kill + resume). Until `scripts/prove_interruptions.py` passes and this skill
196	  gains `features/interruptions.md` + a stage, treat those fields as absent.
