1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Drive the deployed Faultline web UI the way a reviewer does and keep independent evidence.
3	
4	    python3 .claude/skills/verify-faultline/helpers/verify_web.py [--live] [--skip-flows] [--grep F5] [--base-url URL]
5	
6	Stages (a failed check never stops evidence collection):
7	  doctor    HTTP probes of the site: root/no-store, config.json -> healthy harness, SPA fallback,
8	            404 assets never cached, served bundle == local build, every bundled replay valid
9	  flows     apps/web/e2e/flows.spec.ts through Playwright in the installed Google Chrome, one test
10	            per flow id in features/web-ui.md (F1..F11); --live also runs F3/F4 (one Haiku episode)
11	  survival  manifest.json (sha256 of every evidence file), then verification.json written LAST
12	
13	Evidence layout under runs/<UTC ts>_verify_web/ (parallel to verify_backend.py):
14	  commands.log        one JSON line per HTTP call and subprocess, in order
15	  outputs/            raw HTTP bodies; live.json / conversation.json / run.json for --live
16	  screens/            one full-page screenshot per flow
17	  artifacts/          Playwright traces + failure screenshots
18	  playwright.json     raw Playwright JSON reporter output;  playwright.stdout.txt the console
19	  scores.json         the live run's score/checks/tests (only with --live)
20	  verification.json   pass/fail per check, run_id/conversation_id of a browser-started run, overall
21	  manifest.json       sha256 of every evidence file (before verification.json is written)
22	
23	Stdlib only. Needs Node >= 22 (found via PATH or ~/.nvm) and apps/web/node_modules (pnpm 9.15.4).
24	"""
25	
26	from __future__ import annotations
27	
28	import argparse
29	import glob
30	import hashlib
31	import json
32	import os
33	import re
34	import shutil
35	import subprocess
36	import sys
37	import time
38	import urllib.error
39	import urllib.request
40	from datetime import datetime, timezone
41	from pathlib import Path
42	from typing import Any
43	
44	REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
45	WEB = REPO / "apps" / "web"
46	DEFAULT_BASE_URL = "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.us-east.modal.direct"
47	FLOW_IDS = ["F1", "F2", "F5", "F6", "F7", "F8", "F9", "F10", "F11", "F3", "F4", "F4b"]
48	
49	
50	def now() -> str:
51	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
52	
53	
54	class Evidence:
55	    def __init__(self, out: Path):
56	        self.out = out
57	        self.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
58	        for sub in ("outputs", "screens", "artifacts"):
59	            (self.out / sub).mkdir(exist_ok=True)
60	        self.log = (self.out / "commands.log").open("a", encoding="utf-8")
61	        self.results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
62	        self.meta: dict[str, Any] = {}
63	
64	    def cmd(self, kind: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
65	        self.log.write(json.dumps({"ts": now(), "kind": kind, **fields}, default=str) + "\n")
66	        self.log.flush()
67	
68	    def check(self, stage: str, name: str, ok: bool | None, detail: str = "") -> bool | None:
69	        status = "skipped" if ok is None else ("PASS" if ok else "FAIL")
70	        self.results.append({"stage": stage, "name": f"{stage}.{name}", "ok": ok, "status": status, "detail": detail, "ts": now()})
71	        print(f"  [{status:7}] {stage}.{name}{(' — ' + detail) if detail else ''}")
72	        return ok
73	
74	    def write_output(self, name: str, body: Any) -> None:
75	        p = self.out / "outputs" / name
76	        if isinstance(body, (bytes, bytearray)):
77	            p.write_bytes(body)
78	        elif isinstance(body, str):
79	            p.write_text(body)
80	        else:
81	            p.write_text(json.dumps(body, indent=2, default=str))
82	
83	
84	def http(ev: Evidence, method: str, url: str, headers: dict[str, str] | None = None, timeout: float = 60) -> tuple[int, dict[str, str], bytes]:
85	    req = urllib.request.Request(url, method=method, headers=headers or {})
86	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
87	    try:
88	        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
89	            body = resp.read()
90	            hdrs = {k.lower(): v for k, v in resp.headers.items()}
91	            status = resp.status
92	    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
93	        body = e.read()
94	        hdrs = {k.lower(): v for k, v in e.headers.items()}
95	        status = e.code
96	    except Exception as e:  # network failure
97	        ev.cmd("http", method=method, url=url, error=str(e), dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
98	        return 0, {}, b""
99	    ev.cmd("http", method=method, url=url, status=status, bytes=len(body), cache_control=hdrs.get("cache-control"), dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
100	    return status, hdrs, body
101	
102	
103	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- doctor
104	
105	def demo_ids() -> list[str]:
106	    src = (WEB / "src" / "lib" / "replay.ts").read_text()
107	    block = src[src.index("export const DEMOS"):]
108	    block = block[: block.index("\n]")]
109	    return re.findall(r"id:\s*'([a-z0-9-]+)'", block)
110	
111	
112	def local_bundle() -> str | None:
113	    js = sorted(glob.glob(str(WEB / "dist" / "assets" / "index-*.js")))
114	    return Path(js[-1]).name if js else None
115	
116	
117	def doctor(ev: Evidence, base: str) -> None:
118	    print("doctor")
119	    # A Vite dev server (localhost) has no serve.py: cache headers, the served bundle and the
120	    # missing-asset policy are deployment properties and are reported as skipped there.
121	    deployed = not re.match(r"https?://(localhost|127\.0\.0\.1)(:|/|$)", base)
122	    ev.meta["deployed_target"] = deployed
123	    status, hdrs, body = http(ev, "GET", f"{base}/")
124	    html = body.decode("utf-8", "replace")
125	    ev.write_output("index.html", html)
126	    ev.check("http", "root_200", status == 200, f"status {status}")
127	    ev.check("http", "root_no_store", ("no-store" in hdrs.get("cache-control", "")) if deployed else None, hdrs.get("cache-control", "(none)") if deployed else "dev server")
128	    ev.check("http", "root_has_theme_prepaint", "faultline.theme" in html, "inline theme script in index.html")
129	    served = re.search(r"index-[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.js", html)
130	    served_name = served.group(0) if served else None
131	    local = local_bundle()
132	    ev.meta["served_bundle"], ev.meta["local_bundle"] = served_name, local
133	    if deployed and local and served_name != local:
134	        # a Server container can lag a deploy by ~30 s; give it a chance before failing
135	        for _ in range(4):
136	            time.sleep(15)
137	            status, hdrs, body = http(ev, "GET", f"{base}/")
138	            m = re.search(r"index-[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.js", body.decode("utf-8", "replace"))
139	            served_name = m.group(0) if m else None
140	            if served_name == local:
141	                break
142	    ev.check("http", "bundle_matches_local", ((local is None) or served_name == local) if deployed else None, f"served {served_name} local {local or '(no local build)'}" if deployed else "dev server")
143	
144	    status, hdrs, body = http(ev, "GET", f"{base}/config.json")
145	    cfg: dict[str, Any] = {}
146	    try:
147	        cfg = json.loads(body)
148	    except Exception:
149	        pass
150	    ev.write_output("config.json", body)
151	    harness = str(cfg.get("harnessUrl", "")).rstrip("/")
152	    ev.meta["harness_url"] = harness
153	    ev.check("http", "config_json", status == 200 and harness.startswith("https://"), f"harnessUrl={harness or '(missing)'}")
154	    ev.check("http", "config_no_store", ("no-store" in hdrs.get("cache-control", "")) if deployed else None, hdrs.get("cache-control", "(none)") if deployed else "dev server")
155	    if harness:
156	        status, hdrs, body = http(ev, "GET", f"{harness}/health")
157	        ev.write_output("harness_health.json", body)
158	        try:
159	            h = json.loads(body)
160	        except Exception:
161	            h = {}
162	        ev.check("http", "harness_health_ok", status == 200 and h.get("ok") is True, f"status {status}")
163	        ev.check("http", "harness_no_provider_key", h.get("has_provider_key") is False, f"has_provider_key={h.get('has_provider_key')}")
164	        store = (h.get("detail") or {}).get("store") or {}
165	        ev.meta["harness_store"] = store
166	        ev.check("http", "harness_store_reported", bool(store), f"store={'ok' if store.get('ok') else 'absent/not ok'}")
167	
168	    status, hdrs, _ = http(ev, "GET", f"{base}/conversations/c_does_not_exist")
169	    ev.check("http", "spa_fallback_200", status == 200, f"status {status}")
170	    ev.check("http", "spa_fallback_no_store", ("no-store" in hdrs.get("cache-control", "")) if deployed else None, hdrs.get("cache-control", "(none)") if deployed else "dev server")
171	
172	    status, hdrs, _ = http(ev, "GET", f"{base}/assets/does-not-exist-{int(time.time())}.js")
173	    ev.check("http", "missing_asset_404", (status == 404) if deployed else None, f"status {status}" if deployed else "dev server")
174	    ev.check("http", "missing_asset_never_cached", ("no-store" in hdrs.get("cache-control", "") and "immutable" not in hdrs.get("cache-control", "")) if deployed else None, hdrs.get("cache-control", "(none)") if deployed else "dev server")
175	
176	    ids = demo_ids()
177	    ev.meta["demo_ids"] = ids
178	    ev.check("http", "demos_registered", len(ids) >= 4, f"DEMOS={ids}")
179	    for d in ids:
180	        status, _, body = http(ev, "GET", f"{base}/demo/{d}.json")
181	        ok = status == 200
182	        detail = f"status {status}"
183	        if ok:
184	            try:
185	                rec = json.loads(body)
186	                events = rec.get("events", [])
187	                contiguous = all(e.get("id") == i for i, e in enumerate(events))
188	                evaluated = bool(rec.get("evaluation")) and rec["evaluation"].get("score") is not None
189	                ok = bool(events) and contiguous and evaluated and rec.get("scenario_id") == d
190	                detail = f"{len(events)} events, contiguous={contiguous}, score={(rec.get('evaluation') or {}).get('score')}"
191	                ev.write_output(f"demo_{d}.summary.json", {"run_id": rec.get("run_id"), "status": rec.get("status"), "events": len(events), "score": (rec.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score")})
192	            except Exception as e:
193	                ok, detail = False, f"unparseable: {e}"
194	        ev.check("http", f"demo_{d}", ok, detail)
195	
196	
197	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- flows
198	
199	def node_bin_dir() -> Path | None:
200	    """Return a bin dir with node >= 22 (PATH first, then nvm), or None."""
201	    def major(node: str) -> int:
202	        try:
203	            v = subprocess.run([node, "-v"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10).stdout.strip()
204	            return int(v.lstrip("v").split(".")[0])
205	        except Exception:
206	            return 0
207	    on_path = shutil.which("node")
208	    if on_path and major(on_path) >= 22:
209	        return Path(on_path).parent
210	    for pat in ("v22.*", "v24.*", "v2[3-9].*"):
211	        for d in sorted(glob.glob(str(Path.home() / ".nvm" / "versions" / "node" / pat)), reverse=True):
212	            node = Path(d) / "bin" / "node"
213	            if node.exists() and major(str(node)) >= 22:
214	                return node.parent
215	    return None
216	
217	
218	def run_flows(ev: Evidence, base: str, live: bool, grep: str | None, channel: str) -> None:
219	    print("flows")
220	    bin_dir = node_bin_dir()
221	    if bin_dir is None:
222	        ev.check("flows", "node22_available", False, "no node >= 22 on PATH or under ~/.nvm")
223	        return
224	    env = dict(os.environ)
225	    env["PATH"] = f"{bin_dir}{os.pathsep}{env.get('PATH', '')}"
226	    pnpm = shutil.which("pnpm", path=env["PATH"]) or "pnpm"
227	    env.update({
228	        "FAULTLINE_WEB_URL": base,
229	        "FAULTLINE_LIVE": "1" if live else "0",
230	        "PW_JSON_OUT": str(ev.out / "playwright.json"),
231	        "PW_OUTPUT_DIR": str(ev.out / "artifacts"),
232	        "PW_SCREEN_DIR": str(ev.out / "screens"),
233	        "PW_OUTPUTS_DIR": str(ev.out / "outputs"),
234	        "PW_CHANNEL": channel,
235	        "CI": "1",
236	    })
237	    cmd = [pnpm, "exec", "playwright", "test"]  # reporters come from playwright.config.ts (list + json → PW_JSON_OUT)
238	    if grep:
239	        cmd += ["--grep", grep]
240	    ev.check("flows", "node22_available", True, f"{bin_dir} ({pnpm})")
241	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
242	    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=WEB, env=env, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20 * 60)
243	    (ev.out / "playwright.stdout.txt").write_text(proc.stdout + "\n--- stderr ---\n" + proc.stderr)
244	    ev.cmd("subprocess", cmd=cmd, cwd=str(WEB), rc=proc.returncode, dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
245	
246	    report_path = ev.out / "playwright.json"
247	    if not report_path.exists():
248	        ev.check("flows", "report_written", False, f"playwright exited {proc.returncode} without a JSON report; see playwright.stdout.txt")
249	        return
250	    report = json.loads(report_path.read_text())
251	    seen: dict[str, tuple[str, str]] = {}
252	
253	    def walk(suite: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
254	        for spec in suite.get("specs", []):
255	            title = spec.get("title", "")
256	            fid = title.split(" ")[0]
257	            # A result counts as ok when it matches the test's expectedStatus, so a test marked
258	            # `test.fail()` (a documented product gap) is ok while it fails and FAILS when it passes.
259	            outcomes: list[str] = []
260	            errors: list[str] = []
261	            expected_fail = False
262	            for t in spec.get("tests", []):
263	                exp = t.get("expectedStatus", "passed")
264	                expected_fail = expected_fail or exp == "failed"
265	                for r in t.get("results", []):
266	                    st = r.get("status")
267	                    if st == "skipped":
268	                        outcomes.append("skipped")
269	                    elif st == exp:
270	                        outcomes.append("passed")
271	                    else:
272	                        outcomes.append("failed")
273	                        msg = (r.get("error") or {}).get("message", "")[:300]
274	                        errors.append(msg or (f"expected {exp}, got {st}"))
275	            if not outcomes or all(o == "skipped" for o in outcomes):
276	                status = "skipped"
277	            elif any(o == "failed" for o in outcomes):
278	                status = "failed"
279	            else:
280	                status = "passed"
281	            detail = "; ".join(e for e in errors if e) or title
282	            if expected_fail and status == "passed":
283	                detail = "KNOWN GAP still present (test.fail marker) — " + title
284	            seen[fid] = (status, detail)
285	        for child in suite.get("suites", []):
286	            walk(child)
287	
288	    for s in report.get("suites", []):
289	        walk(s)
290	    wanted = FLOW_IDS if not grep else [f for f in FLOW_IDS if any(re.fullmatch(alt.strip(), f) for alt in grep.split("|"))]
291	    for fid in wanted:
292	        name = fid.replace("/", "_")
293	        st, detail = seen.get(fid, ("missing", "no test with this id ran"))
294	        if st == "passed":
295	            ev.check("flows", name, True, detail if len(detail) < 120 else detail[:117] + "…")
296	        elif st == "skipped":
297	            ev.check("flows", name, None, "skipped (needs --live)" if fid in ("F3", "F4", "F4b") else "skipped")
298	        else:
299	            ev.check("flows", name, False, detail)
300	
301	    # screenshots per flow
302	    shots = sorted(p.name for p in (ev.out / "screens").glob("*.png"))
303	    ev.meta["screens"] = shots
304	    ev.check("flows", "screens_captured", len(shots) > 0, f"{len(shots)} screenshots")
305	
306	    if live:
307	        live_json = ev.out / "outputs" / "live.json"
308	        if live_json.exists():
309	            info = json.loads(live_json.read_text())
310	            ev.meta["run_id"], ev.meta["conversation_id"], ev.meta["user_id"] = info.get("run_id"), info.get("conversation_id"), info.get("user_id")
311	            harness = info.get("harness_url") or ev.meta.get("harness_url")
312	            if harness and info.get("run_id"):
313	                status, _, body = http(ev, "GET", f"{harness}/runs/{info['run_id']}", headers={"X-Faultline-User": info.get("user_id") or ""})
314	                ev.write_output("run.json", body)
315	                try:
316	                    rec = json.loads(body)
317	                except Exception:
318	                    rec = {}
319	                evaluation = rec.get("evaluation") or {}
320	                (ev.out / "scores.json").write_text(json.dumps({"live": {
321	                    "run_id": info["run_id"], "conversation_id": info.get("conversation_id"), "status": rec.get("status"),
322	                    "score": evaluation.get("score"), "checks": evaluation.get("checks"), "tests": evaluation.get("tests"),
323	                    "usage": rec.get("usage"), "events": len(rec.get("events", [])),
324	                }}, indent=2))
325	                ev.check("flows", "live_run_recorded_by_harness", status == 200 and rec.get("run_id") == info["run_id"], f"GET /runs/{info['run_id']} → {status}, status={rec.get('status')}, score={evaluation.get('score')}")
326	                ev.check("flows", "live_run_events_contiguous", bool(rec.get("events")) and all(e.get("id") == i for i, e in enumerate(rec.get("events", []))), f"{len(rec.get('events', []))} events")
327	        else:
328	            ev.check("flows", "live_ids_written", False, "outputs/live.json missing (F3/F4 did not get past run start)")
329	
330	
331	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- survival
332	
333	def survival(ev: Evidence) -> None:
334	    manifest: dict[str, str] = {}
335	    for p in sorted(ev.out.rglob("*")):
336	        if p.is_file() and p.name not in ("manifest.json", "verification.json"):
337	            manifest[str(p.relative_to(ev.out))] = hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
338	    (ev.out / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2))
339	    ev.check("survival", "commands_log", (ev.out / "commands.log").exists() and (ev.out / "commands.log").stat().st_size > 0)
340	    ev.check("survival", "manifest", len(manifest) > 0, f"{len(manifest)} files hashed")
341	
342	
343	def main() -> int:
344	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
345	    ap.add_argument("--base-url", default=os.environ.get("FAULTLINE_WEB_URL", DEFAULT_BASE_URL))
346	    ap.add_argument("--live", action="store_true", help="also run F3/F4: start a real run from the table (one Haiku episode)")
347	    ap.add_argument("--skip-flows", action="store_true", help="HTTP doctor only, no browser")
348	    ap.add_argument("--grep", default=None, help="only flows whose title matches, e.g. 'F5|F6'")
349	    ap.add_argument("--channel", default=os.environ.get("PW_CHANNEL", "chrome"), help="Playwright browser channel (chrome | chromium)")
350	    ap.add_argument("--out", default=None, help="evidence dir (default runs/<ts>_verify_web)")
351	    a = ap.parse_args()
352	    base = a.base_url.rstrip("/")
353	    out = Path(a.out) if a.out else REPO / "runs" / f"{datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')}_verify_web"
354	    ev = Evidence(out)
355	    print(f"evidence → {out}")
356	    ev.meta.update({"base_url": base, "live": a.live, "channel": a.channel, "started_at": now()})
357	
358	    doctor(ev, base)
359	    if not a.skip_flows:
360	        run_flows(ev, base, a.live, a.grep, a.channel)
361	    survival(ev)
362	
363	    failed = [r for r in ev.results if r["ok"] is False]
364	    skipped = [r for r in ev.results if r["ok"] is None]
365	    passed = [r for r in ev.results if r["ok"] is True]
366	    overall = "PASS" if not failed else "FAIL"
367	    summary = {"passed": len(passed), "failed": len(failed), "skipped": len(skipped), "failed_names": [r["name"] for r in failed], "skipped_names": [r["name"] for r in skipped]}
368	    (ev.out / "verification.json").write_text(json.dumps({
369	        "ts": now(), "overall": overall, "summary": summary, "results": ev.results,
370	        "run_id": ev.meta.get("run_id"), "conversation_id": ev.meta.get("conversation_id"), "user_id": ev.meta.get("user_id"),
371	        "base_url": base, "harness_url": ev.meta.get("harness_url"), "served_bundle": ev.meta.get("served_bundle"), "local_bundle": ev.meta.get("local_bundle"),
372	        "demo_ids": ev.meta.get("demo_ids"), "screens": ev.meta.get("screens"), "harness_store": ev.meta.get("harness_store"),
373	    }, indent=2))
374	    print(f"{overall}: {summary['passed']} passed, {summary['failed']} failed, {summary['skipped']} skipped → {out}")
375	    return 0 if overall == "PASS" else 1
376	
377	
378	if __name__ == "__main__":
379	    sys.exit(main())

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:45:14.866Z
297	### 5.4 Reliability event contract (confirmed with the harness lead 2026-09-12 ~22:45 UTC)
298	
299	**Status (2026-09-12 19:40 EDT): contract only.** Every field below is defined in `schemas.py` and the
300	docs, but no service emits it yet: sandbox-env still answers a lost sandbox with `EINTERNAL` and has no
301	`/interruptions` route, and the harness has no crash/resume path. The lead is implementing it.
302	
303	Canonical: `packages/common/faultline_common/schemas.py` (the `Event` docstring lists every data
304	shape), labels in `docs/error-taxonomy.md`, injector behaviour in `services/sandbox-env/FAULTS.md`.
305	The UI is a ten-second reliability read — *task → fault (simulated or real, which layer) → did the
306	command execute (executed / failed / not executed / unknown are four different answers) → recovery
307	(agent actions vs harness retries; did the sandbox survive) → diff + independent checks* — and it
308	**never derives an outcome from error text**: anything absent renders as "unknown" with the raw
309	payload expandable. Fields (all additive):
310	
311	| Where | Field | Meaning |
312	|---|---|---|
313	| `tool.result.data` | `outcome: executed \| failed \| not_executed \| unknown` | executed = ran and succeeded; failed = ran and reported failure; not_executed = refused/short-circuited before the sandbox saw it; unknown = may or may not have run (ack lost, transport, worker interrupted) |
314	| `tool.result.data` | `error_class` (iff `is_error`): `{origin: injected\|staged\|real, layer: boundary\|filesystem\|sandbox\|transport\|harness\|model\|gym, code, kind?, label, outcome_known, side_effect_applied?, detail?}` | provenance and layer; `label` is a fixed string rendered verbatim; `error_code` kept for compatibility; raw text stays in `output` |
315	| `tool.result.data` | `attempts`, `sandbox: {id, alive}` | harness-level retries of this call (1 = none); identity/liveness of the workspace that answered |
316	| `fault.fired.data` | `origin` (injected \| staged, never real), `layer` (boundary for injected, filesystem for staged), `description` | one plain sentence per fault |
317	| `episode.reset.data` | `sandbox_id` (short), `attempt` | workspace identity; >1 when re-provisioned |
318	| `episode.sandbox` (new) | `{sandbox_id, status: alive\|terminated\|replaced, reason, step}` | the environment noticed the worker changed |
319	| `interruption` (new) | `Interruption {step?, layer, code, label, tool_use_id?, tool?, path?, outcome_known, planned, resumed, worker_generation, at, detail?}` | always real; `planned: true` when a scenario `harness_faults` entry triggered it |
320	| `run.resumed` (new) | `{worker_generation, resumed_from_event_id, dangling_tool_use_id?, resumed_at}` | a fresh worker picked the run up |
321	| `run.finished.data` | `evaluation_status: ok\|failed\|skipped`, `evaluation_error?`, `steps`, `score?` | a run that could not be graded is `unevaluated`, never "ok" |
322	| `RunStatus` | `+ unevaluated`, `+ interrupted` | "ok" now implies a score |
323	| `RunRecord` | `error_class?`, `interruptions[]`, `worker_generation` | why the run ended as it did |
324	| `GET /scenarios` | `checks: [{id, description, weight}]`, `faults_public: [{kind, origin, layer, description}]`, `harness_faults: [{kind: worker_crash\|transport_abort, tool, path, nth, after_ms}]` | intended outcome and fault classes known before the run; the agent never sees them |
325	| codes (final) | `ENOENT EACCES ETIMEDOUT EINVAL ESANDBOX ETRANSPORT EHARNESS EMODEL EGYM EINTERNAL ENOEPISODE` | `ETIMEDOUT` is ack-lost *or* a real client timeout — origin/layer tell them apart |
326	
327	Older records (e.g. `r_e9bc5c8c6739`) predate these fields; the UI resolves what it can from the
328	evaluate ledger (`outcome: ack_lost` ⇒ the write landed, `short_circuit` ⇒ nothing ran) and says
329	"unknown" otherwise.
330	
331	## 6. Identity
332	
333	- **Minting.** `src/lib/identity.ts`: read `localStorage["faultline.user_id"]`, else the `faultline_uid`
334	  cookie, else mint `"u_" + crypto.randomUUID()`; write back to both. Cookie attributes:
335	  `SameSite=Lax; Secure; Max-Age=31536000; Path=/`. Not `HttpOnly` (JS writes it). All storage access is
336	  wrapped in try/catch; a browser that blocks storage still works for the session with an in-memory id.
337	- **Transport.** The harness API lives on a different origin from the SPA, so cookies are never sent to
338	  it. The id travels as `X-Faultline-User` on every request; the harness CORS config allows that header.
339	- **Why both stores.** localStorage is the primary (no size/attribute quirks, not sent anywhere);
340	  the cookie only exists so the id survives a "clear site data → local storage" that leaves cookies.
341	- **Threat model.** This is scoping, not authentication (explicit non-goal). Ids are 122-bit random and
342	  never listed, so they are unguessable in practice, but anyone holding one can read that browser's
343	  transcripts. Nothing sensitive is stored: scenarios are bundled, the workspace is a fixture, and the
344	  provider key never leaves `run_episode`. There is no per-id rate limit on `POST …/runs` yet.
345	
346	## 7. Web application
347	
348	### 7.1 Stack
349	
350	Vite 8 + React 19 + TypeScript 6, Tailwind v4 (`@tailwindcss/vite`), shadcn v4 CLI (style `base-nova`
351	on Base UI, `neutral` base colour, CSS variables, the `cn` helper package, lucide icons) for primitives,
352	Beautiful UI for the AI-native composites, vitest (jsdom). Path routes with no router dependency
353	(`src/lib/router.ts`, §7.3). **Node ≥ 22.12 is required** (Vite 8
354	`^20.19 || >=22.12`, vitest 5 `^22.12 || ^24`; `.nvmrc` pins 22 and the Modal image installs Node 22).
355	Built into `dist/` (in the Modal image, or prebuilt via `FAULTLINE_WEB_PREBUILT`) and served by a Modal
356	Server running `serve.py`: SPA fallback for extension-less paths, `no-store` on `index.html` and
357	`config.json`, immutable `/assets/`, unified JSON request logs. `serve.py` writes `/config.json` from
358	`$HARNESS_URL` at container start; the SPA resolves the harness URL as injected
359	`window.__FAULTLINE_CONFIG__` → `/config.json` → `VITE_HARNESS_URL` → default, so the URL can change
360	without a rebuild.
361	
362	### 7.2 Beautiful UI: what it is and how it is integrated
363	
364	`https://www.beautifului.dev` is an MIT-licensed gallery (© 2026 Shane Levine) of 21 React + Tailwind
365	components "for AI-native interfaces". It is **not** an npm package and **not** a shadcn registry: each
366	component has a *Copy code* button, and the TSX source is embedded in the page. Integration is therefore
367	copy-paste into `src/components/bui/` with a LICENSE file, plus three adaptations that the source makes
368	necessary:
369	
370	| Adaptation | Reason |
371	|---|---|
372	| icons → `lucide-react` | `SidebarNav` imports twelve icons from `@central-icons-react/*`, a paid set |
373	| unpublished atoms → shadcn | components import `@/components/atoms/{Button,StreamText,Shimmer,ValuePill,EntityChip}` and `@/components/primitives/GlideMenu`, which the gallery does not publish; shadcn `Button`, `Skeleton`, `Badge`, `DropdownMenu` stand in |
374	| drop `glimm` | `PromptBar` imports a shader/sound helper; the branch is deleted |
375	| `accent*` classes → `brand*` | shadcn already owns `accent` / `accent-foreground` (its subtle hover surface); Beautiful UI uses `accent` as the brand colour. Renaming on copy keeps both palettes intact |
376	
377	Components as ported (gallery name → ours): `SidebarNav` → the shadcn sidebar block in `app-sidebar.tsx`
378	(conversation rail), `PromptBar` → `Composer` (scenario `/` command, model and seed), `StreamingText` →
379	`AssistantText` (assistant prose), `ThinkingState` → `ThinkingTrace` (per-step trace), `ToolChips` →
380	`ToolCallChips` (output, exit code, duration, fault badge), `CodeBlock` (outputs and unified diffs),
381	`TaskRows` → `ScoreRows` (score card), `LoadingState`, `DiffTable` → `FileStatusTable` (workspace file
382	status). No animation library
383	is required; the components use CSS transitions and `useLayoutEffect`.
384	
385	Theme bridge: Beautiful UI is written against its own Tailwind tokens (`ink`, `ink-2`, `ink-3`, `canvas`,
386	`surface`, `line`, `line-strong`, and `accent`, `accent-tint`, `accent-ink` → renamed `brand*`). They are
387	defined once in the existing `@theme inline` block of `src/index.css` as aliases of shadcn's CSS
388	variables (`--foreground`, `--muted-foreground`, `--background`, `--card`, `--border`, `--input`,
389	`--primary`, `--primary-foreground`), so both libraries follow the same light/dark palette (exact block
390	in `PLAN.md` §2.10.2).
391	
392	### 7.3 Layout, routes, state
393	
394	```
395	┌──────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────┐
396	│app-sidebar   │top bar: breadcrumb · status chips        │Workspace (resizable) │
397	│ + New run    │         step n/max · tokens · elapsed    │ Files (FileStatus…)  │
398	│ conversations│──────────────────────────────────────────│ Diffs (CodeBlock)    │
399	│  · lost-ack  │[user] task prompt                        │ Logs (virtualized)   │
400	│  · locked…   │[assistant] AssistantText                 │                      │
401	│ replays      │   ThinkingTrace ▸ ToolCallChips          │                      │
402	│  · gauntlet  │     (CodeBlock) · fault badge            │                      │
403	│              │[assistant] summary + ScoreRows           │                      │
404	│              │──────────────────────────────────────────│                      │
405	│ user menu:   │Composer  /scenario  model ▾  seed  Run   │                      │
406	│ id · theme   │                                          │                      │
407	└──────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────┘
408	```
409	
410	- Landing (`/`): five bullets explaining the app, a centered composer (`/` picks a scenario, model and
411	  seed chips), and below it a wide shadcn data table of scenarios (TanStack Table v9, fixed layout)
412	  with separate columns for title, description, max turns, failure badges, Live run and Replay;
413	  every injected-fault scenario ships a real recorded replay; harness reachability is a composer hint
414	  and a line in the identity menu, not a card.
415	- Routes (path based, `src/lib/router.ts`, no router dependency): `/` = the landing page;
416	  `/conversations/:id` = a persisted transcript, `?run=<run_id>` focuses one run (live tail if
417	  unfinished); `/runs/:id` = a run outside a conversation; `/replay/:demoId` = a bundled recorded run
418	  with a scrubber. `serve.py` answers extension-less paths with `index.html`, so deep links survive a
419	  refresh.
420	- One pure reducer (`src/lib/reducer.ts`, already present) builds the view model for live SSE, for
421	  `GET /runs/{id}` restore, and for replay of `public/demo/*.json`. Persisted transcripts from
422	  `GET /conversations/{id}` are mapped to the same view model, so every path renders through the same
423	  components.
424	- `src/lib/api.ts` (already present) reconnects SSE with `Last-Event-ID` and, after two failed opens,
425	  falls back to polling `GET /runs/{id}`; identity is added to it as a default header.
426	- Theme: light / dark / system, chosen from a radio group of icon buttons in the user menu (bottom-left
427	  sidebar footer) and stored
428	  per browser (`faultline.theme`); "system" follows `prefers-color-scheme` live; a one-off
429	  `?theme=light|dark|system` in the URL sets and persists the choice (for sharing a link in a given
430	  look, and for screenshot tooling). The choice is applied
431	  by toggling the `dark` class on `<html>` (shadcn's dark variant) both from an inline pre-paint script
432	  in `index.html` and from `src/lib/theme.ts`, so the first paint is already right. Semantic colour
433	  tones are written as light/dark pairs so status badges keep contrast on both backgrounds.
434	- Workspace panel: shadcn Resizable panels (`react-resizable-panels` 4.x) on ≥1280 px — draggable
435	  handle, collapsible to zero, split persisted in localStorage, the top-bar toggle bound to the
436	  panel's imperative handle; an overlay sheet below that width. The Logs tab virtualizes its rows
437	  (`@tanstack/react-virtual`, rows measured after render because lines wrap) and follows the tail
438	  while a run is live.
439	- Unified logging mirrors the server's JSON shape to the console with `svc:"web"`.
440	
441	### 7.4 Verification of the product flows
442	
443	`.claude/skills/verify-faultline` covers the browser: `features/web-ui.md` is the maintained list of
444	user flows (landing, composer, live run → conversation, persisted transcript, replays,
445	workspace, identity, theme, failure modes, sidebar); `apps/web/e2e/flows.spec.ts` is the
446	executable form (Playwright in the installed Google Chrome, one test per flow, a fresh browser
447	context per test); `helpers/verify_web.py` runs an HTTP doctor of the deployed site and then the
448	flows, writing `runs/<ts>_verify_web/` in the same layout as the backend runner (commands log, raw
449	outputs, screenshots, scores for a live run, `verification.json` with the browser-started
450	`run_id`, `manifest.json`). The rule that keeps it current: a change to a flow in `apps/web`
451	changes its map entry and its test in the same commit.

453	## 8. Deployment and configuration
454	
455	| Item | Value |
456	|---|---|
457	| Workspace / environment | `appliedlabsai` / `local` → web functions at `https://appliedlabsai-local--<app>-<fn>.modal.run`; the static site is a Modal **Server**, whose URL is `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.us-east.modal.direct` (read it back with `modal.Server.from_name("faultline-web", "site").get_url()`; the `.modal.run` shape answers 404 for Servers) |
458	| Deploy order | sandbox-env → harness (creates Volume `faultline-db` on first deploy) → web. Live URLs (2026-09-12): harness `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run`, sandbox-env `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-sandbox-env-api.modal.run`, web `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.us-east.modal.direct` |
459	| Secret | `anthropic-secret` (`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, optional `ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE`; name overridable via `FAULTLINE_ANTHROPIC_SECRET`), attached only to `run_episode` |
460	| Volume | `faultline-db` (v1), mounted at `/data` on `Store` only |
461	| Env | `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` (default `claude-haiku-4-5`), `SANDBOX_ENV_URL`, `HARNESS_URL`, `LOG_LEVEL`, `MODAL_ENVIRONMENT`, `FAULTLINE_ANTHROPIC_SECRET`, `HARNESS_HAIKU_THINKING` (opt-in), `FAULTLINE_WEB_PREBUILT` (see `.env.example`) |
462	| Warm containers | web Server, harness `api`, harness `Store` (`min_containers=1`) |
463	| Web image | Node 22 + pnpm; `pnpm i --frozen-lockfile && pnpm build`; `config.json` written from `HARNESS_URL` at container start |
464	| Sandbox guardrails | `timeout=1800`, `idle_timeout=600`, `block_network=True`, delete in `finally`, `reap` helper |
465	| Export | `modal run … ::checkpoint_now` then `modal volume get faultline-db faultline.sqlite3 runs/` |

467	## 9. Decision log
468	
469	| # | Decision | Alternatives considered | Why |
470	|---|---|---|---|
471	| 1 | Three Modal apps with a strict secret boundary | one app | harness swappable, environment gradeable without trusting the agent, static UI; the provider key exists in exactly one function |
472	| 2 | Faults injected at the tool boundary, not inside the shell | `chmod`, deleting files live, network faults | deterministic, seedable, invisible to the agent, gradeable from a ledger; ack-lost is only expressible at the boundary |
473	| 3 | `spawn` + event log + reconnecting SSE | run the loop inside the request | Modal web requests cap at 150 s |
474	| 4 | Persistence = SQLite on a Modal Volume | Modal Dict only; hosted Postgres; Modal Dict as hot path + SQLite archive | user asked for it; zero external services; a single file is trivial to export as evidence |
475	| 5 | Single-writer `Store` actor (`max_containers=1`) | every function opens the DB on the Volume; per-run DB files | Volumes have no distributed locking and last-write-wins; one process owning the file removes every consistency question |
476	| 6 | Hot DB on local disk, `VACUUM INTO` snapshots renamed atomically onto the Volume | run SQLite directly on the FUSE mount | WAL/locking on FUSE is undocumented, and background commits are not multi-file snapshots; Modal's own example copies-then-commits |
477	| 7 | Volume v1 | v2 | v2 needs an explicit flag and is marked experimental in the 1.5.5 CLI; no benefit for one file |
478	| 8 | `events` as source of truth + `messages`/`blocks`/`llm_calls` projections | events only; projections only | replay/SSE need the log; the UI and "what did the model see" need the message shape; projections are a cheap same-transaction write and a listed scope cut |
479	| 9 | Identity via `X-Faultline-User` header, minted in the browser, mirrored to a cookie | cookie-only; server-minted id | the API is cross-origin so cookies never reach it; localStorage is the reliable store; the cookie is a backup |
480	| 10 | Beautiful UI by copy-paste with lucide/shadcn substitutions | Vercel AI Elements (a real registry); shadcn only | matches the requested look; MIT; the private deps are small and swappable; scope cut #4 if it drags |
481	| 11 | Haiku 4.5 default, Sonnet/Opus 5 allowlisted | Sonnet default | cost and latency for a demo; the ack-lost scenario is more interesting when a cheaper model sometimes fails it |
482	| 12 | Path routes through a small in-house router (`src/lib/router.ts`: `pushState` + `useSyncExternalStore`) | `react-router-dom`; query params only (the scaffold's first design) | four screens with deep links that survive a refresh (`serve.py` SPA fallback); no dependency in a static SPA |
483	| 13 | Node 22 pinned (`.nvmrc`, `engines`, Modal image) | stay on the machine default 21.6 | Vite 8 and vitest 5 refuse to start on 21.6 (`styleText` missing from `node:util`) |
484	
485	## 10. Open questions
486	
487	- Should the composer allow editing the task prompt, or only choosing a scenario? Current answer: editable,
488	  stored in `runs.task_prompt`, because it makes the "conversation" framing honest. Revisit if it invites
489	  prompt-injection-shaped demos that distract from the reliability story.
490	- Do we need per-user rate limiting beyond `POST …/runs`? Probably not for review traffic.
491	- Checkpoint cadence (15 s) vs. cost of `VACUUM INTO` as the DB grows — measure once real runs exist and
492	  record the number in `PLAN.md` §0.

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:45:47.994Z
355	### 2.10 Web UI: shadcn/ui + Beautiful UI (verified 2026-09-12)
356	
357	**What Beautiful UI is** (https://www.beautifului.dev): an MIT-licensed gallery (© 2026 Shane Levine) of
358	21 React + Tailwind components "for AI-native interfaces". It is **not** an npm package and **not** a
359	shadcn registry: each component has a *Copy code* button and the TSX is embedded in the page. So the
360	integration is copy-paste into `src/components/bui/`, adapted to our shadcn theme. shadcn (v4 CLI,
361	style `base-nova` on Base UI, already initialised) stays the primitive layer; add `sheet dialog
362	dropdown-menu collapsible sonner` to the ten components present.
363	
364	#### 2.10.1 Components we take (deps read from the embedded source)
365	As ported into `src/components/bui/`: `SidebarNav` → shadcn sidebar block (`app-sidebar.tsx`) · `PromptBar` →
366	`Composer` · `StreamingText` → `AssistantText` · `ThinkingState` → `ThinkingTrace` · `ToolChips` → `ToolCallChips` ·
367	`TaskRows` → `ScoreRows` · `DiffTable` → `FileStatusTable`; `CodeBlock` and `LoadingState` kept their names;
368	`ChatComposer` was not ported. The table keeps the gallery names.
369	| Component | Lines | Used for | Deps to swap |
370	|---|---|---|---|
371	| `SidebarNav` | 449 | conversation rail (claude.ai-style): new run, search, list | `@central-icons-react/*` ×12 → `lucide-react`; `GlideMenu` → shadcn DropdownMenu |
372	| `PromptBar` | 712 | composer: scenario as `/` command, model picker, seed, Run | `glimm` (shader/sound) → delete that branch |
373	| `StreamingText` | 252 | assistant prose per turn | — |
374	| `ThinkingState` | 301 | collapsible per-step trace (steps = tool calls) | — |
375	| `ToolChips` | 336 | tool-call chips: command, output, exit code, duration, fault/recovered badge, code edits (wraps today's `ToolCallCard` data) | — (`react-dom` portal only) |
376	| `CodeBlock` | 233 | tool output + unified diffs (has a diff mode; replaces `DiffView` rendering) | — |
377	| `TaskRows` | 274 | score card: recovery checks + hidden tests (today's `ScoreCard` data) | — |
378	| `LoadingState` | 152 | provisioning sandbox / waiting on model | — |
379	| `DiffTable` | 250 | workspace file status (today's `WorkspacePanel` data) | internal `Button` → shadcn Button |
380	| `ChatComposer` | 242 | reference only (tabbed reasoning reply pattern) | — |
381	
382	Not taken: `AgentScreen`, `ApprovalCard`, `RecommendationCard`, `ContextCards`, `InsightCards`
383	(`liveline`), `Flowchart`, `FineTuneCard`, `RecordsTable`, `FilterTable`, `SearchList`,
384	`SelectionActions` (`iconoir-react`). Internal atoms the gallery imports but does not publish
385	(`@/components/atoms/{Button,StreamText,Shimmer,ValuePill,EntityChip}`, `@/components/primitives/GlideMenu`)
386	are replaced by shadcn equivalents. No animation library is needed (components use CSS + `useLayoutEffect`).
387	
388	#### 2.10.2 Theme bridge
389	Beautiful UI is written against its own Tailwind tokens — `ink`, `ink-2`, `ink-3` (text), `canvas`,
390	`surface` (backgrounds), `line`, `line-strong` (borders), `accent`, `accent-tint`, `accent-ink`.
391	**Clash:** shadcn already owns `accent` / `accent-foreground` (its subtle hover surface, defined in
392	`src/index.css`), while Beautiful UI uses `accent` as the brand colour. On copy, rename BUI's
393	`accent*` classes to `brand*` (`sed -E 's/\b(bg|text|border)-accent(-tint|-ink)?\b/\1-brand\2/g'`),
394	then add to the existing `@theme inline` block:
395	```css
396	  --color-ink: var(--foreground);
397	  --color-ink-2: color-mix(in oklch, var(--foreground) 75%, transparent);
398	  --color-ink-3: var(--muted-foreground);
399	  --color-canvas: var(--background);
400	  --color-surface: var(--card);
401	  --color-line: var(--border);
402	  --color-line-strong: var(--input);
403	  --color-brand: var(--primary);
404	  --color-brand-ink: var(--primary-foreground);
405	  --color-brand-tint: color-mix(in oklch, var(--primary) 12%, transparent);
406	```
407	Keep the MIT notice in `src/components/bui/LICENSE`.
408	
409	#### 2.10.3 Layout & state
410	- Three columns ≥ 1280 px: rail 280 px · transcript (max-w 48rem, centred) · workspace 420 px
411	  (a Sheet on narrower screens).
412	- Transcript = ChatGPT/claude.ai shape: user bubble (task prompt) → assistant turn = StreamingText
413	  prose + ThinkingState trace whose steps are ToolChips (expand → CodeBlock stdout/stderr, exit code,
414	  duration, `fault` badge from `tool.result.fault`, `recovered` badge when the next call on the same
415	  path succeeds) → final summary + TaskRows score card. Header chips: status, step `n/max`, tokens, elapsed.
416	- Path routes with no router dependency (`src/lib/router.ts`): `/`, `/conversations/:id[?run=]`, `/runs/:id`, `/replay/:demoId`.
417	- Keep the existing reducer (`src/lib/reducer.ts`) as the single view model for live SSE, `GET /runs/{id}`
418	  restore and replay; persisted transcripts (`GET /conversations/{id}` → messages/blocks) are mapped to
419	  the same view model so every path renders through the same components.
420	- `src/lib/{identity,api,config,reducer,replay,log}.ts`; vitest covers reducer, SSE resume, config, identity.

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:46:25.008Z
1	# Faultline
2	
3	**A mini agent harness with a browser view, where the environment fights back.**
4	
5	A Claude agent runs *real* shell commands — reading files, editing code, running tests — inside an
6	isolated sandbox. The environment deliberately injects the failures real tool-using agents meet in
7	production: a file that isn't there, a write that is denied, and a write that **succeeds but whose
8	response times out**. The browser shows every command, its output, the resulting file changes, and
9	whether the agent recovered. The environment is framed as a tiny RL gym
10	(`reset / step / observe / evaluate`) so recovery behaviour is *graded*, not just eyeballed.
11	
12	> Take-home for Anthropic Platform SWE · Theme 3 (Systems & Reliability) with a Theme 4 (Evaluation) twist.
13	> Live demo: **https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.us-east.modal.direct** · Rationale: [RATIONALE.md](RATIONALE.md) · Plan/log: [PLAN.md](PLAN.md)
14	
15	## Try it in 60 seconds
16	
17	1. Open the live URL. Pick a scenario from the table (each row lists which fault kinds are in play, not where or when).
18	2. Press **Run**. Watch the transcript stream: assistant reasoning, tool calls, outputs, fault badges; the
19	   workspace column shows files, diffs and logs.
20	3. Read the score card: hidden tests (60%) + recovery checks (40%), e.g. *"verified the file before
21	   re-writing after the lost ack"* and *"exactly one changelog entry"*.
22	4. No API quota? Press **Replay** on a scenario row — a recorded live run plays back (with a scrubber)
23	   and needs no backend.
24	
25	## Architecture
26	
27	```
28	 browser  ──HTTPS JSON + SSE──▶  agent-harness (Modal)  ──MCP over HTTP + gym REST──▶  sandbox-env (Modal)
29	 apps/web static on Modal        api: no secrets                                       controller · fault plan · grader
30	                                 run_episode: the only fn with ANTHROPIC_API_KEY            │ Sandbox.exec
31	                                                                                            ▼
32	                                                                                 Modal Sandbox (/workspace, no network)
33	```
34	
35	Three separately deployed Modal apps, three trust boundaries:
36	
37	| Component | Holds | Never sees |
38	|---|---|---|
39	| `apps/web` | UI, replay data | secrets, fault plan |
40	| `services/agent-harness` | the model loop (`run_episode` has the provider key), the single-writer SQLite `Store` (conversations, runs, events) on a Modal Volume, SSE | workspace files (only via MCP), fault plan |
41	| `services/sandbox-env` | gym state, fault plan, ledger, grader, Sandbox lifecycle | the provider key |
42	| Modal Sandbox | the agent's shell and files | everything above; outbound network is blocked |
43	
44	The environment keeps its fault plan outside the command sandbox. Transient missing-file and
45	denied-write faults are simulated **at the tool boundary**; the sticky missing-file case starts
46	with the file absent. `ack_lost` performs the write, then substitutes a timeout-style tool error
47	without breaking the connection. The agent must check the file before deciding whether to retry:
48	a careless append retry produces a duplicate that the grader catches.
49	
50	## Simulated vs. real failures
51	
52	The harness now emits `error_class` with failure origin (`injected | staged | real`), layer and
53	human-readable label, plus command `outcome` (`executed | failed | not_executed | unknown`).
54	`unevaluated` identifies unavailable grading; `interrupted` identifies an interruption that ended
55	the run. The model receives the tool error, while the browser and evidence receive provenance.
56	See [the error taxonomy](docs/error-taxonomy.md) and [fault semantics](services/sandbox-env/FAULTS.md).
57	
58	The **`worker-crash` scenario is implemented**: the harness process exits during a dispatched write,
59	Modal retries the same run, and a fresh worker reconstructs the conversation from persisted events
60	and reconnects to the existing episode. It supplies an unknown-outcome result for the pending call
61	so the agent can check the workspace before continuing. In the saved live proof, worker generation
62	advanced to 2, the ledger confirmed the write landed, and the final checks found exactly one release
63	entry and the correct version, scoring 100/100.
64	
65	The saved interruption suite (`runs/20260913T001500Z_interruptions/summary.json`, gitignored local
66	evidence) passed 39/39 checks across worker crash, transport abort, sandbox loss, simulated lost-ack
67	and a prior failure specimen. The proof driver is [scripts/prove_interruptions.py](scripts/prove_interruptions.py).
68	These results are scoped to the tested runs:
69	
70	- The crash proof uses `write_file`; a real shell-append crash experiment still needs separate verification.
71	- The trigger sleeps after dispatch, rather than waiting at a confirmed write-completion barrier.
72	  The ledger establishes that the write landed in the captured run, not that every crash occurs after it.
73	- Recovery assumes the environment and store survive. Forced persistence flushes still tolerate errors;
74	  simultaneous storage failure can lose recovery state. This is not a universal exactly-once guarantee.
75	- Four simulated-fault scenario replays are bundled; a worker-crash replay is not bundled yet.
76	
77	## Repo layout
78	
79	```
80	apps/web/                 Vite + React + shadcn/ui; modal_app.py serves the built site via a Modal Server
81	services/agent-harness/   FastAPI api (conversations, runs, SSE events) + run_episode model loop + single-writer SQLite Store
82	services/sandbox-env/     FastAPI gym (reset/observe/evaluate) + fastmcp tools (run_command/read_file/write_file/list_dir)
83	packages/common/          faultline_common: pydantic wire contracts + unified JSON logging
84	scripts/                  deploy.sh, smoke_roundtrip.py, run_episode_cli.py, fault_proofs.py, export_demo.py, web_check.py
85	docs/                     error taxonomy (contract), store notes, sandbox ops notes, video script
86	runs/                     (gitignored) evidence from every run against Modal
87	PLAN.md                   the living plan: checklists, done conditions, scope cuts, time log
88	ARCHITECTURE.md           system design: trust boundaries, persistence, contracts, decision log
89	```
90	
91	## Running it yourself
92	
93	Prereqs: Python 3.11, `uv`, Node ≥ 22.12 (`apps/web/.nvmrc`) + pnpm 9, a Modal account (`modal token new`), and a Modal secret
94	`anthropic-secret` with `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` and `ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE` (org-scoped keys must send the workspace id header; the harness does).
95	
96	```bash
97	modal secret create anthropic-secret ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=sk-ant-... ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE=wrkspc_... -e local
98	scripts/deploy.sh local          # sandbox-env → harness → web, prints the three URLs
99	```
100	
101	Individual pieces:
102	
103	```bash
104	python scripts/smoke_roundtrip.py --base https://<sandbox-env-url>     # one real shell/MCP round trip, evidence in runs/
105	python scripts/run_episode_cli.py --scenario lost-ack                  # a full model run, live transcript, evidence in runs/
106	services/sandbox-env/.venv/bin/python -m pytest services/sandbox-env/tests   # fault engine + grader tests (no Modal needed)
107	services/agent-harness/.venv/bin/python -m pytest services/agent-harness/tests
108	cd apps/web && pnpm i && pnpm dev                                      # UI; harness URL from public/config.json or VITE_HARNESS_URL
109	```
110	
111	Configuration: `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` (default `claude-haiku-4-5`; the UI can pick Sonnet 5 / Opus 5),
112	`SANDBOX_ENV_URL`, `HARNESS_URL`, `LOG_LEVEL=debug` for argument/output dumps. See `.env.example`.
113	
114	## Verifying it
115	
116	`.claude/skills/verify-faultline/` is the repo's verification skill: it drives the deployed gym over
117	MCP exactly as the harness does (careful and careless recovery, all three fault kinds, an optional
118	live model episode), reads the workspace files back, cross-checks the grader from those files and
119	the ledger, and keeps everything under `runs/<ts>_verify/` (commands, raw outputs, file evidence,
120	scores, pass/fail, and a hash manifest computed after cleanup).
121	
122	```bash
123	services/sandbox-env/.venv/bin/python .claude/skills/verify-faultline/helpers/verify_backend.py --live
124	```
125	
126	Browser flows: `helpers/verify_web.py` runs an HTTP doctor of the deployed site and the Playwright flows in
127	`apps/web/e2e/flows.spec.ts`, writing `runs/<ts>_verify_web/`. `PLAN.md` §4 records what has actually been
128	verified and what is still open.
129	
130	## Logging
131	
132	Every service emits one JSON line per event with the same shape
133	(`ts svc lvl run_id episode_id step ev msg …`), so a single `run_id` can be followed from the browser
134	console through `modal app logs faultline-harness -e local` into `modal app logs faultline-sandbox-env -e local`.
135	
136	## Status and limits
137	
138	See [PLAN.md](PLAN.md) §0 for the status board and §7 for what was cut. Known limits: one sandbox per
139	episode (no pooling), Modal's 150 s web request cap is handled by spawning the run and streaming
140	events from the SQLite event log over reconnecting SSE, and the fault matcher for raw shell commands is token-based (good enough
141	for `cat`, `sed -i`, `>>`; not a full shell parser).

1	# Faultline — 5-minute video script
2	
3	Target 4:30–5:00. Screen: the deployed web app; one terminal tab for `modal app logs`.
4	
5	## 0:00 – 0:30 · The problem
6	- "Agent demos show the happy path. In production the tool boundary fails: the file isn't there, the write is refused, or the write lands but the response times out."
7	- "Faultline is a mini agent harness with a browser view where the environment injects those failures on purpose — and grades whether the agent recovers."
8	
9	## 0:30 – 1:30 · Architecture (show README diagram)
10	- Three Modal apps, three trust boundaries: browser → harness (only `run_episode` has the Anthropic key) → sandbox-env (gym + MCP; fault plan, ledger, grader) → Modal Sandbox (the agent's shell, no network).
11	- "The gym is reset / step / observe / evaluate. Step is MCP, so any MCP-speaking agent can play; evaluate runs hidden tests the agent never saw plus recovery checks from the ledger."
12	- One sentence on the 150 s Modal cap → spawn + SQLite event log (single-writer Store on a Modal Volume) + resumable SSE.
13	
14	## 1:30 – 4:00 · Live run: `lost-ack`
15	- Pick the scenario, keep Haiku, press Run. Narrate the transcript as it streams:
16	  - reset → files appear; the agent reads README/CHANGELOG.
17	  - the write to CHANGELOG.md hangs ~3 s and returns `504 … may or may not have completed` (fault badge).
18	  - "Watch what it does next." Ideal: read_file CHANGELOG.md → sees the entry landed → does not re-append → bumps version → pytest → submit.
19	  - Score card: tests 60% + checks (`verified_before_rewrite`, `no_duplicate_entry`, `version_bumped`).
20	- In the workspace column: Diffs shows exactly one 0.2.0 section. Logs: same `run_id` in every line; flash the terminal `modal app logs faultline-sandbox-env -e local` showing the `ack_lost` decision line.
21	- If time: run `locked-file`, or show the careless scripted control that appended twice and scored 8 (`runs/20260912T220451Z_faults/`; no bundled replay shows a failed recovery).
22	
23	## 4:00 – 4:45 · Decisions and tradeoffs
24	- Faults at the tool boundary, outside the shell (why not chmod: ack-lost is impossible to fake from inside; the plan stays hidden).
25	- Grade the ops, not the prose: the ledger, not the transcript.
26	- Haiku by default so reviewers can run many episodes; Sonnet/Opus one click away.
27	- Scope cuts made (see PLAN.md §7) and time spent.
28	
29	## 4:45 – 5:00 · With more time
30	- More fault kinds (partial writes, flaky test runner), warm sandbox pools, batch runs across seeds × models with recovery-rate charts, trajectory export for evals.
31

import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'

/**
 * Theme preference: light, dark, or follow the OS. Persisted per browser; applied by toggling the
 * `dark` class on <html> (shadcn's `@custom-variant dark (&:is(.dark *))`). index.html runs the
 * same logic inline before first paint so there is no flash of the wrong theme.
 */
export type Theme = 'light' | 'dark' | 'system'

export const THEME_KEY = 'faultline.theme'
export const THEMES: readonly Theme[] = ['light', 'dark', 'system']
const QUERY = '(prefers-color-scheme: dark)'

let memory: Theme = 'system'
const listeners = new Set<() => void>()

function isTheme(v: unknown): v is Theme {
  return v === 'light' || v === 'dark' || v === 'system'
}

export function getTheme(): Theme {
  try {
    const v = localStorage.getItem(THEME_KEY)
    // Storage is the source of truth when it is readable; anything unrecognised means "system".
    return isTheme(v) ? v : 'system'
  } catch {
    // Storage blocked (private mode, sandboxed frame): remember the choice for this page only.
    return memory
  }
}

export function systemPrefersDark(): boolean {
  return typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.matchMedia === 'function'
    ? window.matchMedia(QUERY).matches
    : true
}

/** The theme actually in effect (system resolved). */
export function resolvedTheme(theme: Theme = getTheme()): 'light' | 'dark' {
  return theme === 'system' ? (systemPrefersDark() ? 'dark' : 'light') : theme

17	FaultKind = Literal["missing_file", "denied_write", "ack_lost"]
18	FaultMode = Literal["sticky", "transient"]
19	
20	
21	class FaultSpec(BaseModel):
22	    """One injected failure. Lives ONLY in sandbox-env state (never inside the command sandbox).
23	
24	    sticky    : applied at reset (e.g. the file is really deleted) and never lifted.
25	    transient : intercepted at the tool boundary for the first `hits` matching calls, then lifted.
26	    """
27	
28	    kind: FaultKind
29	    path: str = Field(description="workspace-relative path the fault targets")
30	    mode: FaultMode = "transient"
31	    hits: int | None = Field(default=1, description="matching calls affected before the fault lifts (None = forever)")
32	    delay_ms: int = Field(default=3000, description="ack_lost only: how long to hold the response before failing it")
33	
34	
35	class FaultPlan(BaseModel):
36	    faults: list[FaultSpec] = Field(default_factory=list)
37	
38	
39	HarnessFaultKind = Literal["worker_crash", "transport_abort"]
40	
41	
42	class Scenario(BaseModel):
43	    """Public scenario fields (safe to show the agent and the browser)."""
44	
45	    id: str
46	    title: str
47	    description: str = Field(description="one-paragraph human description shown in the UI")
48	    task_prompt: str = Field(description="what the agent is asked to do (goes into the user turn)")
49	    max_steps: int = 20
50	    fault_kinds: list[FaultKind] = Field(default_factory=list, description="which kinds are in play (not where/when)")
51	    harness_faults: list["HarnessFault"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="REAL interruptions the harness inflicts on itself (see HarnessFault)")
52	    checks: list["CheckSpec"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="what the grader will look for (public: id, description, weight)")
53	    faults_public: list["FaultPublic"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="fault classes in play (kind, layer, origin, description) — never paths, hits or timing")
54	
55	
56	class CheckSpec(BaseModel):
57	    id: str
58	    description: str
59	    weight: float = 1.0
60	
61	
62	class FaultPublic(BaseModel):
63	    kind: FaultKind | HarnessFaultKind
64	    origin: ErrorOrigin
65	    layer: ErrorLayer
66	    description: str
67	
68	
69	class HarnessFault(BaseModel):
70	    """A real interruption the harness inflicts on ITSELF at a chosen call (chaos trigger, real failure mode).
71	
72	    worker_crash    : after dispatching the matching tool call and waiting `after_ms`, the run_episode
73	                      process exits hard (os._exit) — the request is already at sandbox-env, so the
74	                      side effect completes; a fresh worker resumes the run from the persisted events.
75	    transport_abort : the in-flight HTTP request is cancelled client-side after `after_ms`; the server
76	                      still completes it; the agent gets ETRANSPORT with outcome unknown.
77	    """
78	
79	    kind: HarnessFaultKind
80	    tool: ToolName = "write_file"
81	    path: str
82	    nth: int = Field(default=1, ge=1, description="fire on the nth matching call")
83	    after_ms: int = Field(default=400, ge=0, description="delay after dispatch before interrupting (lets the request reach the server)")

The previous web items are mostly fixed, but the cleanup left one real gap: the backend now sends interruption, resume, sandbox and grading-status events, and the UI stores them but shows none of them. Checks: tsc 0 errors, vitest 187/187 passing, oxlint 0 errors and 12 warnings. apps/web has no changes against HEAD, and I edited no repo files.

1. Previous web items

FIXED (checked in the tree): - B18: transcriptFromMessages now takes an overlay (transcript.ts:149-217), ConversationPage.tsx:141 passes evaluation, error and status in, and TranscriptView.tsx:208 shows a "Not graded" callout. - B19: lib/callStatus.ts drives the tool-call status pill and the rail glyph. - B20: asRunStatus (reducer.ts:58) and outcome_known === true (:538). One path still skips it (N4). - B21: FaultFiredBadge uses origin plus taxonomyLabel, and faultBlurb depends on origin. - B22: the badge now reads "read-back seen" and is labelled a heuristic. A related wording problem remains (N2). - B23: story.ts uses structured fields. The pytest regex only reads the command input to choose wording. - B24: healthPhase has four states, used by the home hint and the sidebar dot. - Section C: every listed deletion is gone (reliability.*, WorkspacePanel, old playback and replaying, the four shadcn primitives, FAULT_LABEL/fmtTime, the log helpers, the history-unavailable fallbacks, legacy ?c=/?demo= routes, the duplicate color-scheme meta, --color-brand-tint, Timeline, RunPage's second getRun). public/demo/README.md is fixed too.

KEPT-ON-PURPOSE: api.ts EVENT_TYPES (it registers the SSE listeners at :520), me/updateConversation/archiveConversation (only tests call them), and the bui/index.ts barrel (4 importers).

STILL PRESENT: - B25 (replays): none of the 4 JSONs has error_class or evaluation_status; lost-ack has no llm.call and its fault.fired has no origin. scripts/export_demo.py: - EVENT_TYPES (:54-68) rejects interruption, run.resumed and episode.sandbox. - sanitise strips sandbox_id (:150), which the reducer's episode.sandbox handler needs. - Result: a worker-crash replay can't be exported. High confidence. - serve.py:4-5: docstring still says "a refresh on /?run=… is fine, but any future path route would 404". High. - format.ts:84 plural(): unreferenced, and ThinkingTrace.tsx:40 has its own copy. High. - useHarness state: base, source, healthError, refresh and userId have no reader outside the hook. Medium. - A14: modal_app.py:8,200 still print …faultline-web-site.modal.run, which returns 404 for Servers. High.

2. New dead / legacy code

  • N1 lib/reducer.ts, state nothing renders (high). interruptions, resumes, workerGeneration, sandboxEvents, sandboxId/Alive, resetAttempt, evaluationStatus/Error, plus result.attempts and result.sandbox, are folded (:619-871) but have no reader outside the reducer, types and tests. The harness emits them (loop.py:404,610,656,997). A worker-crash run therefore shows no interruption, no "worker 2", no resume and no sandbox loss, even though PLAN §3.3.2 L527 expects it. Action: render them (status strip, story checkpoint, reason in the Not-graded callout) or remove them.
  • N2 replay/StoryBar.tsx TONE (high). The label comes from a generic tone: ok → "recovered", warn → "fault". story.ts sets ok for any pytest exit 0 (:213) and warn for any non-zero exit or truncated (:209, :263). So a step can read "· fault" with no fault fired, or "· recovered" with nothing to recover. Action: give each checkpoint an explicit kind and update e2e F5's /recovered/ check.
  • N3, grade colours disagree (high). ScoreRows scoreTone is green at ≥85 and its pill uses evaluation.passed. runStatus.gradeOf only counts a pass when every check passes. A score of 90 shows a green headline next to an amber "graded with failures" badge. Action: have ScoreRows use gradeOf.
  • N4 useRunView.ts:140 (medium). The polling onRecord sets status: r.status ?? … without validation and drops error_class, interruptions and worker_generation, which bypasses B20. Action: use asRunStatus or the fromRunRecord merge.
  • N5 TranscriptView.tsx:225-229 (medium). The title "finished but could not be graded" is chosen because error text is present, so interrupted runs get it too. It never looks at evaluation_status or error_class.
  • N6 lib/story.ts (high).
  • It ignores tool.result.outcome and re-derives "unknown", duplicating callStatus.effectiveOutcome.
  • :141 says "the worker really is killed" for every harness fault, including transport_abort, which only cancels the request.
  • N7, duplicated rendering (medium). ThinkingTrace Glyph and ToolCallChips StatusPill have the same icon switch, and both render the same badge trio, so each call's badges show twice when expanded.
  • N8, exports only tests use (high):
  • router.ts: sameRoute, currentRoute.
  • api.ts: createRun and its request/response types; the UI never calls POST /runs.
  • api.ts:181-193: the positional HarnessClient(base, fetchImpl) constructor form.
  • N9, unread fields and types (low-medium):
  • CODE_TEXT .title/.verifyHint and CHECK_TEXT .description are never read; the latter duplicates the backend's Scenario.checks[].description.
  • faults_public is never read.
  • Unimported contract mirrors: EpisodeResetData, RunStartedData, RunFinishedData, TurnTextData, TurnThinkingData, LlmCall(Data), WorkspaceDiffData, ObserveResponse, *Output, FaultPlan.
